Atsɛɔ mi Nii Kwei ni mitsɔɔ Ga kɛ mɛi pii le Nkran.
Ganyobi ji mi ni mihe yɛ fɛo yɛ he fɛɛ he kɔŋkɔŋ.
Ga wiemɔ lɛ yɛ fɛo waa ni eŋɔɔ mɛi pii naabu.
Bɔni afee ni owie Ga jogbaŋŋ lɛ, bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ okase daa gbi.
Hɔmɔwɔ afahyɛ lɛ ji afahyɛ kɛse kɛha Ga mashi bii fɛɛ.
Wɔkɛ kpokpoi kɛ palm nut soup yeɔ afahyɛ lɛ be mli.
Gbalamɔ kɛ suɔmɔ yɛ weku lɛ mli be fɛɛ be.
Wɔbaa ya skul kɛkase nikasemɔ kɛ kɔmputa nitsumɔ.
Nu nɛ nɛɛ nyɛ gbɛkɛ ni gbɛ lɛ fɛɛ yi kɛ nu.
Nii Mei kɛ Naa Mei ji gbɛtsɔɔlɔi kpakpai kɛha bii.
Maŋtsɛ lɛ wie hewale wiemɔ kɛtsɔɔ maŋbii lɛ fɛɛ.
Mɛi pii basara Ga maŋ lɛ be ni ayeɔ afahyɛ lɛ.
Gbɛkɛbii lɛ mii shwe bɔɔl yɛ agona lɛ nɔ gbɛkɛ nɛɛ.
Nitsulɔ lɛ tsu nii waa kɛha e-shia kɛ e-weku.
Wɔsumɔɔ ni wɔkase Twi, Ewe, Ga kɛ Fante wiemɔi lɛ.
Nyɔŋmɔ ajɔɔ Ghana maŋ lɛ kɛ emli bii fɛɛ daa gbi.
Tsu nii kpakpa koni onya hewalɛ kɛ nibii kpakpai.
Ga mashi ji he kpo kɛha kusum kɛ blema nibii pii.
Kɛ otee Makola jra nɔ lɛ obaana nibii vovoo pii.
Nuŋtsɔ Nyɔŋmɔ ji kpojiemɔ kɛ hewale kɛha wɔ fɛɛ.

Week 1 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ (nk; naabu tsɔɔmɔi, lilɛi nɔwomɔ yɛ hei komɛi)
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ kɔnsonanti lɛ (nk; gbɛɛmɔi afeemɔ, hei ni gbɛɛmɔi jeɔ kpo yɛ kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ Yitso: Gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ vaoli kɛ kɔnsonanti Voali Eji wiemɔ gbɛɛmɔi ni hoɔ yɔɔ kɛtsɔɔ daaŋ ni nɔ ko etsiii enaa.
Yɛ efeemɔ mli lɛ sɛŋ kpaa lɛ kpokpoɔ.
Vaolii asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ: Yɛ vaolii asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ mli lɛ, otii komɛi yɛ ni akwɛɔ; nomɛi ji naabu tsɔɔmɔi, lilɛi nɔwomɔ kɛ lilɛi hei komɛi kɛ vaolii gbɛɛmɔi afeemɔ.
Naabu shikamɔ: Enɛ tsɔɔ bɔ ni naabu shikamɔ lɛ yɔɔ haa, kɛji abɔɔ naabu lɛ tako loo kokloo aloo agbɛɔ naabu lɛ mli.
Lilɛi lɛ nɔwomɔ: Enɛ tsɔɔ he ni lilɛi lɛ kaa yɛ vaoli gbɛɛmɔfeemɔ mli.
Lilɛi lɛ hei komɛi: Enɛ tsɔɔ lilɛi lɛ hei komɛi ni yeɔ buaa vaoli gbɛɛmɔ lɛ feemɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ (nk;
naabu tsɔɔmɔi, lilɛi nɔwomɔ yɛ hei komɛi)
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ kɔnsonanti lɛ (nk;
gbɛɛmɔi afeemɔ, hei ni gbɛɛmɔi jeɔ kpo yɛ kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ Yitso: Gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ vaoli kɛ kɔnsonanti Voali Eji wiemɔ gbɛɛmɔi ni hoɔ yɔɔ kɛtsɔɔ daaŋ ni nɔ ko etsiii enaa.
Vaolii asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ: Yɛ vaolii asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ mli lɛ, otii komɛi yɛ ni akwɛɔ;
nomɛi ji naabu tsɔɔmɔi, lilɛi nɔwomɔ kɛ lilɛi hei komɛi kɛ vaolii gbɛɛmɔi afeemɔ.
Afeɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔ nɛɛ yɛ lilɛi lɛ hiɛ, teŋ loo esɛɛ gbɛ.
/ɔ/-baashi-sɛɛ-kokloo Kɔnsonanti Eji gbɛɛmɔi, ni yɛ amɛsɔɔ mli lɛ, lilɛi lɛ he ko tsiɔ kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ naa dani efɛɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ, ni akɛfataa vaoli he kɛfeɔ blawa.
Kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ : gbɛɛmɔfeemɔ, he ni afeɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ yɛ kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ.
Gbɛɛmɔfeemɔ: Yɛ enɛ feemɔ mli lɛ, ayɛ kɔnsonanti ni haa gbeekpaa lɛ kpokpoɔ kɛ nɔ ni gbeekpaa lɛ kpokpooo.
Kɔsonanti ni haa gbeekpaa lɛ kpokpoɔ lɛ ekomɛi ji;
/g/, /d/, /m/, /b/ ni nɔ ni haaa gbeekpaa lɛ akpokpo lɛ ekomɛi ji;
Hei ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ yɛ: Hei komɛi ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ yɛ ji naabu shishi kɛ ŋwɛi, naabu kɛ nyanyɔŋ, nyanyɔŋ, agba bɔdɔɔ, agba keketee, lilɛi lɛ sɛɛ kn.
Bɔ ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ ahaa: Enɛ tsɔɔ bɔ ni hei kɛ nibii ni akɛsɔɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ kɛ amehe shraa amɛhaa dani gbɛɛmɔ lɛ jeɔ kpo.
Kɔnsonantii komɛi ni atsiɔ kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ naa kɛsɔɔ.
Nibii komɛi ni akwɛɔ kɛsɔɔ kɔnsonantii komɛ ji kɔɔyɔɔ naatsii (plossives), ashrahe (fricatives), ashrahe kpoo (affricate), gugɔjiaŋ (nasal), kn.
Ŋmaa vaolii etɛ kɛ hooo kwraa.
Ŋmalamɔ vaolii lɛ taakɛ amɛjeɔ kpo yɛ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ amli lɛ: ŋawie, shwane, ablekuma, oshishibrishii, kn kn.
Ŋmalamɔ kɔnsonantii ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ
Gbalamɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ asusuɔ vaolii atsɔɔ lɛ amli.
Gbalamɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ asusuɔ kɔnsonantii atsɔɔ lɛ amli .
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni asɔɔ vaolii gbɛɛmɔi ahaa.
Susumɔ vaolii gbɛɛmɔi ni baa nɛɛ otsɔɔ a.
Jaramɔ kɔnsonantii ni baa nɛɛ (b/, /d/, /g/, /t/) amli owo kui ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ amli:
Hei ni asɔɔ yɛ:
Bɔ ni asɔɔ lɛ:
Okɛ gbɛjianɔtoi ni aha lɛ asusu kɔnsonantii enumɔ otsɔɔ.
Week 2 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii
Gbalamɔ bɔ ni vaoli shikamɔ yɔɔ yɛ wiemɔ mli.
(nk., hiɛ, teŋ kɛ nagbee).
Gbalamɔ bɔ ni kɔnsonanti shikamɔ yɔɔ yɛ wiemɔ mli (nk., hiɛ, teŋ kɛ nagbee).
Yitso: Vaoli gbɛhe Vaolii Agbɛhe Vaoli gbɛhe ji he pɔtɛɛ ni vaoli lɛ ka yɛ wiemɔ ko mli.
Vaolii komɛi jeɔ wiemɔ shishi, ekomɛi kaa teŋ ni ekomɛi gbeɔ wiemɔkuli naa.
Kɛji vaoli ko je wiemɔ ko shishi lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ shishijee Kɛji eba yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ teŋ.
Ni kɛji egbe wiemɔkuli ko hu naa lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ naagbee.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Kwɛmɔ vaoli gbɛhe ni /a/ ka yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli:
sisa Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kome lɛ mli lɛ, /a/ eje wiemɔkuli lɛ shishi.
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /a/ nyɛɔ ejeɔ wiemɔkuli ko shishi.
Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ni ji enyɔ lɛ mli lɛ, /a/ eba yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ teŋ.
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /a/ nyɛɔ ebaa yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ.
Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ni ji etɛ lɛ mli lɛ, /a/ eba yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ naagbee.
No hewɔ lɛ, /a/ nyɛɔ egbeɔ wiemɔkuli ko naa.
NB: Tsɔɔlɔi akakpa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi lɛ fɛɛ ka ŋlɛ kome nɔ yɛ nikasemɔ mli.
Aawo tsɔɔlɔi ahewalɛ ni amɛkɛ nitsɔɔmɔ henɔi srɔtoi atsɔɔ nii kɛye abua kaselɔi ni yɔɔ shɔɔ kɛyanina amɛtipɛŋfoi lɛ.
Te vaolii kɛ kɔnsonantii nɛɛ ateŋ nɔ ni nyɛɔ ejeɔ wiemɔkuli ko shishi?
/a/, /e/, /ɛ/, /i/, /o/, /ɔ/ kɛ /u/, /b/, /d/, /f/, /g/, /h/, /j/ kɛ /k/
Te vaoli ni aha lɛ ateŋ nɔ ni nyɛɔ ehiɔ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ?
Te vaolii kɛ kɔnsonantii nɛɛ ateŋ nɔ ni nyɛɔ egbeɔ wiemɔkuli ko naa?
Kwɛmɔ gbɛhe ni vaolii kɛ kɔnsonantii lɛ kamɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli.
NB: Tsɔɔlɔ ahala wiemɔkulibii ni hiɛ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi ni hiɛ kɛlɛmɔi srɔtoi kɛwo kaselɔi ahewalɛ.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ1 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa Ha wiemɔkulibii enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ enyɔ ni abaana vaoli gbɛɛmɔi ni jeɔ wiemɔ shihsi, baa yɛ teŋ kɛ nɔ ni gbeɔ wiemɔ naa yɛ mli.
/u/: Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ
Taomɔ vaolii ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni nɛɛ amli ni otsɔɔ amɛgbɛhe.
shwane Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Nii Asɛɛ kɔmɔ Jwɛŋmɔ
Tsɛrɛmɔ wiemɔkulibii ni abaanyɛ ayɔse vaoli kome ni jeɔ shishi, baa yɛ teŋ ni asaŋ ebaa yɛ wiemɔkulibii lɛ naagbee.
Yitso: Kɔnsonantii Agbɛhe Kɔnsonanti Gbɛhe Kɔnsonanti gbɛhe ji he pɔtɛɛ ni kɔnsonanti ko ka yɛ wiemɔkuli ko mli.
Kɔnsonantii komɛi nyɛɔ amɛjeɔ wiemɔkuli shishi, amɛnyɛɔ amɛbaa yɛ teŋ shi jeee naagbee bɛja gugɔjiaŋ kɔnsonantii etɛ lɛ (m, n kɛ ŋ) pɛ ni nyɛɔ amɛgbeɔ wiemɔkuli naa.
Kɛji kɔnsonanti ko je wiemɔ ko shishi lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ shishijee.
Kɛji eba yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ teŋ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ Kwɛmɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛhe ni /b/ ka yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli:
abɔɔ Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kome lɛ mli lɛ, /b/ eje wiemɔkuli lɛ shishi.
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /b/ nyɛɔ ejeɔ wiemɔkuli ko shishi.
Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ni ji enyɔ lɛ mli lɛ, /b/ ba yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ teŋ.
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /b/ nyɛɔ ebaa yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ.
Tsɔɔlɔ ahala wiemɔkulibii ni hiɛ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni hiɛ kɛlɛmɔi srɔtoi kɛwo kaselɔi ahewalɛ.
Nyɛgbaa kɔnsonanti lɛ akaamɔhei ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni baa lɛ ahe sane.
Okɛ kɔnsonantii ni wɔna yɛ wiemɔkulibii amli momo lɛ atsɛrɛ wiemɔkulibii krokomɛi koni otsɔɔ amɛshikamɔhei.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Tsɛrɛmɔ wiemɔkulibii ni abaanyɛ ayɔse kɔnsonanti ni baa nɛɛ eko yɛ mli.
/n/ Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ:
Tsɛrɛmɔ wiemɔkulibii ni abaanyɛ ayɔse kɔnsonantii ni baa nɛɛ eko yɛ mli koni okɛ amɛ awo shishijee, kɛ teŋ.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Nii Asɛɛ kɔmɔ Jwɛŋmɔ
Okɛ kɔnsonantii atsɛrɛ wiemɔkulibii nyɔŋma ni jeɔ wiemɔkuli ko shishi, eka teŋ kɛ nɔ ni gbeɔ wiemɔkuli naa koni ogbala ohetoo lɛ mli.
Week 3 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii
Taomɔ otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ sane ko mli.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ, subaŋ kpakpai( cultural values) (mɔbulɛ, mlijɔlɛ, shishinumɔ , ŋmɛ nɔ ko he gbɛ kɛ ekrokomɛi), tsɔsemɔ, gbekɛbii anyɔŋyeli, wɔhewɔŋ, nibii ahiɛtsakemɔ kɛ feemɔ yɛ Ghana, su mli jwɛtri, kɛ GESI kn.
Susumɔ mɛi ayiŋtoo yɛ saji ni sa komɛi ahe kɛtsɔ sanegbaa mli.
Okɛ nilee ni kɔɔ nikanemɔ vii kɛ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ akane sane ni oha sanebimɔi ahetoo.
Kɛ gbɛjianɔtoo asusu hiɛhiɛ sane kanemɔ he.
(nk., hiŋmɛi loo wao ni okɛbaanyiɛ sane ko nɔ oyayai, kɛtao wiemɔkulibii loo sanekuku, kn.)
Kɛ sanegbaa asusu toiboo kɛ saneŋmaa he, kn.
Yitso: Susumɔ oti taomɔ yɛ sane mli he Susumɔ oti Susumɔ oti ji hiamɔ nɔ ko ni sanetsomuu lɛ fɛɛ damɔ nɔ.
No ji niŋmalɔ lɛ adafitswaa ni sanemuu lɛ hiɛ ni afɔɔ no kpo jiemɔ faŋŋ yɛ yitso lɛ mli loo anaa lɛ yɛ sane lɛ mli wiemɔi komɛi amli.
No ni buaa sane lɛ naa.
Koni ona susumɔ oti yɛ sane ko mli, feemɔ nibii ni baa nɛɛ
Taomɔ mɔ loo nɔ ni he ni sane lɛ wieɔ lɛ: Ani sane lɛ miiwie gbɔmɔ, ku ko loo nɔ ko he?
Bi be ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ: Ani adafitswaa lɛ tsɔɔ be pɔtɛɛ ko?
Taomɔ he ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ yɛ: Ani sane lɛ tsɛ he ko gbɛi?
Bi nɔ hewɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ: Ani onanɔ hewɔ loo agbala nɔ ko ni tee nɔ mli atsɔɔ?
Bi bɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ ha: Ani adafitswaa nɛɛ tsɔɔ gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔ afee loo yiŋtoo ko kpo?
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ otii etɛ komɛi ni ona yɛ sane ko ni aha he.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni susumɔ oti ji, koni otao susumɔ otii etɛ yɛ sane ko ni aha he.
Gbalamɔ susumɔ otii amli koni ogbɛlɛ sane ko ni aha mli kɛtao susumɔ otii lɛ.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ
Nyɛgbaa nɔ ni susumɔ oti ji he sane.
Yɛ bo diɛŋtsɛ ojwɛŋmɔ naa lɛ tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji susumɔ oti.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ
Enɛ hako Ghana aye ehe kɛjɛ kukrudu shimɔ mli.
Kukrudu wuji ashiimɔ esa Ghana he waa yɛ bei saŋŋ ni eho lɛ mli, ni shikpɔŋ hosomɔ hu saa ehe waa gbii etɛ nɛɛ.
Klɛŋklɛŋ kukrudu shimɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ ba nɔ 1615.
Efite Sao Jorge Mɔɔ ni yɔɔ Elmina lɛ 1636, kukrudu kroko shi yɛ Axim.
Yɛ 1862 mli , lɛ kukrudu wulu ko shi yɛ Ga ni efite Christiansburg Mɔɔ lɛ, mɔji krokomɛi kɛ tsui komɛi ni amamɔ hu.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ
Okɛ susumɔ oti he nilee lɛ aŋma nii kɛtsa nitsumɔ ni ho lɛ nɔ.
Gbalamɔ onɔ lɛ mli.
Yitso: Mɛi Ajwɛŋmɔ kɛ susumɔi yɛ Sanegbaa mli Pɛɛmɔ Sane Mɛi ateŋ wiemɔ loo niŋmaa, adafitswaa, titri lɛ yɛ nɔ ko ni he miihia he loo yɛ yitso ko he.
Jwɛŋmɔ Eji susumɔ loo jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ yɛ nɔ ko he.
Yiŋshwiemɔ Eji susumɔ ni ona yɛ nɔ ko he shi jeee ni ole akɛ no ji anɔkwale lɛ loo oyɛ he nilee.
Eeenyɛ eba lɛ akɛ mɔ ko ni damɔ ehesalɛ nɔ kɛmiiwo ŋaa.
Kɛji aawie susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi ahe yɛ sane ko he lɛ:
Okɛ toiboo aje shishi
Na hemɔkɛyeli akɛ anɔkwale ni awieɔ lɛ
Wiemɔ nɔ ni ataoɔ lɛ kpoo koni okɛ nibii ni baama nɔ mi afata he.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji sane, susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi.
Gbalamɔ susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi amli bo diɛŋtsɛ koni otao susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi kɛjɛ saji ni aŋmala amli
Gbalamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ni okɛgbalaa susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi amli lɛ.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshwiemɔ
Gbalamɔ susumɔi, sane kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi lɛ amli.
Bo adesa ko toi koni nyɛkwɛ susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ mli lɛ.
Okɛ mfoniri ni aha bo ata adesa ko.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee tswaa
Daamɔ nɔ ni okase lɛ nɔ, ni okɛtsɔɔ osusumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi yɛ yitsei ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ he “Eji gbekɛbii yei/yei agbɛnaa akɛ amɛhoo nii be fɛɛ be amɛha weku lɛ ” Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛdɛɛ
Okɛ osusumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi lɛ atotoi he, koni okwɛ nibii ni kɛlɛ yaa kɛ nɔ ni tsɔɔ srɔtoi yɛ yitsei ni ahahai lɛ ahe kɛmu nikasemɔ lɛ naa.
Week 4 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Okɛ nikanemɔ vii kɛ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ nikasemɔ ni ona lɛakane nii kɛye abua jeŋshihilɛ mli haomɔi anaamɔmɔ Yitso: Nikanemɔ vii Nikanemɔ Vii Nikanemɔ vii ji nikanemɔ oyayai koni ona adafitswaa ni akɛba lɛ.
Akɛtaoɔ otii krɛdɛɛ komɛi yɛ sane mli.
Nikanemɔ vii ji nifeemɔ ni akɛkaneɔ nii oyayaayai kɛkadi susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli.
Preview: Hegbɛ ni onaa kɛleɔ nɔ ko dani oheɔ loo onaa eko.
Preview: Afeɔ enɛ kɛleɔ susumɔi ni yɔɔ kɛ bɔ ni amɛjebɔ yɔɔ ha.
Overview: Saa kwɛ: Akɛkwɛɔ nikanemɔ woji lɛ amli fitsofitso.
Saamɔ kɛ kwɛmɔ ekoŋŋ.
Kwɛ mli fɛɛ loo doo yitso lɛ nɔ Mlikwɛmɔ ekoŋŋ: Akwɛɔ mli ekoŋŋ koni akɛyɔse nɔ ni akane momo loo akane lɛ vii lɛ jogbaŋŋ Akwɛɔ mli jogbaŋŋ akɛ ani nɔ ko yɛ he ni sa akɛ atsake lo.
Gbalamɔ wiemɔ otii lɛ amli kɛ bo diɛŋtsɛ owiemɔkulibii.
Kanemɔ klɛŋklɛŋ mlijaramɔi etɛ ni yɔɔ adesa ni aha lɛ mli lɛ oyayaayai koni ohala wiemɔ otii etɛ
Okɛ wiemɔ otii lɛ awo sanemuu ntai ni kɔɔ yitso lɛ he lɛ amli.
Doomɔ sane mlijaramɔi etɛ ni yɔɔ sane ko mli nɔ.
Nyɛgbaa nikanemɔ oyayaayai gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ he sane Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Okɛ nikanemɔ oyayaayai gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ atsu kɛtao adafitswaa gbajaa ni yɔɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli
Okɛ nikanemɔ oyayaayai akane niŋmaa ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii 400 koni okɛ nilee ni ona lɛ adoo susumɔ gbajaa/ otii lɛ anɔ.
Kwɛmɔ sane lɛ mli jogbaŋŋ koni ohala wiemɔ otii lɛ.
Okɛ wiemɔ otii lɛ afee sanemuji ntai ni kɔɔ yitso ni akwɛɔ lɛ he.
Week 5 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii(s): Nyɛkɛ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ he gbɛjianɔtoi asusua nikanemɔ nɛɛ he(nkn., hiŋmɛi loo wao kɛ wiemɔi anɔ nyiɛmɔ kɛtao wiemɔkulibii loo wiemɔkukuji k.n) Yitso: Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ mligbalamɔ Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ ji nikanemɔ ni awoɔ lɛ foi kɛtaoɔ oti loo adafitswaa ko pɔtɛɛ shi jeee ni ookane wolo tso muu lɛ fɛɛ.
Dani hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ baaye emuu lɛ esa akɛ ole bɔ ni aŋma nii lɛ aha koni onu nɔ ni okaneɔ lɛ shishi koni onyɛ oyɔse adafitswaa ni otaoɔ lɛ.
Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ yeɔ buaa kɛyɔseɔ nɔ ni otaoɔ lɛ ni onaa lɛ mra.
Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ haa okaneɔ nii babaoo ni onaa suɔmɔ ohaa nikanemɔ.
Ehe hiaa akɛ obaana nɔ hewɔ ni obaakane nii hiɛhiɛ.
Yɛ enɛ gbɛfaŋ lɛ ehe hiaa akɛ obaale nɔ hewɔ ni hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ he hiaa bo lɛ, ani wolo ko, aloo intanɛti gbɛ, adafitswaa ko, wiemɔkuli ko, loo wiemɔkuku loo adesa ko ni ootao, k.n.
Ehe miihia hu akɛ ona hiamɔ nii ni sa lɛ, wolo ni sa lɛ, taomɔ ni ona akɛ adafitswaa lɛ yɛ nakai wolo lɛ mli koni ona baafa ni eyɔɔ lɛ.
Esa ni ole bɔ ni wolo lɛ jebɔ yɔɔ.
Abaanyɛ ato naa ni aha amɛ kadimɔ yibɔi kɛji wolo ni loo nibii krokomɛi loo aha amɛ A,B,D kadimɔi, Be ni akɛŋma, kn.
Be ni kaselɔi kɛkaneɔ nii hiɛhiɛ
Kaneɔ sanebimɔi kɛtaoɔ emli nɔ ni obaanyɛ hetoo oha
Kɛji eetao adafitswaa pɔtɛɛ ko nkn, be, wiemɔ oti, kn.
Bɔ ni akaneɔ nii hiɛhiɛ Kɛji ona sane ko da ni oyɔse emlijaramɔi ni ootao okane lɛ.
Bɔimɔ sane lɛ kanemɔ hiɛhiɛ, okɛ ohiŋmɛi kɛ owao anyiɛ sane lɛ nɔ oyayaayai..
Kɛji oyɔse adafitswaa, wiemɔkuli, wiemɔkuku ni otaoɔ lɛ, kanemɔ adafitswaa ni bɔle nɔ ni otaoɔ lɛ jogbaŋŋ.
Kɛji oyɔse adafitswaa ni he hiaa nitsumɔ lɛ, oŋmɛɔ otsui shi ni okaneɔ mli fitsofitso Nitsumɔ
Tsɔɔmɔ otii nɛɛ ashishi
Nikasemɔ lɛ yiŋshwiemɔ
Nikasemɔ mli kwɛmɔ ekoŋŋ
Kanemɔ sane ko ni aha koni oŋmala saji srɔtoi etɛ kɛjɛ mli
Okɛ nilee ni ona yɛ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ kɛ nikanemɔ oyayaaya mli lɛ atao adafitswaa gbajaa kɛ nilee pɔtɛɛ ko.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Sane kaimɔ
Tsɔɔmɔ nikanemɔ oyayaayai mli nilee enyɔ ni okase.
Te abaafee tɛŋŋ akɛ nikanemɔ oyayaayai mli nilee atao adafitswaa pɔtɛɛ ko yɛ sane ko mli
Kwɛmɔ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ mli nilee ni okɛtao sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ he kɛ nɔ ni ehiii yɛ nikanemɔ nɛɛ he.
Week 6 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Nyɛsusua naajiemɔi yɛ naabu he be mli ni ookwɛ toiboo kpɛŋŋ, kwɛmɔ nɔ ni onu kɛ nɔ ni obaakɛɛ.
Yitso: Naajiemɔ Mligbalamɔi Enɛ ji gbɛjianɔtoo ni gbalaa nibii amli tsɔɔ.
Eji hesalɛ gbɛ ni akɛsɔɔ nibii dani afeɔ.
Encoding: Eji gbɛjianɔtoo ni akɛtsakeɔ adafitswaa loo sane hiɛ kɛmajeɔ shweshweeshwe ni tsakemɔ ko baa mli Decoding;
Enɛ ji gbɛjianɔtoo ni akɛtsakeɔ niŋmaa ko hiɛ, ni gbalaa niŋmaa ko mli ni anuɔ shishi Enɛ haa mɛi nuɔ shishi ni amɛgbalaa mli.
Ni oyaajie sane naa otsɔɔ
Bo toi jogbaŋŋ
Esa akɛ ohe asa yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ mli.
Ha shishinumɔ ni jɛ shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ ahi shi, ni ekatsake .
Okɛ sanegbaa lɛ ahi ŋɛlɛ kome nɔ.
Gbalamɔ wiemɔ shishitsɔɔmɔ ni jɛ mɔ ko naa mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nilee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ shishitsɔɔmɔ ni jɛ mɔ ko naa mli
Tsɔɔmɔ sane wiemɔkulibii 150 ni aŋma loo wiemɔkuli 300 ni amɔ awo tsɔne nɔ shishi kɛba owiemɔ mli otsɔɔ.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshwiemɔ:
Te onuɔ wiemɔ naajielɔ shishi tɛŋŋ?
Nyɛsusua nilee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ naajiemɔ mli.
Nyɛgbalaa wiemɔ naajiemɔ srɔtoi lɛ mli Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee tswaa
Bo naawiemɔ ni aatswa loo aakane toi koni ojie naa kɛba owiemɔ mli.
Week 7 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Gbɛiwiemɔ yɛ amɛkui amli (Kɔsuaŋgbɛi, kwamaŋgbɛi, gbɛisisa/kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi, gbɛi ni anyɛɔ ataa he kn Yitso: Gbɛiwiemɔ kɛ amɛhenɔi Gbɛiwiemɔ Eji wiemɔ ko ni damɔ shi ha adesa, hei, kooloi loo nɔ ko.
Kɔsuaŋgbɛi ji gbɛi ni akɛtsɛɔ mɔ, maŋ, akrowa, nitsumɔhe loo nikasemɔhe ko.
Akɛ niŋmaa ŋaabii wulu ni jeɔ shishi.
Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni kɔɔ adesa he (nk.
Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni kɔɔ hei (nk.
Ga, Afuamaŋ, Nsakina) Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni kɔɔ nyɔji lɛ ahe (nk.
Gbii lɛ agbɛi (nk.
faa gbɛi (Nsaki, Tano, Densu, kn.)
Kwamaŋgbɛi ji gbɛi ni akɛwoɔ nibii fɛɛ ni yɔɔ je lɛ mli.
Ekomɛi ji abotia, duade, sɛi, nane kn.
Gbɛisisa loo Kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi: Gbɛisisa loo Kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi ji gbɛiwiemɔ ni akɛtsɛɔ nibii ni anaaa kɛ hiɛŋmɛi loo anyɛɛɛ he ata kɛ niji tamɔ ekpakpafeemɔ, miishɛɛ, kɛ jalɛsaneyeli.
Gbɛi ni anyɛɔ ataa he kɔɔ nibii ni anaa kɛ hiŋmɛi, anuɔ he ŋma, ataa he, aboɔ toi kɛ nibii ni asaaa naa.
Susumɔ gbɛiwiemɔ he otsɔɔ ni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enumɔ.
Gbalamɔ gbɛiwiemɔi srɔtoi ejwɛ amli ni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii nyɔŋma.
Jaramɔ gbɛiwiemɔi nɛɛ amli owo kɔsuaŋgbɛi, kwamaŋgbɛi, gbɛisisa/kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi kɛ gbɛi ni anyɛɔ ataa he
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni okɛ gbɛiwiemɔi lɛ wowoi amɛgbɛhei lɛ.
Gbalamɔ nɔ ni gbɛiwiemɔ ji mli ni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii.
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni okadi gbɛiwiemɔi ni yɔɔ mli.
Julɔi etɛ lɛ nyɛ amɛjo foi yɛ be mli ni amɛkɛ polisifoi kɛ tu ta naa lɛ.
Kpokpaanɔbii lɛ nyɛ amɛyɔse mɛi enyɔ ni gbɛi ji Jato kɛ Christopher, ni nine shɛ amɛnɔ lɛ.
Atsɔɔ akɛ amɛfata ojotswalɔi akuu ni yɔɔ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ lɛ he ni amɛjuɔ mɛi daa jenamɔ mli.
Amɛhe ni eyabaa nii lɛ, polisifoi na he ni amɛyɔɔ.
Gbi ko ni amɛtee amɛdaadaa juu ni amɛba lɛ, Polisifoi lɛ yati amɛnɔ be mli ni amɛjaa nibii ni amɛyaju lɛ.
Amɛkɛ amɛhe kɛ tui kɛ juu dadei ni yɔɔ amɛdeŋ lɛ ha polisifoi lɛ.
Mɔmɔ ni amɔmɔ amɛ lɛ gbɛ sha tamɔ la ni eje yɛ Aharabata nyɔŋ nɔ, mɛi nu he sha foi kɛtee kɛyakwɛ awuiyelɔi ni polisifoi nine eshɛ amɛnɔ lɛ yɛ polisifoi anitsumɔh lɛ.
Mɛi ni awuiyelɔi lɛ ebɔ amɛkɔkɔ akɛ amɛbaaba amɛba ju amɛ lɛ miishɛ amɛhe akɛ amɔmɔ amɛ.Gbeyeishemɔ ni yɔɔ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ lɛ fɛɛ gboi
Jaramɔ gbɛiwiemɔi lɛ owo kɔsuaŋgbɛi, kwamaŋgbɛi, gbɛisisa/kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi kɛ gbɛi ni anyɛɔ ataa he.
Kɛ kwamaŋgbɛi ekofɛɛeko ni oyɔse lɛ afee sanemuu enumɔ.
Week 8 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Jaramɔ sutsɔɔlɔi owo amɛhenɔi amli (nk.
Mɔ sɛɛgbɛtsɔɔlɔi, yifalɛ kɛ kadilɔi) kɛtsɔɔ Yitso: Sutsɔɔlɔi Henɔi Sutsɔɔlɔi Sutsɔɔlɔ ji wiemɔ ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni mɔ ko, nɔ ko loo he ko yɔɔ ha loo eji wiemɔ ni gbalaa gbɛiwiemɔ loo najiaŋgbɛi ko najiaŋ.
Gbekɛ nuu legelege kɛ nuu kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Kwaku hiɛ ka shi fe enyɛminuu lɛ.
Mɔ sɛɛgbɛtsɔɔlɔi ji sutsɔɔlɔi loo kadilɔi ni akɛsusuɔ mɔ ko.
nakai, nɛkɛ, lɛ nɛ.
Yifalɛ ji sutsɔɔlɔi loo kadilɔi ni tsɔɔ gbɛiwiemɔ yifalɛ.
Susumɔ sutsɔɔlɔ nitsumɔ ni etsuɔ.
Gbalamɔ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ sutsɔɔlɔi amli otsɔɔ.
Susumɔ ni ojara sutsɔɔlɔi loo kadilɔi nɛɛ amli owo sɛɛgbɛtsɔɔlɔ, yifalɛ kɛ kadilɔi krokomɛi lɛ amli.
Be ni wuɔ gbɛɛ shii etɛ lɛ nakai leebi lɛ, mɛi fɛɛ ni yɔɔ Alata akrowa lɛ naa ekpe shi yɛ maŋtsɛwe lɛ.
Maŋtsɛwe bii lɛ fɛɛ ewula amɛhe kɛ atade yeŋ.
Amɛkɛ kladuku efimɔ amɛyitsei ni amɛnyiɛ nane folo.
Amɛkpe kɛmiiye maŋtsɛ lɛ he odase.
Kusum tsɔɔ akɛ afii babaoo ni eho lɛ, maŋtsɛ lɛ tsɛmɛi asisai lɛ ye bua akrowa lɛ jogbaŋŋ.
Ewa amɛ gbi ko shwane be mli ni akrowa lɛ ŋmɔji fɛɛ sha.
Hɔmɔ ba ni awerɛho mɔmɔ mɔ fɛɛ mɔ yɛ akrowa lɛŋ.
Maŋtsɛ lɛ tsɛmɛi ni ye bua akrowa lɛ mli bii fɛɛ kɛ niyenii babaoo.
Akrowaŋbii lɛ tsɔ enɛ nɔ kɛwo lɛ maŋtsɛ kɛtsɔɔ amɛhiɛsɔlɛ.
Daa afi lɛ amɛbɔleɔ kutuu kɛkaiɔ nibii kpakpai ni etsɛmɛi efee kɛho lɛ.
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Reproduction / Sane kaimɔ
Yɛ osusumɔ naa lɛ, gbalamɔ nɔ ni sutsɔɔlɔ ji, ko ni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii nyɔŋma.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ: Yiŋshiewmɔ
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni okadi sutsɔɔlɔi ni yɔɔ mli.
Kpokpaanɔbii lɛ nyɛ amɛyɔse mɛi enyɔ ni gbɛi ji Jato kɛ Christopher ni nineshɛ amɛnɔ, Atsɔɔ akɛ amɛfata ojotswalɔi akuu ni yɔɔ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ ni amɛjuɔ mɛi daa jenamɔ mli lɛ ahe.
Amɛhe ni ayabaa nii lɛ, polisifoi na he ni amɛyɔɔ.
Gbi ko ni amɛtee amɛdaadaa juu ni amɛba lɛ.
Polisifoi lɛ yati amɛnɔ be mli ni amɛjaa nibii ni amɛyaju lɛ.
Amɛkɛ amɛhe kɛ tui kɛ juu dadei ni yɔɔ amɛdeŋ lɛ fɛɛ ha polisifoi lɛ.
Mɔmɔ ni amɔmɔ amɛ lɛ gbɛ sha tamɔ la eje yɛ Aharabata nyɔŋ, ni mɛi sha foi kɛtee kɛyakwɛ awuiyelɔi lɛ ni polisifoi nine eshɛ amɛnɔ lɛ yɛ polisifoi anitsumɔ lɛ.
Mɛi ni awuiyelɔi lɛ ebo amɛkɔkɔ akɛ amɛbaaba amɛba ju amɛ lɛ miishɛ amɛhe akɛ amɔmɔ amɛ.
Gbeyeishemɔ ni yɔɔ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ fɛɛ laajei
Jaramɔ sutsɔɔlɔi ni oyɔse lɛ owo sɛɛgbɛtsɔɔlɔ, yifalɛ kɛ kadilɔ shishi.
Kɔlɔmɔ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enumɔ kɛjɛ sutsɔɔlɔi lɛ mli kɛfee sanemuu nta.
Week 9 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otiis
Jaramɔ feemɔwiemɔ owo feemɔwiemɔi ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ, kɛ nɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ enyɔ loo.
bɔ ni, he ni, be ni, degree).
Yitso: Feemɔwiemɔ Henɔi Feemɔwiemɔ Eji wiemɔ loo sanekuku ni tsɔɔ nifeemɔ ko loo bɔ ni nɔkofeemɔ ko yɔɔ ha.
Anaa lɛ akɛ nɔnalɔ yɛ feemɔwiemɔ gbɛfaŋ.
Jaramɔ feemɔwiemɔi lɛ amli owo;
nɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ, kɛ nɔ ni eŋɔɔɔ nɔnalɔ shishi.
Feemɔwiemɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ ko akɛ yigbelɔ.
Kofi ye niyenii lɛ.
Akwele shi loo lɛ.
Oko kpɔ shitɔ lɛ.
Feemɔwiemɔ ni eŋɔɔɔ nɔnalɔ ko akɛ yigbelɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii: Feemɔwiemɔi ni eŋɔɔ yigbelɔ:
Abifao lɛ miida.
Gbekɛ yoo lɛ la Feemɔwiemɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔi enyɔ loo etɛ akɛ yigbelɔ dani shishinuumɔ lɛ jeɔ kpo.Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii;
Kɔ wolo lɛ oha mi
Susumɔ feemɔwiemɔ he otsɔɔ.
Gbalamɔ feemɔwiemɔ henɔi ni yɔɔ amli.
Susumɔ ni ojara feemɔwiemɔ nɛɛ mli awo feemɔwiemɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ, kɛ nɔ ni eŋɔɔɔ nɔnalɔ shishi
Faa lɛ mlikwɔlɛ shɛɛɛ mitai nyɔŋmai enumɔ shikulɛ masumɔ ni mawu kilomitai saŋ yɛ faa ni mli kwɔ ni ehiɛ hu tse mli.
Jɛmɛ miije fu baa he.
Be mli ni mifee miyiŋ akɛ mabote faa lɛ mli lɛ, mikɛ mitu lɛ ma minaa kɛ sɔlemɔ, mishane kɛbote ŋmɔtɔ lɛ mli.
Faa lɛ mli yɛ kulɔɔ.
Shi gbeyei sɔŋŋ ha fɛi ye mi ni minyɛɛɛ masere oyaoyai.
Faa lɛ teŋ gbɛ kwɔlɛ ha mitsitsi yashi ŋmɔtɔ kpɔ ko.
Mijwɛŋ akɛ baa ni hewɔ lɛ gbeyei ni mɔ mi ha tu ni ma minaa lɛ gbee faa lɛ mli.
Mitee hiɛ mitai fioo lɛ, naa bu voo yɛ nu lɛ mli ekoŋŋ.
Be ni mihɛleɔ shi lɛ mishɛ faa lɛ sɛɛ.
Mikɛ hekpokpomɔ sha foi kɛje kpo kɛjɛ faa lɛ mli.
Agbenaa shi milaaje mitu lɛ.
Jaramɔ feemɔwiemɔi ni ona loo oyɔse lɛ owo feemɔwiemɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ kɛ nɔ ni eŋɔɔɔ nɔnalɔ mli.
Kɛ feemɔwiemɔ ni ona lɛ afee sanemuu.
Kɛ ohetoo lɛ atsɔɔ oklaasibii lɛ koni nyɛsusu he.
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Sane kaimɔ
Gbalamɔ feemɔwiemɔ mli ko ni oha henɔi lɛ.
Gbalamɔ henɔi ni ona lɛ mli otsɔɔ.
Ha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enumɔ yɛ feemɔwiemɔi srɔtoi lɛ ashishi.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshiewmɔ
Kɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kome afee sanemuu yɛ feemɔwiemɔ srɔtoi lɛ fɛɛ ashishi.
Kɔlɔmɔ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enyɔ kɛjɛ nɔ ni oha lɛ mli kɛfee sanemuu nta.
Kɔ sane ni naa shɛɔ wiemɔkulibii ohai enumɔ ni kɔɔ yitsei nɛɛ ateaŋ ekome he.
Kusum hesɛɛnamɔi, STEM, Hewalɛ ni akɛ tsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ, GESI, kn.
Kanemɔ sane lɛ ni ohala feemɔwiemɔi nyɔŋmai enyɔ kɛwo feemɔwiemɔ henɔi amli.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ ni mli kwɔ
Kwɛmɔ ni ogbala sɛɛnamɔi ni ona yɛ sanemuu lɛ mli lɛ otsɔɔ.
Yitso: Mlijaramɔi yɛ Mligbalamɔi amli.
Mligbalalɔi Mligbalalɔi ji wiemɔ ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni afee nɔ ko aha, bɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ eha aloo be ni nɔ ko tee nɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii yɛ sanemuu mli
Helatsɛ lɛ ye nii jogbaŋŋ.
Nɛgbɛ sukuubii lɛ yaa?
gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔ afee nɔ ko, he ni nɔ ko tee nɔ yɛ, be ni nɔ ko tee nɔ, bɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ eha, nɔ hewɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔ gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔ kɛfee nɔ ko yɛ sanemuu mli.
Nk., blɛo, waa, oyayai, kn Mligabalalɔi ni tsɔɔ he ni nɔ ko tee nɔ yɛ yɛ sanemuu mli.
Nk., he fɛɛ he, ŋshɔ lɛ mli, Ga, kn.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔ be ni nɔ ko tee nɔ yɛ sanemuu mli.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ eha yɛ sanemuu mli.
Nk., ejaakɛ, nɔ hewɔ, kn.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔbɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ ha yɛ sanemuu mli.
Ŋmaa nɔ ni mligbalalɔ ji koni oha henɔi lɛ.
Gbalamɔ mligbalalɔi henɔi lɛ amli koni oha eko fee eko nɔkwɛmɔnɔi enumɔ.
Kɛ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enyɔ ni oŋma lɛ afee sanemuu
Kɔ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enyɔ ni oŋma lɛ ni okɛwo mligbalalɔ henɔi ni okase lɛ ko
ni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni okɛ wo henɔi lɛ ashishi.
Gbalamɔ mligbalalɔ mli otsɔɔ ni oha henɔi lɛ.
Gbalamɔ henɔi lɛ amli koni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii.
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ koni ohala mligbalalɔi ni onaa yɛ mli.
Okwaayeli he nikasemɔ ni akaseɔ yɛ Ghana sukuu ewo sukuubii ekaa waa fe tsutsu.
Sukuubii komɛi tsaa amɛnikasemɔ lɛ nɔ kɛyaa hiɛ ni ekomɛi hu tsɔmɔɔ okwaafoi kɛji amɛgbe siniɔ hia sukuu naa.
Enɛ haa akpaa gbɛ akɛ, nikasemɔ ni amɛyana kɛjɛ sukuu kɛtsɔ nikanemɔ kɛ ŋma mli baawa okwaa yɛ gbɛjianɔ strɔtoi ni amɛyakase ni akɛ gbɛi hei srɔtoi baatsu nii.
Enɛ baaha ni niyenii aba babaoo.
Amralo lɛ ewa kɛto gbɛjianɔ srɔtoi ni baawa okwaayeli yɛ Ghana bɔ ni afee ni niyenii babaoo aba maŋ lɛ mli.
Nikasemɔ ni eteenɔ yɛ okwaayeli nikasemɔ he hewɔ lɛ, naagbai babaoo ni yɔɔ bei ko lɛ naa ebashi kɛtsɔ nɛkɛ nikasemɔi nɛɛ amli.
Bɔ ni anyɛ ni awo okwaafoi ahewalɛ ni amɛnyɛ amɛtsu nii hewɔ lɛ, amralo lɛ eŋmɛ shikatoohei srɔtoi ahegbɛ ni amɛfa okwaafoi ashika ni hekpa tsɔ ko bɛ mli.
Kɛfata he lɛ, amɛkɛ shika ayato yɛ shikatoohe bibii ni bɛŋkɛ amɛ lɛ bɔ ni afee ni amɛna shika kɛtsu nii jogbaŋŋ.s
Buamɔ mligbalalɔi ni ona lɛ anaa owo mligbalalɔ henɔi lɛ ashishi: be ni nɔ ko tee nɔ, mligabalɔi ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ, bei abɔ ni afee nɔ ko, bɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ aha.
Kɔlɔmɔ mligbalalɔi enumɔ ni ona, kɔ mligbalalɔ henɔi lɛ kɛfee wiemɔmuu nta.
Week 10 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii(s): Okɛ gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋ gbɛi ahe mlai afee wiemɔmuji Yitso: Gbɛiwiemɔ (najiaŋgbɛi) kɛ sanemuu feemɔ Gbɛiwiemɔ Gbɛiwiemɔ ji wiemɔ ko ni yeɔ gbɔmɔ, nɔ ko, susumɔ ko loo he ko najiaŋ..
Esani wɔle akɛ sanemuji titri fɛɛ hiɛɔ gbɛiwiemɔ kome kɛ hooo kwraa lɛ .
sɛɛgbɛ tsɔɔlɔ (lɛ) fɔɔ gbɛiwiemɔi asɛɛ nyiɛmɔ shi jeee be fɛɛ be Najiaŋgbɛi Najiaŋgbɛi ji wiemɔkuli ni nyɛɔ damɔɔ shi haa gbɛiwiemɔ sanekuku.
Nɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ baa lɛ: mi, lɛ, e, bo, wɔ, nyɛ, amɛ, .
Srɔtoi yɛ mlai ni kulaa sanemuu feemɔ kɛ gbɛiwiemɔi mli.
Etsakeɔ yɛ Ghana wiemɔ fɛɛ wiemɔ mli.
Titri lɛ, yɛ gbɛiwiemɔi agbɛfaŋ lɛ eyaa lɛ nɛkɛ:
Feelɔ yɛ sanemuu mli: nkn., Kofi tee sukuu .
Yɛ enɛ mli lɛ Kofi ji feelɔ lɛ
Nɔnalɔi niyɔɔ sanemuu mli (nɔnalɔ diɛtsɛ loo helɔ): Miha Okwei pɛn (Nɔnalɔ diɛtsɛ).
Yifalɛ (yɛ Ghana wiemɔi lɛ ekomɛi amli lɛ): E.g.
Rɔba tsɛnsi lɛ yɛ Adu ŋɔɔ.
Yitso ni tere gbɛiwiemɔ wiemɔkuku ji gbɛi ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli lɛ.
Akutu tso kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Yɛ enɛ mli lɛ yitso ni tere wiemɔkuku lɛ ji tso
Mla ni kulaa najiaŋgbɛi ni akɛfeɔ wiemɔmuu tsakeɔ yɛ Ghana wiemɔi lɛ amli.
Titri lɛ, najiaŋgbɛi damɔɔ shi akɛ: Najiaŋgbɛi ni yeɔ gbɛiwiemɔ najiaŋ, no hewɔ atsɛɔ lɛ najiaŋ gbɛi lɛ
Feelɔ yɛ wiemɔmuu lɛ mli.
Nɔnalɔi niyɔɔ sanemuu mli (nɔnalɔ diɛtsɛ loo helɔ) (direct or indirect): Akwei ha lɛ pɛn (nɔnalɔ diɛtsɛ loo helɔ).
Ama ni ha lɛ pɛn lɛ.
Yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ, najiaŋgbɛi ni damɔ feelɔ gbɛhe lɛ akɛ enii ni efeɔ (feemɔwiemɔ) lɛ fataa ehe ni aŋmaa lɛ akɛ wiemɔkuli kome.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji gbɛiwiemɔi koni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nii nyɔŋma.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji najiaŋgbɛi koni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nii enumɔ.
Halamɔ nɔkwɛmɔ nii ni aha lɛ mli etɛ yɛ kui enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ mli koni okɛfee wiemɔmuu ntai.
nTsɔɔmɔ gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛii ashishi.
Ha gbɛiwiemɔi nyɔŋma kɛ najiaŋgbɛi enumɔ.
Kɔ emli etɛ yɛ fɛɛ eko shishi koni okɛfee wiemɔmuu nta.
Ŋɛlɛ 2: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛdɛɛ
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ akane sane koni amɛsusu he.
Amɛkwɛ mlai ni yɔɔ he lɛ kɛtao tɔmɔi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ koni amɛkɛŋmɛ jaraŋ ni asusu he.
Week 11 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Okɛ kadilɔi/ sutsɔɔlɔi ahe mlai afee wiemɔmuji Yitso: Okɛ sutsɔɔlɔi/kadilɔi afee wiemɔmuu (Wiemɔmuji feemɔ kɛ kadilɔi /sutsɔɔlɔi) Sutsɔɔlɔi/Kadilɔi Sutsɔɔlɔi/kadilɔi ji wiemɔi loo nifeemɔi ni tsɔɔ mɔ ko loo nɔ ko su loo jieɔ kadimɔ ko kpo etsɔɔ.
Ehaa anaa nɔ ko pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ mɔ ko he.
Akɛsusuɔ mɔ ko loo nɔ ko ni anaa ejebɔ, enifeemɔ, esu kɛ ebaŋ ni ehiɛ ni no haa efeɔ srɔto kɛjɛɔ mɛi krokomɛi ahe Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii
Nuu lɛ kwɔ ni etaloo hu.
Ajoa he yɛ oya fe enyɛmi nuu lɛ.
Ekadiɔ gbɛiwiemɔ ko ni yɔɔ wiemɔmuu mli: Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Okpɔŋɔ diŋ lɛ joɔ foi fe okpɔŋɔi lɛ fɛɛ.
Kadilɔi/ sutsɔɔlɔi nyiɛɔ gbɛiwiemɔi ni awieɔ amɛhe lɛ sɛɛ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ, Wuɔ agbo lɛ damɔ wuɔ tsu lɛ mli.
Okɛ kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi enumɔ afee sanemuji koni okɛ enumɔ ni eshwɛ lɛ hu afee sanemuu ntai
Kanemɔ sanekuku ko koni oŋmala sutsɔɔlɔi kɛ kadilɔi ni yɔɔ mli.
Okɛ sutsɔɔlɔi/kadilɔi ni ona lɛ afee sane haŋtsiŋ.
Klasi muu lɛ
Tsɔɔlɔ kɛ gbekɛbii asusu mlai ni kulaa kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi kɛnitsumɔ hesanemuu feemɔ he.
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshwiemɔ
Okɛ kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi nɛɛ mli enumɔ afee: wiemɔmuji ni baa nɛɛ:
Wiemɔ haŋtsiŋ Tsɔɔmɔ mlai ni kulaa sutsɔɔlɔi kɛfeɔ wiemɔmuji yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 Jwɛŋmɔ krɛdɛɛ
Kanemɔ sane koni nyɛsusu he.
Kwɛmɔ mlai lɛ koni oŋmala tɔmɔi fɛɛ ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli..
(Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ aha sanekuku ko ni tɔmɔi yɛ kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi ahe nitsumɔ lɛ mli, sane lɛ mliwalɛ ahi kaselɔi aŋɛlɛ nɔ.
Week 12 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Feemɔwiemɔ kɛ mligbalalɔi kɛ wiemɔmuu feemɔ Yitso: Okɛ feemɔwiemɔ kɛ mligbalalɔi afee wiemɔmuu Feemɔwiemɔ Feemɔwiemɔ ji wiemɔkuli ko ni tsɔɔ nifeemɔ ko ni etee nɔ, miiya nɔ, loo baaya nɔ yɛ wiemɔmuu ko mli.
Ni no ji feemɔwiemɔ wiemɔkuku lɛ yitso.
Feemɔwiemɔi komɛi nɛ: nu, tee, ba kn kn Nɔkwɛmɔnɔi yɛ wiemɔmuji amli
Kojo ye omɔ kɛ flɔɔ.
Mlai Mlai ni kulaa feemɔwiemɔi kɛ sanemuu feemɔ lɛ ekomɛi nɛ:
Etsɔɔ nɔ ni feelɔ lɛ feɔ: Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Okpɔŋɔ fioo lɛ joɔ foi fe ekrokomɛi lɛ fɛɛ.
Etsuɔ nii akɛ hewiemɔ wiemɔkuku lɛ yitso.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Nuu lɛ joɔ foi fe mɛi ni eshwɛ lɛ.
Kɛji najiaŋgbɛi ye gbɛiwiemɔ najiaŋ lɛ, aŋmaa ekɛ feemɔwiemɔ lɛ atsaa akɛ wiemɔkuli.
Mligbalalɔ Mligbalalɔ tsɔɔ bɔ ni nifeemɔ ko tee nɔ ha.
Etsɔɔ sharamɔ ni ka mligbalalɔ lɛ kɛ feemɔwiemɔ lɛ teŋ Etsɔɔ be, he ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ yɛ kɛ bɔ ni afee lɛ aha, kɛnii.
Nuu lɛ ba nyɛ
Nifeemɔ lɛ ba naagbee mra tsɔ.
Mlai Mlai lɛ ekomɛi nɛ:
Edamɔɔ shi akɛ mligbalalɔ wiemɔkuku lɛ yitso.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ.Nuu lɛ wie waa diɛŋtsɛ.
Etsuɔ nii akɛ mligbalalɔ ehaa esasoi krokomɛi.Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Nu lɛ tswa oya.
Mligbalalɔi ni kɛ wiemɔweku ko tsaaa lɛ aŋmaa lɛ akɛ wiemɔkuli .
Tsɔɔlɔ aha nɔkwɛmɔ nii pii efata he.
Gbalamɔ feemɔwiemɔ kɛ mligbalalɔ mli otsɔɔ koni oha fɛɛ eko nɔkwɛmɔnɔi nyɔŋma.
Okɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii ni oha lɛ afee wiemɔmuji oha:
Tsɔɔmɔ feemɔwiemɔi srɔtoi ahe nii ni okase lɛ
Gbalamɔ feemɔwiemɔ kɛ mligbalalɔ mli.
Ha feemɔwiemɔi enumɔ kɛ mligbalalɔi hu enumɔ.
Okɛ feemɔwiemɔi kɛ mligbalalɔi ni oha lɛ afee wiemɔmuji.
Owiemɔmuji lɛ ahi yitsei nɛɛ shishi.
Wiemɔmuu ii.wiemɔmuu nta iii.Wiemɔmuu Haŋtsiŋ Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛdɛɛ
Kanemɔ sane ko koni nyɛsusu he.
Daamɔ mlai ni nyɛkase lɛ nɔ kɛhala tɔmɔi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ fɛɛ.
Week 13 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Ŋmaa sanegbaa ko.
Yitso: Gbaa saneŋmaa Nyɛkwɛa nilee “Sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ni nyɛkai nyɛhe.
Sanemuu oti ji sane ni jieɔ yiŋtoo oti ni yɔɔ mlijaa lɛ mli lɛ kpo.
Sanemuu oti walɔi ji wiemɔi ni yeɔ buaa ni sanemuu oti lɛ jeɔ kpo.
Hesalɛ ni akɛbuaa nilee naa awoɔ mlijaramɔi amli Enɛmɛi fata he: Susumɔ otii ni bɛŋkɛ lɛ anaabuamɔ, nifeemɔi anaatoo yɛ amɛbei amli, nibii ni maa nɔ ko nɔ mi kɛ tɛŋmɔi, nibii ni akɛtoɔ eko he kɛ nifeemɔi ni kɛ nɔ ko ba, kɛnii.
Saneŋmaa Eji niŋmaa ko loo nifeemɔi loo gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ atsɔɔ jwɛŋmɔ yɛ yitsei komɛi ahe.
Gbaa saneŋmaa Gbaa Gbaa sane lɛ wojiaŋ sane ni, ni edamɔɔ yitso ko nɔ .
hesalɛ ni akɛwieɔ, koni mɔ ko atsɔɔ nibii amli ni etsɔmɔ hu.
Esa ni ole gbaa sane jebɔ ni onyɛ okɛtsu nii.
Abaanyɛ adamɔ afoata loo anɔkwale saji agbɛjianɔtoo nɔ.
Gbaa sane gbɛkpamɔ ji akɛ akɛ gbɛjianɔtoo kpakpa ni ato aha adesatamɔ atsu nii, tamɔ sane lɛ yitso, hekɛhɛlɛmɔ, Ga sɛɛsɛɛ, kn.
Yɛ gbɛ nɛɛ nɔ lɛ kanelɔ lɛ jwɛŋmɔ baanyiɛ sane lɛ sɛɛ Akɛ klɛŋklɛŋ mɔ najiaŋgbɛi”mi” ni fɔɔ ŋmaa koni ŋmalɔ lɛ loo kanelɔ lɛ ana ehe loo toiboloi ana lɛ akɛ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ehe sane ni.
Ehaa anaa nɔmimaa ahaa sanebimɔi ni jeɔ kpo lɛ, asɔɔ wiemɔi lɛ, ni aŋmaa yɛ eho be mli.
Esa akɛ atoɔ nifeemɔ bei lɛ anaa taakɛ eba lɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ, ni sane lɛ amɔ toibolɔi ajwɛŋmɔ be ni eyaa hiɛ lɛ.Wielɔ lɛ wiemɔi lɛ asha jwɛŋmɔŋ mfoniri yɛ toibolɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mli, ni enyɛ ekɛ fɔŋwiemɔi atsu nii kɛgbala toibolɔi ajwɛŋmɔ kɛba sane lɛ nɔ Gbaa saneŋmaa nɔkwɛmɔnɔi:
Ŋmaa sane yɛ oklɛŋklɛŋ gbi yɛ sukuu he.
Ŋmaa sane yɛ niyenii ni osumɔɔ waa he.
Ŋmaa sane yɛ oblonya gbɛhamɔ ni ho nɔŋŋ lɛ he.
Ŋmaa sane yɛ nɔ ko ni ba onɔ ni okɛ kase nii he.
Ŋmaa sane yɛ omɔdɛŋbɔɔ ko nI ohiɛ mɛɔ he kɛ gbɛ nɔ ni otsɔ kɛfee he.
Gbaa saneŋmaa jebɔ Hiɛgbelemɔ Gbaa saneŋmaa hiɛgbelemɔ: Sane ni gbalaa jwɛŋmɔ: okɛ sanebimɔ, abɛ, wiemɔ ko ni gbalaa jwɛŋmɔ loo nɔ ko ni he miihia toibolɔi aje shishi.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ sane ko ni jieɔ yitso lɛ kpo.
Sanetso mlijaramɔ Okɛ sanemuu oti lɛ aŋmɛ jaraŋ, Sanemuu oti walɔi, kɛ naamuu loo naamuu hewalɛwoo wiemɔs.
Ha toibolɔi nibii ni tee nɔ fɛɛ lɛ amligbalamɔi loo nibii ni otsɔmɔ mli ni maa sane lɛ nɔ mi.
Ŋmaa nɔ ko yɛ be atsu lɛ he.
Kwɛmɔ gbaa sane jebɔi lɛ koni otsu fɛɛ he nii.
Saamɔ okwɛ oti hewɔ ni otsɔɔ nɛkɛ yitso nɛɛ Saamɔ okwɛ otii lɛ ekoŋŋ akɛ otsu fɛɛ he nii lo.
Ha naamuu wiemɔ ni baadoo sanemuu lɛ fɛɛ nɔ.
Nyɛsusua nɔ ni ji saneŋmaa
Nyɛsusua nɔ ni ji gbaa saneŋmaa koni nyɛtsɔɔ jebɔi ni ehiɛ.
Kɔ yitso ko koni otsɔɔ gbɛi anɔ ni obaatsɔ oŋma sane kpakpa kɛ nilee ni ona lɛ.
Mɛni ji gbaa saneŋmaa?
Ŋmalamɔ gbaa saneŋmaa mli otii lɛ.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Nyɛsusua otii ni yɔɔ gbaa saneŋmaa mli.
Nyɛkwɛa gbaa sane ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ koni nyɛŋmala otii ni ehiɛ lɛ fɛɛ: Atsɔse bo ni oda yɛ Ghana Dalɛ yɛ Ghana yɛ 1990 bei lɛ amli, eji haomɔ waa nakai bei lɛ amli kɛha mi.
Eji be mli ni Africa Anai maŋ nɛɛ miibɔ ehe mɔdɛŋ kɛje shika haomɔi ni eyɔɔ mli ni asusu akɛ asraafoi amaŋkuramɔ kɛba, ni egu shika he ŋaatsɔɔ nitsumɔi fɛɛ lɛ.
Nɛkɛ asraafoi anɔyeli nɛɛ, kɛyashi he ko lɛ, ejwa maŋ ni be ko ni eho lɛ damɔ enaji anɔ ni enyiɛ Africa heyeli hiɛ kwraa lɛ ;
Nifitemɔ nɛɛ ha nijiaŋwujee ba ni mɛi bɛ hiɛnɔkamɔ dɔŋŋ fe hefimɔ.
Amralofoi ni yɔɔ no beiaŋ, National Democratic Congress lɛ bɔ ehe mɔdɛŋ wo mlai ni naa wa akɛ eeekpɔ maŋ lɛ kɛbadamɔ enaji anɔ lo.
Mikaiɔ akɛ shika mla gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ eko ni amɛkɛba ji Valued Added Tax too lɛ, ni esa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ he pɛpɛɛpɛ yɛ Ghana.
Gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ ha nibii ajara tee ŋwɛi fe nine ni esa niyenii ni misumɔɔ lɛ hu he.
Enɛ ha mikai nɔ ko ni ba nakai be lɛ mli.
Enɛ ha mikai nɔ ko ni mitsɔ mli nakai bei lɛ amli.
Glasiŋ tsɛnsii ni maŋkuralɔi ni kɛ NDC shiɔ mpleŋ kɛ NGO bii kɛ CSO bii tswia lɛ.
Fɛɛ sɛɛ lɛ ana akɛ mla lɛ tsu nii kɛ.
Mikaiɔ akɛ mɛi gboi yɛ nakai glasiŋ tsɛnsii lɛ tswaa mli.
Naakpɛɛ sane ji akɛ, amralofoi ni ba yɛ Jerry John Rawlings kɛ NDC ku lɛ sɛɛ lɛ nyɛɛɛ afo nɛkɛ Valued Added Taɩ mla nɛɛ mli.
Mla nɛɛ kɛ wɔ ehi shi yɛ Ghana kɛbashi ŋmɛnɛ.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛɛ
Ŋmaa Gbaa sane, mlijaramɔi etɛ yɛ nɔ hewɔ ni sa akɛ ana jwɛŋmɔ yɛ wiemɔ kome he.
Week 14 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Ŋmaa susumɔ kɛ tsɔɔmɔ wolo Yitso: Susumɔ kɛ tsɔɔmɔ Twsiamɔ sanemuu kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ nɔ.
Twsiamɔ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛŋmaa sane yɛ mlijaramɔi amli.
Gbaa saneŋmaa Saneŋmaa henɔ ko ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni afeɔ nɔ ko ahaa;
nɔ ko, gbɔmɔ, he, hesalɛ, henumɔ loo sane ko he kn., ni aŋmaa kɛ hesalɛ ko bɔ ni afee ni ejie nɔ fee nɔ he ni awieɔ lɛ ŋmiiŋmi.
Gbaa saneŋmaa hiɔ sui ni jieɔ henumɔ ko kpo yɛ nibii pɔtɛɛ he ni awieɔ lɛ he.
Gbaa saneŋmaa jebɔ Hiɛgbelemɔ: Enɛ gbalaa mɔ jwɛŋmɔ kɛbaa sane pɔtɛɛ he ni awieɔ lɛ.
Anyɛɔ ajeɔ shishi kɛ sanebimɔ ni he ehiaaa hetoo.
Sanetso: Enɛ gbalaa sane he pɔtɛɛ ni awieɔ lɛ.
Kuku fɛɛ kuku hiɛ sanemuu oti ko ni ewieɔ he ni no tsɛɔ kanelɔ lɛ toi sɛɛ kɛha nibii ni amɛkpaa gbɛ lɛ.
Ehaa anaa nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni sa akɛ ana kɛ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni sa akɛ ana.
Naamuu: Ewieɔ loo emaa sanetso ni yɔɔ sane kuku ni ji ekome lɛ nɔ mi yɛ nibii pɔtɛɛ hewɔ ni agbaa sane lɛ.
(Kaawie yitso kroko he yɛ kuku nɛɛ mli.
Kɛji oyɛ oti krokomɛi ni sa lɛ, okɛ nakai oti lɛ awo kuku kroko mli.) Yitsei ni kɔɔ gbaa saneŋmaa he:
Gbaa sini loo niyenii ni osumɔɔ waa he otsɔɔ,
Maŋ heyeli gbijurɔ
Gbaa susumɔi ni kɔɔ maŋheyeli he.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni osusuɔ akɛ Ghana shihilɛ aji aha bo.
Kɛ fɔŋwiemɔ atsu nii
Kɛ wiemɔ wuji ni tsɛɔ mɔ toi sɛɛ atsu nii.
Ŋaagbɛ ni atsɔɔ nɔ kɛŋmaa gbaa sane
Taomɔ yitso ni aha bo lɛ dani okɛ ŋaa loo hesalɛ aje shishi.
Ŋmaa susumɔi lɛ kukuji.
Ŋmaa bɔ ni obaato naa oha.
Ŋmaa ogbaa sane lɛ.
Obaanyɛ otsɔ ŋaagbɛi ni oŋma lɛ kɛŋma ogbaa sane lɛ, enɛ baaha oniŋmaa lɛ afee mlɛo.
Saamɔ okane ni okwɛ tɔmɔi ni yɔɔ ogbaa sane lɛ mli ni osaa fɛɛ.
Gbaa sane lɛ otsɔɔ.
Susumɔ jebɔi ni kɔɔ gbaa saneŋmaa he ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kɛpia ohetoo lɛ.
Kɔ yitso ni osumɔɔ ni odamɔ gbaa saneŋmaa jebɔi lɛ nɔ kɛŋma.
Mɛni ji sane oti kɛ sane oti walɔi?
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Susumɔ gbaasane jebɔi lɛ ahe ni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii.
Ŋmaa gbaa sane ni hiɛ mlijaramɔi etɛ yɛ yitso ko he.
(nk., kusumii hesɛɛnamɔi, hemɔkɛyeli, kn.), STEM, GESI, sane ni kɔɔ falefa he, maŋ nɛɛ kɛ maŋsɛɛ ahaumɔ, kn.).
Nɔ ni kɔɔ falefale he, maŋ nɛɛ kɛ maŋsɛɛ nɔ, kn.).
Week 15 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa Yitso: Nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa Nɔkofeemɔ Saneŋmaa Enɛmɛi ji saneŋmaa ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni afeɔ nɔ ko loo agbalaa nɔ ko mli atsɔɔ.loo asusuɔ nɔ ko atsɔɔ.
Ehaa wɔnaa lɛ faŋŋ ni wɔnuɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ shishi faŋŋ .
Gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ aŋmaa Nɔkofeemɔ sane Akɔ kɛjɛ he ko loo adoo nɔ kɛjɛ sanebimɔ ko mli Nɔkofeemɔ:Saneŋmaa lɛ hiɛgbelemɔ Nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa.
Ajeɔ shishi kɛ hiɛgbelemɔ.
Enɛ mɔɔ kanelɔ lɛ jwɛŋmɔ, Kɛkɛ lɛ okɛ oti lɛ efɔ jaraŋ, koni otsɔɔ nɔ pɔtɛɛ ni otaoɔ ofee.
Mlijaramɔi ni yɔɔ yitso lɛ mli.
Biɛ ji he ni onyɛɔ otsuɔ yitso lɛ he nii fɛɛ kɛmɔɔ shi yɛ Efɔɔ kaa akɛ mlijaramɔi etɛ pɛ akɛtsuɔ nii shi kɛji oti lɛ mli kwɔ lɛ enyɛɔ efaa fe etɛ.
Biɛ ji he ni onaa gbɛ kɛŋmaa nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni otaoɔ oŋma lɛ yɛ;
gbɛjianɔtoi lɛ fɛɛ,jwɛŋmɔi ni ohiɛ loo otii amli ni ogbalaa.
Nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa mli lɛ akɛ nɔdoomɔ feɔ naamuu.
Okɛ kaselɔi baagba he sane kɛbi amɛyiŋtoo kɛ emligbalamɔ kɛkɛ lɛ okɛ ojwɛŋmɔ efɔ jaraŋ kɛmu naa.
Tsɔɔmɔ wiemɔ ‘saneŋmaa’ lɛ shishi
Mɛni ji nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa?
Gbalamɔ nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa otii kɛ jebɔi amli koni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii.
Ŋmaa nɔkofeemɔ sane yɛ yitso ni aha he.
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Strand: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ mankuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo 4 Sub-Strand: Kusum nifeemɔ 4 YITSO: Mumɔi, loo jemawɔji agbɛi 6 YITSO: Kpokuafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ hebejemɔ: 8 YITSO: Kpokuafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ he susumɔ 11
Oti: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ mankuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo 14 Oti mlijaa: Kusum nifeemɔ 14 YITSO: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo (Shia) 15 YITSO: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo 17
Oti: Jeŋnilee ni kɔɔ Ghana wiemɔ he kasemɔ 24 Oti mlijaa: Akrɔkuaŋ saji/ŋaaŋ saji 24 YITSO: Ŋkpaiyeli 26 YITSO: Yarawoo lalai (enitsumɔ kɛ sɛɛnamɔi).
29 YITSO: Afoata mli ootui 31 YITSO: Afoata sane henyamɔ 33 ACKNOWLEDGEMENTS 36
Kusum Nifeemɔi kɛ Mankuramɔ Gbɛjianɔtoo Kusum nifeemɔ 2 2 3 2 2 5 2 2 4 Shikwɛɛ loo gamɛi amaŋkuramɔ Gbɛjianɔtoo 1 1 2 1 1 3 - - -
Ana sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ wowɛi agbɛi ahe.
Ha subaŋ kpakpai kɛ sɛɛnamɔi ni anaa yɛ kpkuafoaiŋ hetsuu kɛ hebɛjemɔ yɛ Gamɛi asateŋ kɛ mɛi kroko ahe.
Afee abifao kpojiemɔ kusum lɛ atsɔɔ.
Akwɛ ni afee bɔ ni anu hetsuumɔ shishi atsɔɔ.
Nikasemɔ otii: Nyɛtaoa mumɔi loo jemawɔji ni yɔɔ kpɔ lɛ mli agbɛi, hei ni amɛjɛ kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni amɛhiɛ nakai gbɛii lɛ YITSO: Mumɔi, loo jemawɔji agbɛi Mumɔi/Jemawɔji Mumɔ ko, jemawɔŋ.
Eji mumɔ ko loo jemawɔŋ, tamɔ wɔŋ loo wɔyoo ni aheɔ lɛ ayeɔ yɛ kpɔ ko mli akɛ eyeɔ ebuaa amɛnifeemɔi loo eyɛ hewalɛ yɛ amɛnifeemɔi anɔ, hewɔ lɛ ajaa lɛ.
Wɔji nɛɛ ahenɔi ni yɔɔ maji krokomɛi ajamɔ mli lɛ ekomɛi ji Zeus, Apollo, Athena, Ares, Aphrodite, Poseidon, Hades, Thanatos, Dionysus, Hera, Artemis, Hephaestus kɛ Hermes.
Anaa wɔji nɛɛ ekomɛi yɛ blema maji tamɔ Greek jamɔi tamɔ Norse mli, jemawɔji tamɔ Thor, Odin, Tyr, Frey, Freya, Baldur kɛ Loki lɛ abuɔ amɛ jogbaŋŋ ni ajaa amɛ.
Yɛ Ghana lɛ kpokpa wuji loo wiemɔ fɛɛ wiemɔ yɛ ejemawɔji ni eheɔ amɛɛ eyeɔ.
Nyama kɛ Bosumtwi yɛ Kumase .Jemawɔji nɛɛ ekomɛi yɛ Ga ni nomɛi ji: Ga: Dantu, Nae, Sakumɔ, Kɔɔle, Klote, La Kpa, k.n.k.n..
Wɔŋ gbɛi ni akɛhaa adesai Eji gbɛi ni akɛhaa adesai ni kɛ wɔji nɛɛ naa sharamɔ ni amɛheɔ amɛ amɛyeɔ ni amɛjaa amɛ.
Wɔji komɛi agbɛi: Gua, Oyaadu, Gbɔbu, Sakumɔ, Naaye, Dantu, Kɔɔle, Afiee, Klɔte, Densu kɛ ekrokomɛi.
NB: Tsɔɔlɔi baanyɛ amɛkɛ gbɛii nɛɛ ekomɛi afata he pii.
Sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ mumɔi loo wɔji agbɛii ahe Ehaa ayɔseɔ he ni mɔ ni akɛ gbɛi lɛ ewo lɛ lɛ jɛ.
Ehaa ayɔseɔ he ni mɔ ni akɛ gbɛi lɛ ewo lɛ lɛ jɛ.
Ehaa anaa tsakpaa ni ka mɔ ni hiɛ gbɛi lɛ kɛ jemawɔŋ lɛ teŋ.
Gbɛi lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ tsakpaa ko ka mɔ ni akɛ gbɛi lɛ ewo lɛ lɛ kɛ jemawɔŋ lɛ teŋ, ni anaa wɔŋ lɛ nifeemɔi komɛi yɛ mɔ ni akɛ gbɛi lɛ ewo lɛ lɛ he.
Eyeɔ ebuaa kɛfaa mɔ ni hiɛ egbɛi lɛ he, kɛkuraa kusumi lɛ.
Ewaa kɛbuaa gbɛiwoo kusum nifeemɔ lɛ naa ni elaajeee ejaakɛ akɛmiitsu nii be fɛɛ be.
Efaa jemawɔji agbɛi ni fata gbɛi henɔi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ gbɛiwoo mli lɛ ahe ni elaajeee.
Jemawɔji agbɛi fata gbɛi henɔi srɔtoi lɛ ahe yɛ Ghana fɛɛ.
Shii abɔ ni akɛ jemawɔji agbɛi woɔ abifabii lɛ nakai nɔŋŋ anaa amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi kɛ yibaamɔi lɛ.
Etsɔɔ fɔlɔi lɛ ahemɔkɛyeli ni etswaa adafi yɛ abifao lɛ hɔŋɔɔ gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔ kɛna abifao lɛ.
Yɛ gbɛi komɛi anɔ lɛ kɛji mɛi ni ebote gbala mli naaa bi afɔ lɛ, amɛyashwaa akɛ kɛji jemawɔŋ lɛ ha amɛ bi ni amɛfɔ lɛ, amɛkɛ abifao lɛ baawo jemawɔŋ lɛ.
Enɛ ha ni akɛ abifabii komɛi woɔ jemawɔji yɛ akutsei amli lɛ.
Nikasemɔ mli Nitsumɔ
Mɛni ji jemawɔŋ.
Tsɔɔmɔ sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ jemawɔji agbɛi etɛ he.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji jemawɔji agbɛi.
Ŋmalamɔ jemawɔji agbɛii enumɔ yɛ okutso lɛ mli koni nyɛsusu amɛjɛɛhei ahe.
Gbalamɔ sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ jemawɔji agbɛi etɛ mli kɛ bo diɛŋtsɛ owiemɔkulibii
Ŋɛlɛ 3 Nitsumɔ: Susumɔ ni mli kwɔ kɛnitsumɔ
Ani oheɔ wiemɔmuu nɛɛ owoɔ mli kɛyashiɔ nɛgbɛ: ‘akutsei aka he amɛkɛ jemawɔji agbɛi awo abifabii ni afɔfɔi’.
Nyɛsusua oblayei kɛ hii ahetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoi ni haa anaa mɔ ko akɛ onukpa he (mɔ floŋŋ onukpatsɔmɔ gbɛjianɔtoo: dani, beiaŋ, kɛ no sɛɛ).
Okɛ hetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoi ni akɛkpeɔ mɛi floŋŋ kɛbaa amɛnukpatsɔmɔ mli yɛ akutsei srɔtoi amli yɛ Ghana lɛ atotoi he.
YITSO: Kpokuafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ hebejemɔ: Kpokuafoiaŋ: Enɛ ji beiaŋ ni gbekɛ nuu loo yoo hiɛsalɛ tsɔɔ akɛ ebaanyɛ ewo loo eŋɔ hɔ ni efɔ bi.
Eji gbɛjianɔtoi ni baa kɛjɛ afii 10-14 yɛ yei amli ni ebaa kɛjɛ afii 12-16 yɛ hii amli.
(Bei komɛi lɛ ebaa mra loo esɛɛ tsɛɔ yɛ mɛi komɛi amli) Gbɔmɔtsoŋ tsakemɔi yɛ yei amli
Shitsaa (tswɛitsaa yɛ shi) kɛ ŋaashitsaa (tswɛi tsaa yɛ ŋaashi)
Tsakemɔi yɛ gbɔmɔtsoŋ Gbɔmɔtsoŋ tsakemɔi yɛ gbekɛbii hii amli
Hamɔ kɛ dɔlɔ feɔ agbo
Tswɛi tsaa yɛ shi kɛ ŋaashi
Enaa kpɔiaŋ tuamɔ kɛ hewalɛ
Gbee mli gbeleɔ
Tsɛŋ kɛ tsweitsaa yɛ hiɛ
Enaa kwɔlɛ Gbɛjianɔtoi ni akɛkpeɔ mɛi floŋŋ kɛbaa amɛnukpatsɔmɔ mli: Enɛmɛi ji hetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoi ni afeɔ ahaa mɛi floŋŋ yɛ amɛwalashihilɛ mli.
Kɛjɛ gbekɛbiashi kɛyaboteɔ amɛnukpatsɔmɔ mli yɛ gbɛiwoo kusum sɛɛ.
Eji gbɛjianɔtoo ni hii kɛ yei fɛɛ tsɔmɔɔ mli kɛtsɔɔ he ni amɛshɛ yɛ jeŋshihilɛ mli, kɛtsakeɔ amɛ kɛboteɔ onukpayeli mli.
Eji kadimɔ ni jieɔ he fɛo kpo, ehaa ofeɔ krɔŋkrɔŋ ni anaa ogbojee.
Gamɛi tsɛɔ bei nɛɛ akɛ hetsuumɔ bei ni kusumii lɛ ekomɛi ji otseŋtse, dɛŋtuwoo loo otofo.
Hetsuumɔ Dani abaabote gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli Gbaa nɔ ni sa feemɔ Gbaa nɔ ni sa dani aje nifeemɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ shishi.
Akadi tsakemɔi ni eba yɛ mɔ floŋŋ lɛ mli.
Tsi he kɛjɛ mɛi bibii ahe koni atsɔɔ lɛ nɔ ni sa feemɔ
Okɛ onukpai ni yɔɔ akutso lɛ mli ana sanegbaa loo mɛi ni baatsu kusum lɛ kɛ jemawɔŋ lɛ hu.
Yɛ kusumtsumɔ lɛ mli
Hejuu yɛ ŋshɔ loo faa mli
Shia sɛi nɔtamɔ loo yɛ tɛ diŋ nɔ
Kusumi nibii kɛ hesaamɔ
Kpojee yɛ maŋjara nɔ
Ŋkpaiyeli Kusumfeemɔ lɛ sɛɛ
Miishɛɛnamɔ Mɔ floŋŋ kpeemɔ he sɛɛnamɔi
Ehaa anaa akɛ oje gbekɛbii ateŋ kɛyabote onukpai amli
Ehaa ankroankro nilee loo jwɛŋmɔ ni he hiaa kɛha gbɛhe hee mli ni eyabote lɛ kuramɔ.
Ehaa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ naa leɔ akɛ nakai mɔ floŋŋ lɛ eshɛ nuu loo yoo ni baanyɛ ebote gbalashihilɛ mli.
Ehaa shihilɛ krɔŋŋ yɛ akutso lɛ mli
Ekɛ nyam bahaa mɔ floŋŋ lɛ kɛ eweku fɛɛ
Eyeɔ ebuaa akutso lɛ
Enaa shihilɛ mli hesalɛi Nikasemɔ mli nitsumɔ
Mɛni ji kpokoafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ kusum lɛ?
Ha sɛɛnamɔi etɛ ni kpokoafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ lɛ kɛbaa.
Gbalamɔ mli koni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni sɛɛnamɔi enyɔ ni otsɛ lɛ he hiaa lɛ
Ajara hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli etɛ, tsɛ fɛɛ koni otsɔɔ nɔ ni yaa nɔ yɛ fɛɛ eko mli.
Okɛ nilee ni ona lɛ akwɛ mfoniri lɛ koni okɛ mɔ ni ta omasɛi lɛ agba nɔ ni ona lɛ he sane
Gbalamɔ hetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli
Gbalamɔ nifeemɔi etɛ ni afeɔ dani aboteɔ hetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli.
Gbalamɔ nifeemɔi etɛ ni anaa yɛ mɔ hetsuumɔ kusumfeemɔ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ ni afeɔ kɛji agbe hetsuumɔ kusum oti lɛ diɛŋtsɛ naa.
Kwɛmɔ nifeemɔi ahe sɛɛnamɔ yɛ wɔŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ bei nɛɛ amli yɛ wɔkutso nɛɛ mli.
YITSO: Kpokuafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ he susumɔ Susumɔ kpokuafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ he Otii ni je amɛhe
Akɛ kusum nibii tsuɔ nii
Afeɔ yɛ gbii srɔtoi komɛi anɔ
Afeɔ be ni mɔ lɛ ji mɔ floŋŋ
Akɛ mɛi lɛ toɔ yɛ tsui srɔtoi komɛi amli
Afeɔ yitsɔi srɔtoi ahaa mɛi nɛɛ
NB: Nɔkwɛlɔi kɛ nibii ni je amɛhe lɛ eko afata he kɛji pii yɛ.
Srɔtoi ni yɔɔ nifeemɔi nɛɛ amli.
Srɔtoi yɛ nibii ni akɛtsuɔ kusumii nɛɛ amli
Srɔtoi yɛ gbii ni akɛtsuɔ kusumii nɛɛ amli
Srɔtoi yɛ niyenii ni ahaa amɛ lɛ mli
Afeɔ mumɔŋ kɛ heloŋ hebuamɔi.
Nɔkwɛlɔ baanyɛ eha kaselɔi aya intanɛti lɛ nɔ kɛyatao bɔ ni afeɔ hetsuumɔ kusumii nɛɛ.
Nikasemɔ mli nitsumɔ
Kwɛmɔ Kpokuafoaiŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ he vidio yɛ wiemɔi krokomɛi amli (Dangme, Ashante, Mfantse, Nzema, kn.kn)
Kaselɔi asusu nɔ ni amɛna yɛ vidio nɛɛ mli lɛ ahe ni amɛkɛto amɛnɔ lɛ he koni amɛtsɔɔ nɔ ni yeɔ he gbɔ kɛ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ mli hu.
Pɛimɔ tsakpaa ni yɔɔ omaŋ kɛ maŋ kroko ni kɔɔ kpokuafoaiŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ kusum tsumɔ lɛ mli.
Ŋɛlɛ 4 Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ:
Kɛji ona hegbɛ koni otsake kpokuafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kusum nifeemɔi komɛi yɛ owiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ, te emli nɔ ni obaatsake hu, ni mɛni hewɔ?
GBƐJIANƆTOO Oti: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ mankuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo Oti mlijaa: Kusum nifeemɔ Nikasemɔ mli gbɛkpamɔ:Akwɛ subaŋ kpakpai kɛ sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ kusumii nifeemɔi ahe.
Content Standard: Ha nilee kɛ bɔ ni onuɔ kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ shishi ohaa.
Nikasemɔ oti: Atao kusumnaa nɔyeli kuramɔi srɔtoi lɛ (Papa, wekuyitso, weku tɔkpaa yitso) YITSO: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo (Shia) Kusumnaa Gbɛjianɔtoo Eji nɔyeli ko ni shikwɛɛbii kɛ amɛnilee sharaa, kɛkuraa, kɛtsɔɔ he kɛ mliwoo gbɛjianɔtoo nɔ.
Nɔyeli shikamɔ yɛ wekui amli Weku Tɔkpaa Hiɛnyiɛlɔ: Weku-tɔkpaa ji mɛi komɛi ni tsakpaa ko ka amɛteŋ loo ni yɔɔ gbalashihilɛ mli ni amɛbua amɛhe naa akɛ ku kome.
Eji wekui ni hiɛ tsakpaa kome, amɛjɛ blematsɛ kome mli, ni amɛhiɛ hiɛnyiɛlɔ kome ni amɛkɛ woo haa.
Efɔɔ kaa akɛ amɛhiɛ subaŋ kome, yiŋtoo kome kɛ gbɛkpamɔ kome ni buaa amɛnaa.
Mɔ ni mɛi nɛɛ kɔɔ akɛ enyiɛ amɛhiɛ lɛ batsɔɔ nɛkɛ tɔkpaa nɛɛ hiɛnyiɛlɔ ji yitso lɛ.
Yɛ kusumii komɛi amli lɛ wekuyitsei lɛ kpeɔ ni amɛwoɔ amɛteŋ mɔ kome ni lɛ etsɔɔ tɔkpaa lɛ yitso.
Weku yitsei: Mɛi nɛɛ ji mɛi ni wekumɛi abii ehala akɛ amɛweku hiɛnyiɛlɔ ni anaa amɛ akɛ weku yitsei.
Tsɛ Ataa: Tsɛ Ataa ji weku kɔklɔɔ lɛ yitso ni lɛ, eŋa kɛ ebii yɔɔ mli lɛ.
Amɛkwɛɔ amɛweku kɔklɔɔ lɛ mli bii ahiamɔ nibii fɛɛ.
Hiɛnyiɛlɔi srɔtoi lɛ kɛ amɛnɔkwɔlɔmɔ nɛ:
Tsɛ Ataa Gbɛnaa hiɛlɔi lɛ anitsumɔ kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi: Weku Tɔkpaa hiɛnyiɛlɔ:
Lɛ ji Tɔkpaa lɛ kojolɔnukpa
Lɛ ebaa Tɔkpaa lɛ blema saji ayi
Lɛ ekuraa Tɔkpaa lɛ kusumi fɛɛ.
Eji egbɛnaa akɛ ebaa tɔkpaa lɛ shikpɔji fɛɛ ayi.
Ebɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ atsɔse yinɔbii ni baa lɛ koni amɛnu tɔkpaa lɛ shishi ni amɛbaa gboshi nibii lɛ ayi.
Efaa weku lɛ he kɛjɛɔ haomɔi amli
Ekwɛɔ ni toijɔlɛ ahi weku lɛ mli Tsɛ Ataa:
Ewoɔ mla ni ekwɛɔ akɛ aye nɔ
Ehaa ewebii naa he afabaŋfoo
Ehaa amɛnaa shifimɔ kɛ hewalɛ yɛ amɛmli
Lɛ ji klɛŋklɛŋ tsɔselɔ
Ehaa ewebii lɛ anitaomɔ nibii
Ewebii lɛ naa lɛ akɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kpakpa NB: Tsɔɔlɔ aha kaselɔi ale akɛ nyɛmɛi hu amɛgbɛnaa nitsumɔ yɛ weku lɛ mli..
Tsɔɔmɔ kusumnaa nɔyeli shikamɔ yɛ shia mli.
Nyɛsusua wekutɔkpaa hiɛnyiɛlɔ gbɛnaa nitsumɔi etɛ he.
Nyɛpɛia Tsɛ Ataa gbɛnaa nitsumɔi etɛ lɛ amli.
Kusumnaa nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ jeɔ shishi yɛ wekukɔklɔɔ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔyeli ŋɛlɛi srɔtoi lɛ ni okase he nii lɛ.
Nyɛsusua kusumnaa nɔyelɔi ni yɔɔ shia lɛ ahe.
Too nɔyelɔi nɛɛ anaa kɛjɛ nɔkwɔlɛ mli.
Ŋɛlɛ 3: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Kwɛmɔ kusumnaa nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli koni osusu sɛɛnamɔi ni maŋbii kɛ shiabii naa yɛ he.
Nikasemɔ oti: Kwɛmɔ kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo maŋtsɛmɛi (maŋtsɛwalɔi, maŋtsɛ wolɔi, knkn) YITSO: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo Gbɛnaahiɛlɔi anɔkwɔlɔmɔ yɛ maŋtsɛyeli gbɛjianɔtoi amli Eji tso ni tsɔɔ gbɛnaahiɛlɔi anɔkwɔlɔmɔ.
Etsɔɔ nɔkwɔlɛ shishi ni mɔ ko yɔɔ kɛ nɔkwɔlɛi ni yɔɔ lɛ hu eshishi, ni amɛnaa amɛhewalɛ kɛjɛɔ edɛŋ lɛ anaatoo.
Bɔ ni eyɔɔ lɛ nɛ:
Nɛkɛ ji bɔ ni naatoo lɛ yaa lɛ ehaa Jɛ yitsei nɛɛ asɛɛ lɛ, ayɛ gbɛnaa hiɛlɔi krokomɛi ni fataa he kɛyeɔ amɛbuaa maŋtsɛ lɛ kɛkuraa maji lɛ yɛ ŋɛlɛi fɛɛ anɔ.
Gbɛnaahiɛlɔi lɛ ekomɛi nɛ:
Maŋnyɛ Maji lɛ fɛɛ woɔ maŋnyɛ kɛyeɔ buaa maŋkuramɔ lɛ, titri lɛ, yei agbɛjianɔtoo.
Maŋkralo Awoɔ maŋkralo ni ekwɛɔ maŋtsɛ sɛɛ kɛji ebɛ.
Jaasetsɛ Ga maji pii woɔ ni mɛi komɛi hu woo jasetsɛ.
Akwashɔŋtsɛ Awoɔ gbɛnaa nɛɛ yɛ ŋɛlɛi fɛɛ anɔ kɛyeɔ buaa maŋkuramɔ lɛ.
Shipi Awoɔ lumɔ nɛɛ ni eyeɔ ebuaa akwashɔŋtsɛ kɛtsuɔ nii
Wulɔmɔ Gbɛnaa nɛɛ hiɛ ehe.
Yɛ mumɔŋ gbɛfaŋ lɛ, lɛ ji hiɛnyiɛlɔ.
Sɛitsɛ Lumɔ nɛɛ ji mɔ ni kuraa maŋtsɛ sɛi lɛ ni etsuɔ he kusum.
Sɛinyɛ Lumɔ ni yeɔ buaa sɛitsɛ kɛtsuɔ sɛi kɛ etsu lɛ mli kuramɔ he nii.
Atofotsɛ Awoɔ lumɔ nɛɛ ni etoɔ maŋtsɛ wulamɔ nibii fɛɛ, hei pii lɛ fotro hu.
Wolɛiatsɛ Atoɔ lumɔ nɛɛ kɛji ŋshɔnaa maŋ ni ayaawo yɛ mli.
Okwaafoiatsɛ Atoɔ lumɔ nɛɛ kɛji okwaafoi ji maŋbii lɛ.
Asafoiatsɛ Lumɔ nɛɛ hɔ Shipi shishi ni ekɛ lɛ tsuɔ nii
Asafoianyɛ Lumɔ nɛɛ hɔ shipi shishi ni ekɛ lɛ tsuɔ nii
Wekuyitso Wekui ni yɔɔ maŋ lɛ mli lɛ fɛɛ yɛ amɛweku yitsei ni wekui lɛ diɛŋtsɛ woɔ.
Lumɛi ni ato amɛnaa nɛɛ fɛɛ yɛ gbɛnaa nitsumɔi ni amɛtsuɔ amɛhaa amɛmaŋtsɛmɛi lɛ kɛyeɔ buaa amɛmaji, kpokpaa kɛ Jaku lɛ kuramɔ, Enɛ hewɔ lɛ, maŋtsɛ fɛɛ maŋtsɛ naa nɛkɛ lumɛi nɛɛ eko efataa esɛi lɛ he kɛyeɔ buaa emaŋtsɛyeli lɛ.
Maŋtsɛ wulu kɛ eshishi bii Yɛ Gamɛi lɛ amli lɛ maŋtsɛ ni da fe fɛɛ ji Maŋtsɛ Wulu (King).
Lɛ ji tsɛ yɛ Ga maji lɛ nɔ
Ekwɛɔ Ga maji lɛ nɔ
Ekwɛɔ ni ayeɔ afi ni atsuɔ kusumi
Lɛ ji shi tsɛ
Ekɛ nɔyaa bahaa emaŋ lɛ
Lɛ ji kojolɔnukpa yɛ Ga Jaku kɛ emajiatsɛmɛi lɛ anɔ.
Etsuɔ sɛi lɛ kusumi
Ewoɔ mla Jɛ lɛ esɛɛ dani wɔnaa Maŋtsɛnukpa (Paramount chief) Amɛji ehewɔŋ tsei ni yeɔ buaa lɛ kɛkuraa Ga Jaku lɛ (Ga State).
Ekwɛɔ ekpokpa lɛ nɔ
Lɛ ji kojolɔnukpa yɛ maji ni yɔɔ eshishi lɛ anɔ
Amɛkɛ amɛshishi bii lɛ ahaomɔi yahaa lɛ ni amɛjwɛŋɔ he kɛtaoɔ tsabaa yɛ amɛhaomɔi lɛ ahe.
Kpokpa agboi anɔ maŋtsɛmɛi ni ji Maŋtsɛnukpa lɛ hu yɛ kpokpa bibii anɔ Maŋtsɛmɛi (Divisional Chiefs) yɛ amɛshishi ni hiɛ maji babaoo Kpokpa Bibii anɔ Maŋtsɛmɛi (Divisional Chiefs)
Lɛ ji shi tsɛ yɛ ekpokpa lɛ nɔ
Lɛ ji kojolɔnukpa yɛ emaji ni yɔɔ eshishi lɛ anɔ
Amɛkɛ amɛmaji lɛ ahaomɔi yahaa Kpokpa bibioo ni ehɔ eshishi lɛ nɔ maŋtsɛ lɛ.
NB: Srɔtoi yɛ maŋtsɛmɛi ahalamɔ, amɛwomɔ kɛ amɛkpojiemɔ mli yɛ maji srɔtoi anɔ.
Tsɔɔlɔi akwɛ ni amɛyi gbɛ ni kɔɔ he ni amɛyɔɔ lɛ kusum lɛ nɔ.
Tsɔɔlɔi baanyɛ amɛkɛ mfoniri ni fata he lɛ awo nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ obɔ NB: Tsɔɔlɔi atao mfonirii ni sa afata he kɛtsɔɔ nii lɛ.
Source: Art+Feminism (2022) Nikasemɔ nitsumɔ Kadaagbamɔ Mɛi komɛi susuɔ akɛ Maŋtsɛmɛi amaŋkuramɔ bei eho ni ehe ehiaaa dɔŋŋ.
Mɛni ji osusumɔ yɛ nɛkɛ jwɛŋmɔ tsɔɔmɔ nɛɛ he.
Ani okɛ amɛ kpaa gbee loo mɛni oyɔɔ kɛɛmɔ?
Namɔ ji maŋtsɛ?
Namɛi ji maŋtsɛ wolɔi?
Namɛi ji maŋtsɛmɛi bibii?
Te awoɔ maŋtsɛ tɛŋŋ yɛ kpokpa lɛ nɔ?
Feemɔ nɔyeli tso ni maŋtsɛmɛi kɛtsuɔ nii yɛ nyɛmaŋ lɛ mli.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 Asɛsisimɛnti: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Yɛ osusumɔ mli lɛ, ani ehe hiaa akɛ aya nɔ akɛ maŋtsɛmɛi amaŋkuramɔ atsu nii NB: Tsɔɔlɔi akwɛ akɛ kaselɔ atsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni amɛkɛɔ nɔ ni amɛkɛɔ lɛ.
Oti: Jeŋnilee ni kɔɔ Ghana wiemɔ he kasemɔ Oti mlijaa: Akrɔkuaŋ saji/ŋaaŋ saji Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ:
Kaselɔ kɛ nilee kɛ shishinumɔ ni ana atsu ŋkpaiyeli kɛ yarawoo lala kusumi lɛ.
Kaselɔ kɛ nilee kɛ shishinumɔ ni ena yɛ otui ni yɔɔ Afoata he ajie agugui amli yɛ sane ko he.
Atsɔɔ susumɔi kɛ shishinumɔ ni ana yɛ ŋkpaiyeli kɛ yarawoo lala he.
Atsɔɔ shishinumɔ yɛ Afoata he.
Nikasemɔ oti: Akwɛ otii ni yɔɔ ŋkpaiyeli mli (Jaomɔ, shɛɛwiemɔi kɛ naamuu) YITSO: Ŋkpaiyeli Ŋkpaiyeli Eji nifeemɔ ko ni akɛ daa, akpɛtɛshi, weŋ, ŋmadaa, nu, tɛŋdaa loo nɔ ko ni hiɛ nu su, ni akɛshwieɔ shi ahaa mumɔŋ bii (sisai, jemawɔŋ, wɔji loo mumɔŋ bii) koni amɛtsu nɔ ko pɔtɛɛ ko amɛha.
Akɛ daa tamɔ shinapu, weŋ, akpɛtɛshi, ŋmadaa, kɛ ekrokomɛi lɛ shwieɔ shikpɔŋ ni ajaoɔ kɛwoɔ mumɔi lɛ atoii anɔ ni afaa amɛ koni amɛfee nɔ ko pɔtɛɛ amɛha yɛ nɔ hewɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai lɛ he.
Yɛ hei komɛi lɛ, akɛ yibii tamɔ abele loo omɔ woɔ nu lɛ mli kɛyeɔ ŋkpai lɛ.
Ŋkpaiyeli yeɔ he eko jogbaŋŋ yɛ Afrika bii ashihilɛ mli.
Kɛtsɔ ŋkpaiyeli lɛ nɔ lɛ, akɛtsɛɔ blema tsɛmɛi kɛ mumɔi lɛ atoi sɛɛ ni akɛfɔɔ amɛ nine kɛbaa nifeemɔi ashishi hu.
Ŋkpaiyeli mli tso
Agboshimɔ: Aboɔ agoo kɛshiɔ agbo kɛbiɔ gbɛ yɛ gbi ni afeɔ nii lɛ mli hewalɛ koni tsɛmɛi kɛ mumɔi lɛ ahɛle shi yɛ nifeemɔ ni baa lɛ he ni amɛye amɛbua.
Jaomɔ: Atsɛɔ Tsɛ Ofe lɛ klɛŋklɛŋ, ŋwɛi kɛ shikpɔŋ, jemawɔji, sisai, kɛ mumɔi krokomɛi fɛɛ ni ole koni amɛbaye amɛbua nifeemɔ lɛ.
Akwɛɔ amɛtsiimɔ kɛ hewalɛ kɛtoɔ naa ni atsɛɔ
Amaniɛbɔɔ: Mɔ ni yeɔ ŋkpai lɛ bɔɔ mumɔi lɛ amaniɛ kɛtsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ yaa nɔ lɛ.
Ebiɔ koni amɛkɛ amɛhewalɛ abapia nifeemɔ lɛ kɛyashi naagbee.
Naamuu: Yɛ nɛkɛ ŋɛlɛ nɛɛ nɔ lɛ ebiɔ jɔɔmɔ ehaa mɛi ni yɔɔ jɛmɛ lɛ, kɛ mɛi ni hiɛ jwɛŋmɔ kpakpa yɛ adesai ahe lɛ, ni elomɔɔ mɛi gbohii ni ka amɛhewɔŋ kulɛ nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni afeɔ lɛ afite lɛ koni ajie amɛ kɛjɛ wɔteiaŋ.
NB: Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ baanyɛ etao mfonirii krokomɛi yɛ ŋkpaiyeli he efata mfonirii lɛ ahe kɛye abua kaselɔi koni amɛnu nikasemɔ lɛ shishi jogbaŋŋ.
Eka gbɛ aʋkɛnɔkwɛlɔ lɛ baanyɛ ekɛ eko agbe Ŋkpaiyeli gbɛjianɔtooɛ he.
Kɛji okɛ eko gbe yi lɛ gbalamɔ mli otsɔɔ kaselɔi lɛ.
Source: Nana Osei (2014) immigrantslenz.tumblr.com Ŋkpaiyeli gbɛjianɔtoo: NB: Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ akwɛ akɛ ebaana video yɛ ŋkpaiyeli he lo, koni ejie etsɔɔ kaselɔi yɛ amɛnikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ kɛ amɛ agba he sane koni amɛkadi mlijaramɔ otii ni yɔɔ mli Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ akpa nibii ni baa nɛɛ agbɛ:
Akɛ nibii ni yeɔ ŋkpai lɛ.
Mɔ ni yeɔ ŋkpai lɛ etsi emama lɛ kɛba shi.
Mɔ ni yeɔ ŋkpai lɛ ejie etokota.
Ŋkpaiyelɔ lɛ kɛ ehiɛ tsɔɔ he ko.
Ekɛ jaomɔ ni je shishi, ni ekɛ shɛɛwiemɔ ni tsa nɔ ni ekɛ wiemɔkulibiii komɛi mu naa.
NB: Nɔkwɛlɔ akadi akɛ yɛ kui komɛi amli lɛ yei yeee ŋkpai bɛja efi ni nuu bɛ (shi wɔyei nyɛɔ yeɔ) Yɛ nɛkɛ gbɛ nɔ lɛ agbala ‘yoo yee ŋkpai’wiemɔ lɛ mli atsɔɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Nɔkwɛlɔi baanyɛ aya intanɛti lɛ nɔ kɛyakwɛ ŋkpaiyeli video lɛ.
Akɛ woo haa Nyɔŋmɔ.
Akɛ woo haa jemawɔji kɛ tsɛmɛi asisai lɛ.
Ehaa maŋbii ni yɔɔ jɛmɛ lɛ naa amɛshishi fa.
Eji nifeemɔ ni haa anaa naatselɛ.
Etsɔɔ feemɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ nɔ ko ehaa kusumii kɛ blema saji.
Etsɔɔ bulɛ ni okɛhaa wekumɛi kɛ nanemɛi ni etsɔ amɛsɛɛ lɛ.
Ehaa mɛi kɛ mɛi shara jogbaŋŋ.
Nibii ni akɛyeɔ ŋkpai: Srɔtoi yɛ nibii ni kui srɔtoi kɛyeɔ ŋkpai mli ni ekomɛi nɛ: tsene, nu, weŋ, omɔ, tɛŋdaa, akpɛtɛshi, glase, kɔɔpoo, k.n.k.n.
Nibii komɛi ni akɛyeɔ ŋkpai (Source: jumia.com.gh/general) Kaa oti Kaselɔi akwɛ bɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai ahaa kɛŋmala nibii nɛɛ agbɛi lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji ŋkpaiyeli.
Susumɔ ŋkpaiyeli mli otii enyɔ otsɔɔ.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 Asɛsimɛnti: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Nyɛkwɛa ŋkpaiyeli he sɛɛnamɔi.
Esa ni afo ŋkpaiyeli mli yɛ maŋ nifeemɔi amli.
Ani oheɔ mɛi ni kɛɔ nakai lɛ owoɔ mli?
Tsɔɔmɔ ojwɛŋmɔ koni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni oheɔ owoɔ mli loo eheee owooo mki.
Nikasemɔ Otii: Yarawoo lalai (Enitsumɔ kɛ sɛɛnamɔi).
YITSO: Yarawoo lalai (enitsumɔ kɛ sɛɛnamɔi).
Yarawoo lala Eji nyanyɔji ashishikpemɔ kɛ awerɛhoo lala ni akɛfoɔ mɛi ni egboi.’ Eji lala ni alaa lɛ blɛoo kɛtsɔɔ dɔlɛ kɛ awerɛhoo, ni alaa yɛ yara kɛ gbele sɔɔmɔ feemɔ nɔ.
Bei komɛi lɛ akɛ jieɔ mɔ ni egbo lɛ yi Yarawoo lalai ahesɛɛnamɔ
Akɛfoɔ mɔ ni egbo lɛ.
Etsɔɔ tsakpaa ni kaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ wala beiaŋ kɛ be ni egbo nɛɛ.
Akɛgbeleɔ sane ko ni he miihia jogbaŋŋ hiɛ.
Akɛhaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ yaawɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Akɛtsɔɔ ŋmɛɛmɔ ni ko kpakpa eŋmɛɛ wɔ he
Aatao atsɔɔ akɛ mɔ lɛ egbo moŋ shi hiɛnɔkamɔ ji akɛ eyɛ.
Yɛ maji komɛi amli lɛ anaa yarawoo lalai nɛɛ akɛ faafoo kusum kɛha mɔ ni egbo lɛ.
NB: Mfoniri nɛɛ miitsɔɔ mɔ ko ni miila yarawoo lala.
Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ akwɛ enɛ nɔ kɛgbala yarawoo lala nifeemɔ lɛ mli etsɔɔ.
Nɔkwɛlɔ atao mfonirii kɛ vidio krokomɛi yɛ yarawoo lalai ahe ni ekɛgbala nilee nɛɛ mli etsɔɔ..
be/-9IN1IfdVz0 Eekpa yarawoo lala: Source: Atenteben (2023) NB: Srɔtoi yɛ yarawoo lalai nɛɛ akpamɔ mli yɛ hei pii.
Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ atsɔɔ bɔ ni awoɔ yara loo akpaa lalai nɛɛ ahaa yɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli Kaa oti
Mɛni ji yarawoo lala?
Ha sɛɛnamɔi enumɔ ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ lalai ahe.
Gbalamɔ sɛɛnamɔi ni oha lɛ mli etɛ otsɔɔ.
Te alaa yarafeemɔ lalai tɛŋŋ yɛ okutso lɛ mli?
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni yarafeemɔ lala ji.
Yarafeemɔ lalai yeɔ he eko jogbaŋŋ yɛ gbonyo ko yarafeemɔ kusum mli.
Te alaa yarafeemɔ lalai yɛ omaŋ lɛ mli ahaa tɛŋŋ?
Ŋɛlɛ 3 Asɛsimɛnti: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Asaa akwɛ sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ lalai ahe.
Nikasemɔ oti: Nyɛsusua Afoata he otii lɛ ahe.
(Nk., shwɛlɔi, oti he ni awieɔ, naatoo, he ni sane lɛ ba yɛ, mɔ susumɔ/jwɛŋmɔ, henumɔ, k.n k.n) YITSO: Afoata mli ootui Afoata Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ akɛtsuɔ Nilee Saji ahe nii.
Etsɔɔ bɔ ni atsɔɔ wiemɔ nɔ kɛŋmaa nii loo sane ko.
Atsɛrɛɔ wiemɔi lɛ jogbaŋŋ bɔ ni awieɔ lɛ lɛ.
Eŋɔɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ henɔ kome su asaŋ edamɔɔɔ shi tiimɔ nɔ.
Afoata tsɔɔ adesatamɔ nɔ kɛtsɔɔ mɔ lɛ su kɛ enilee yɛ yitso ko he.
Shwɛlɔi: Amɛ ji mɛi ni niŋmalɔ lɛ taa sane woɔ amɛnaa kɛjieɔ eyiŋtoo lɛ kpo.
Saji ni jeɔ kpo lɛ damɔɔ tsakpaa ni yɔɔ shwɛlɔi ateŋ nɔ.
Shɛlɔi ahe hiaa yɛ adesa ni ataa lɛ he ni shwɛlɔi nɛɛ nɔŋŋ tsɔɔ bɔ ni sane lɛ naatoo yaa lɛ.
Shwɛlɔi otii ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli lɛ haa anaa bɔ ni sane lɛ naatoo yɔɔ.
Shwɛlɔi ahenɔi ji shwɛlɔ oti kɛ najiaŋwolɔ.
Shwɛlɔ oti lɛ ji mɔ ni anuɔ ehe jogbaŋŋ yɛ adesa lɛ mli.
Najiaŋwolɔ lɛ ji mɔ ni woɔ shwɛlɔ oti lɛ naneteŋ ni ehaa lɛ naagbai srɔtoi.
Shwɛlɔ kroko hu ji Tsuisalɔ, lɛ lɛ etsakee eyiŋtoo, nɔ ni ka ehiɛ lɛ no efeɔ.
Mɔ ko nyɛɛɛ akɔne eyiŋtoo yɛ sane ko he.
Shwɛlɔ kroko hu ji Oyiŋ-etsɛɛɛ- tsɔmɔ, shwɛlɔ nɛɛ nyɛɛ edamɔ yiŋtoo kome nɔ.
Edamɔɔ ŋaa fɛɛ ŋaa ni abaawo lɛ nɔ kɛtsakeɔ enifeemɔi kɛ ejwɛŋmɔ.
Oti he ni awieɔ: Enɛ ji nɔ nɔ ni adesa lɛ damɔɔ asaŋ no nɔŋŋ akɛkudɔɔ adesa lɛ nɔyaa.
Subaŋi ni shwɛlɔi lɛ jieɔ lɛ kpo lɛ haa ahaa oti ni awieɔ he lɛ, subaŋi lɛ tsɔɔ tsakpaa ni ka amɛteŋ.
Adesai komɛi nyɛɔ amɛjieɔ otii krokomɛi kpo kɛfataa oti ni awieɔ he lɛ he.
He ni sane lɛ ba yɛ: Enɛ ji adesa lɛ sɛɛtso.
No tsɔɔ maŋ loo akrowa loo shihilɛhe kɛ be nɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ ba nɔ.
Naatoo: Eji nifeemɔi ni tee nɔ yɛ adesa lɛ mli lɛ naatoo.
Esaa etsɔɔ wiemɔi ni je kpo lɛ anaatoo kɛ bɔ ni nifeemɔi srɔtoi lɛ eko nyiɛɔ ekroko sɛɛ yɛ adesa lɛ mli.
Naatoo lɛ tsuɔ naagbai srɔtoi lɛ ahe nii, awuŋayeli kɛ mataŋi srɔtoi.
Enyɛɔ efeɔ gbɛkpamɔ nɔ ko loo mɔ shwee nɔ ko loo mɛi lɛ anitaomɔ nii.
Bei komɛi lɛ nibii ahiɛ nyɛɔ tsakeɔ trukaa ni akpaaa gbɛ.
Mɔ ko susumɔ/Jwɛŋmɔ: Enɛ ji hiŋmɛi ni mɔ ko kɛkwɛɔ adesa lɛ mli nifeemɔi lɛ.
Ebaanyɛ efee mɔ ni jaje loo toibolɔ.
Henumɔ: Enɛ tsɔɔ musuŋtsɔlɛ loo nyamɔ ni niŋmalɔ lɛ taoɔ ko ni ehi kanelɔi lɛ amli.
Eji gbɛ nɔ ni niŋmalɔ lɛ jieɔ henumɔ ko kpo kɛtsɔɔ jwɛŋmɔ mli mfoniri nɔ loo nifeemɔi kɛ nibii krokomɛi tamɔ nikwɛmɔ nɔ.
Enyɛɔ efeɔ awerɛho sane, kunimyeli, nifeemɔi ni yɔɔ fɛo, hiɛnɔkamɔ loo oshra k.n k.n.
Afoata he nikasemɔ lɛ nɔdoomɔ: Shwɛlɔi, oti he ni awieɔ, naatoo, he ni sane lɛ ba yɛ, mɔ susumɔ/ jwɛŋmɔ, henumɔ.
Mɛɛ gbɛi anɔ shwɛlɔi ahe hia yɛ niŋmaa mli?
Mɛɛ gbɛi anɔ he ni nɔ lɛ ba yɛ he hia yɛ niŋmaa mli?
Yɛ osusumɔ mli lɛ, mɛni nibii haa naatoo lɛ jeɔŋma yɛ niŋmaa mli?
Nikasemɔ Otii: Afoata sane henyamɔ (yitso, oti he ni awieɔ, wiemɔ halamɔ, niŋmaa mli ŋaagbɛi, k.n.).
YITSO: Afoata sane henyamɔ Nyɛbotea Afoata otui lɛ amli ekoŋŋ.
Otui lɛ ji shwɛlɔi, oti, naatoo, he ni sane lɛ ba yɛ, mɔsusmɔ/jwɛŋmɔ kɛ henumɔ.
Kaselɔi atsɔɔ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ ashishi yɛ kui enyɔ enyɔ mli.
Afoata he nyamɔ: Afoata sane ko henyamɔ ji;
bɔ ni obaakane nii lɛ, kɛ shishi ni obaana yɛ oti lɛ he, ŋaagbɛ nɔ ni akɛ ŋma, bɔ ni akɛ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi kɛ ŋaagbɛi krokomɛi tsu nii.
Kɛji obaanya Afoata sane he lɛ, tsu nibii nɛɛ: GBƐJIANƆTOO NƆ LƐ SUSUMƆ KUDƆMƆ OTII 1 Mlinii (Nɔ ni) • Mɛni mlihalamɔ lɛ tsɔɔ?
Mɛni ji otii krɛdɛɛ ni yɔɔ mli.
2 Oti (Mɛni hewɔ) Mɛni ji niŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo krɛdɛɛ?
3 Mɔ susumɔ/Jwɛŋmɔ • Namɔ wiemɔ anuɔ lɛ?
Namɔ gbee anuɔ yɛ sane lɛ mli lɛ?
Ani niŋmalɔ lɛ aloo shwɛlɔi lɛ ateŋ mɔ ko?
4 He ni nɔ lɛ ba yɛ (Nɛgbɛ kɛ Mɛɛbe)
Mɛɛ toisɛɛtsɛmɔ wiemɔ tsɔɔ be kɛ he ni aŋma nii lɛ yɛ.
Taomɔ nɔkwɛmɔnii kpakpai kɛma nɔ mi.
Mɛɛ gbɛ nɔ ni he ni sane lɛ ba yɛ lɛ yeɔ bua oti lɛ.
5 Ŋaagbɛ ni akɛŋma (Te eba lɛ tɛŋŋ?)
Kwɛmɔ wiemɔi lɛ tsɛrɛmɔ kɛ hidɔɔ saji ni yɔɔ mli lɛ.
Mɛɛ wiemɔkulibii niŋmalɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii?
Kwɛmɔ gbɛnɔ ni niŋmalɔ lɛ kɛ niŋmaa mli okadii lɛ tsu nii yɛ, kɛ ehesalɛ.
Ani sanegbaa yɛ mli?
Mɛni jwɛŋmɔ mli mfoniri asha?
Mɛni hiɛdɔɔ loo henumɔ yɔɔ sane lɛ mli?
Yɛ mɛɛ gbɛ nɔ hiɛdɔɔ sane lɛ wo oti lɛ obɔ?
Afoata sane lɛ henyamɔ lɛ nɔdoomɔ: Mlinii (Nɔ ni) Mɔsusumɔ/Jwɛŋmɔ (Namɔ) Oti (Mɛni hewɔ) He ni nɔ lɛ ba yɛ (Nɛgbɛ kɛ Mɛɛ be)
Tsɔɔmɔ Afoata mli otui lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii ni asusuɔ he kɛji aanya Afoata sane he.
Susumɔ nibii etɛ ni atsi ta lɛ he kukubɔ.
Daamɔ nikasemɔ ni ona lɛ nɔ ko ni okane Afoata sane ko ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii ohai enumɔ.
Ŋmaa ko ni otsɔɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ anyaa Afoata sane lɛ mli.
Kaselɔ akane Afoats sane kuku ko, ko ni amɛnya he kɛtsɔ shwɛlɔi lɛ kɛ he nɔ lɛ ba yɛ lɛ nɔ.
Mɛi enyɔenyɔ lɛ atsɔɔ nɔ ko kɛtsɔ nɔ ni amɛkase lɛ nɔ.
Kanemɔ Afoata sane ko.
Taomɔ otui komɛi yɛ mli.
OTI: WIEMƆ KƐ ENITSUMƆ 46
OTI: KUSUM NIFEEMƆI KƐ MAŊKURAMƆ 99 Oti Mlijaa: Kusum Nifeemɔi 99 OTSI 15 101 Yitso Oti Ni Akwɛɔ: Afiyeli 101 OTSI 16 106
vii List of Tables Bɔ ni afeɔ mlijaa A kɛ B he sanebimɔi lɛ 41 Tsɔɔlɔi afee kaa sanebimɔi 50 kɛha mlijaa nɛɛ.
SANEGBAA MLI Oti: SANEGBAA Oti Mlijaa: Sanegbaa/Wiemɔ Yɛ Nɔ Pɔtɛɛ Ko He Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa atsu nii kɛgba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋ maji saji komɛi ahe.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie hesalɛ kpo kɛgba sane jogbaŋŋ yɛ saji pɔtɛɛ komɛi amli.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ bɔ ni akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ kɛwieɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe ni awie naagbai ahe jogbaŋŋ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ baatsa nɔ ni akase momo yɛ Afi 1, mlijaa 2 lɛ nɔ.
Kaselɔi kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa baatsu nii kɛwie ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe beiaŋ ni amɛkɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) ŋaagbɛ lɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ amɛsanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, kaselɔi baakase mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) ni amɛkɛtsu nii yɛ klasi lɛ mli kɛ amɛwalashihilɛi amli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ baawa kaselɔi ni amɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa agba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe kɛjie shishinumɔ pɔtɛɛ kpo yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hia kɛha bo jogbaŋŋ akɛ kaselɔ, jeee yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ kasemɔ pɛ hewɔ shi moŋ ekɛ tsakpaa ko baa nikasemɔi krokomɛi tamɔ Blɔfo wiemɔ lɛ kɛ wiemɔi krokomɛi lɛ amli.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ kɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi kɛ kaa he gbɛjianɔtoo kpakpa atsu nii kɛye ebua nikasemɔ lɛ.
Otsii kɛ nibii ni akase yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ ji:
Otsi 1: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa atsu nii yɛ daadaa sanegbaa mli.
Ajie mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) he ŋaagbɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli kpo.
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa yɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ agbaa ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe sane.
NITSƆƆMƆ ŊAAGBƐ LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ ni aksɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ kɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi babaoo ni akɛtsɔɔ nii yɛ Ghana wiemɔi amli lɛ tsuɔ nii.
Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) ji ŋaagbɛ ni akɛ jeŋ saji ni ale lɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ nikasemɔ mli kɛwaa ni kaselɔi ashishinumɔ kɛ nifeemɔ aya hiɛ.
Etamɔɔɔ nɔ ni atsɔɔ mɔ nii lɛ traa lɛ.
Eji ŋaagbɛi pɔtɛɛ komɛi tamɔ ankroankro nikasemɔ, mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ, kuu ni afutu mɛi kɛ klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ.
Nifeemɔ nɛɛ baanyɛ eha hesalɛ yɛ yiŋkaa, saji anaataomɔ
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa MliMlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli kɛ sanegbaa kɛ wiemɔ mli aya hiɛ pam.
Ekɛ hegbɛi tamɔ ekomefeemɔ kɛ hiɛnyiɛmɔ baa.
Kaselɔi hu kaseɔ bɔ ni amɛbaanyɛ amɛhara saji komɛi ni amɛpɛi mli.
Yɛ kaselɔi ni yɛ dromɔ nikenii hu mli lɛ, ahaa amɛ nitsumɔi lɛ ekomɛi kɛ fataa he ni amɛfee hiɛnyiɛmɔ nitsumɔ tamɔ tsɔɔlɔ ni amɛnyiɛ amɛkaselɔi ahiɛ kɛna shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ yɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ lɛ he.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi akadi kaselɔi ni yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ kɛ sanegbaa naagbaai lɛ ni amɛtsɔ ŋaagbɛ nɔ kɛjaje susumɔ gbohii lɛ.
KAA HE NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaagbɛ ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ ŋmɛɔ kaa ŋaagbɛi krokomɛi kɛha hesalɛnamɔ, shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ kɛ yiŋkaa gbɛ.
Naajiemɔ kpitiokpitiohamɔ lɛ haa kaselɔi anikasemɔ muu lɛ yaa hiɛ.
Kaa ŋaagbɛ kɛ ŋaagbɛi ni waa kɛkadiɔ kaselɔi ashishinumɔ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ kasemɔ he.
Ahaa ŋɛlɛ etɛ yiŋkaa he sanebimɔi (level 3 strategic reasoning) kɛtsɔ niŋmaa loo saji amli nikasemɔ kɛwkɛ kaselɔi lɛ asusumɔi kɛ amɛniiashishinumɔ mlikwɔlɛ yɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli (turn taking) kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa gbaa ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋ maji saji ahe sane lɛ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ aka kaselɔi lɛ beiaŋ ni nikasemɔ lɛ yaa nɔ lɛ nɔŋŋ kɛ nikasemɔ lɛ naagbee hu bɔ ni afee ni ena nibii babaoo kɛkɔ kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko amɔdɛŋbɔɔ, mii ni enaa yɛ kaa lɛ mli, kɛ nɔ ni jɛɔ mli kɛbaa kɛha amɛnɔyaa.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi aje gbɛ ato nikasemɔ lɛ he gbɛjianɔ jogbaŋŋ ni amɛkɛ Universal Design for learning (UDL) ŋaagbe lɛ atsu nii bɔ ni afee ni eŋmɛ nikasemɔ hiamɔ nibii srɔto lɛ agbɛ.
Nine aaanyɛ ashɛ enɛ nɔ kɛtsɔ
Nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi (nitsumɔi srɔtoi tamɔ sanegbaai, nibii ni anaa, nibii ni ataraa he, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he).
Atsɔɔ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ ni akɛtsɔɔ nii lɛ (niŋmaa, gbɛɛmɔ nibii, nibii ni anaa, kɛ mligbalamɔi ni ka shi).
Aŋmɛ gbɛi srɔtoi kɛha nifeemɔ kɛ gbɛtsɔɔmɔ (aŋmɛ kaselɔi agbɛ kɛha sanebimɔi ahetoo kɛtsɔ wiemɔ, niŋmaa, nitɛŋmɔ nɔ).
Kɛfata he lɛ, esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi lɛ adamɔ kaselɔi ahiamɔ nibii anɔ ni ekudɔ enitsɔɔmɔ lɛ tamɔ eto be srɔto eha amɛ, ekɛ yelikɛbuamɔ nibii atsu nii, etsake nitsumɔ lɛ hiɛ loo eha nanemɛi aye abua amɛhe kɛji efi.
Ŋaagbɛi ni haa kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko kɛ amɛhe woɔ nitsɔɔmɔ kɛ nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli OTSI 1 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa atsu nii yɛ daadaa sanegbaa mli Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsii 1 kɛ 2
Daamɔ nibii ni ja nɔ kɛtsɔɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ amli (tamɔ., naabufeemɔ, lilɛi henɔwomɔ kɛ egbɛhe)
Daamɔ nibii ni ja nɔ kɛtsɔɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi lɛ amli (tamɔ., gbekpaa shikamɔ, he ni afee yɛ, bɔ ni gbɛɛmɔ lɛ je gbo) Afii 2 Otsii 1 kɛ 2
Tsɔɔmɔ blawa henɔi yɛ Ga mli
Gbalamɔ blawa jebɔ lɛ mli yɛ Ga wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ mli.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: WIEM ƆKULIBII NI SA K ƐHA SANEGBAA
Wiemɔkubii hee amligbalamɔ: Rɛjista nɛɛ ji wiemɔi henɔi srɔtoi ni akɛtsuɔ nii ni damɔɔ wielɔi lɛ nɔ pɔtɛɛ ni awieɔ he lɛ nɔ.
Ekomɛi ji wiemɔkulibii srɔtoi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ nitsumɔhei srɔtoi amli lɛ.
Tamɔ, kɛji otee datrɛfonyo loo helatsamɔhe lɛ, wɔkɛ wiemɔkulibii komɛi kpeɔ ni wɔkɛtsuuu nii yɛ wɔdaadaa sanegbaai amli.
Wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ ekomɛi ji, tsofai, atridii, nɛɛsifonyo, helasaa, sɛtoskopu, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Yɛ nakai gbɛ nɔŋŋ nɔ lɛ, wɔbaanyɛ wɔna wiemɔkulibii pɔtɛɛ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ mlawoohe, adafitswaa, sukuu, kɛ nitsumɔhei krokomɛi hu amli.
Gbɛ srɔto nɔ ni akɛ wiemɔ, titri, wiemɔkulibii hee lɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ tsake baa bɔ ni gbɔmɔ flo ko kɛ nakai wiemɔkulibii lɛ nɔŋŋ tsuɔ nii yyɛ sanegbaa mli.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji, wiemɔkuli ‘mouse’ yɛ blɔfo mli ni akɛtsuɔ nii daadaa lɛ, eji wiemɔ kroko ko kwraa yɛ kɔmpiuta mli.
Eeenyɛ efee kwakwe ni anaa yɛ kpatashi loo tsɔne ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ kɔmpiuta mli.
Yɛ nɔ fɛɛ nɔ mli lɛ, wɔkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsuɔ nii yɛ nɔ pɔtɛɛ ni wɔwieɔ lɛ he.
Srɔto yɛ wiemɔ ni wɔkɛtsuɔ nii yɛ tipɛŋfoi asanegbaa mli lɛ kɛ gbɛnaa hiɛlɔi lɛ nɔ lɛ teŋ.
Rɛjistai henɔi lɛ ji: wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ agwaseiaŋ kɛ tipɛŋfoi sanegbaa mli.
Wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ agwaseiaŋ sanegbaa mli: Enɛ ji gbɛi anɔ ni akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Akɛ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulibii tsuɔ nii titri yɛ nitsumɔhei, sukuu nifeemɔi loo agwaseiaŋ nifeemɔi amli ni faŋfeemɔ, otii kɛ bulɛ ahe hia jogbaŋŋ.
Kɛji akɛ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulibii tsu nii lɛ, ehaa sanegbaa feɔ faŋŋ.
Gbɛi kpakpa nɔ ni akɛ agwaseiaŋ tsuɔ nii lɛ ɛie hesalɛ, nyɛmɔ kɛ hewalɛ ko kpo.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa MliMlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli Agwaseiaŋ Wiemɔkulibii Sui Ekɛ wiemɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ tsuɔ nii Agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulibii kɛ oblaŋ wiemɔi, akutso loo kuubuu awiemɔi loo wiemɔkulibii fafai tsuuu nii ejaakɛ amɛkɛ wiemɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ tsuɔ nii.
Ekɛ wiemɔkulibii hee ni ja tsuɔ nii Agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulibii kɛ wiemɔkulibii kuku ni sa nɔ he ni awieɔ lɛ tsuɔ nii Sanemuji haŋtsii amli Bei pii lɛ, anaa agwasieaŋ wiemɔkulibii yɛ sanemuji haŋtsii amli.
Eyɛ yiŋtoo pɔtɛɛ ko Agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulinii hiɛ oti pɔtɛɛ ko, edamɔɔɔ ankroankro susumɔi, henumɔi loo mlijiemɔ nɔ.
Ekɛ gbee ni ka shi tsuɔ nii Gbee ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulibii amli lɛ jieɔ hesalɛ, jwɛŋmɔmlikwɔlɛ, bulɛ kɛ heshibaa kpo.
Tipɛŋfoi Awiemɔkulibii Nɛkɛ wiemɔ henɔ nɛɛ lɛ, gbɛjianɔtoo ko bɛ he ni mɔ fɛɛ mɔ nyɛɔ ekɛtsuɔ nii ni anaa yɛ daadaa sanegbaai amli.
Bei pii lɛ yɛ nanemɛi, wekumɛi, intanɛti lɛ mɛi anaanyobɔɔ mli.
Tipɛŋfoi awiemɔkulibii sui komɛi Akɛ wiemɔkulibii kukuji, wiemɔkulibii ni yɛ srɔto eha kpokpaabii pɔtɛɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ tipɛŋfoi awiemɔkulibii mli.
Wiemɔkulibii hee lɛ ka shi Wiemɔ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ tipɛŋfoi aŋɛlɛ lɛ ka shi, tamɔ daadaa wiemɔkulibii hee tamɔ wiemɔkulibii ni afɔlɔ nɔ.
Sanemuu ganee Bei pii lɛ, anaa tipɛŋfoi awiemɔkulibii yɛ sanemuu ganeei amli, tamɔ sanemuji ni yeko emuu kɛ wiemɔkulibii kukuji ni akɛfɔlɔɔ sanegbaa mli Akɛ mɔdiɛŋtsɛ gbee tsuɔ nii Nɛkɛ wiemɔ henɔ nɛɛ jieɔ wielɔ lɛ henumɔ kɛ esu kpo.
Amɛ le wiemɔkulibii lɛ jogbaŋŋ Mɛi ni kɛ nɛkɛ tipɛŋfoi awiemɔkulibii lɛ tsuɔ nii lɛ nuɔ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ ashishi jogbaŋŋ.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli Nitsumɔ Nyɛtsɔa wiemɔkulibii komɛi ni nyɛsusuɔ akɛ akɛbaatsu nii yɛ hei nɛɛ amli;
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: datrɛfonyo kɛ helatsa ko sanegbaa mli: tsofa,
Tsɔnesaalɔ kɛ mɔ ni tsɔne efite lɛ sanegbaa mli
Mɛi ni kɛ niyenii baa yɛ niyelihe lɛ kɛ gbɔi lɛ asanegbaa mli: niyenii,
Akuntaabuu tsɔɔlɔ kɛ ekaselɔi yɛ nikasemɔ tsu mli: ajiekɛjɛmli, abɔhe,
Kaselɔi ni miikase nitɛŋ he nii kɛ etsɔɔlɔ, su, brɔshi,
Gbekɛnuu ni kɛ enaa miigba sane yɛ tɛlifonu nɔ: oshwie,
Nanemɛi enyɛ ni miigba bɔɔlu he sane: bɔɔlu, tswa, ntaŋ.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔi Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) (ankroankro kɛ kuu)
Kuu nitsumɔ: Kuuŋbii lɛ akɔ oti pɔtɛɛ ko ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii henɔi srɔtoi ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ha amɛ lɛ mli ni amɛka amɛyiŋ yɛ he.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ anyiɛ amɛ nɔ yɛ klasi lɛ mli bɔ ni afee ni kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko kɛ amɛhe awo nifeemɔ mli lɛ ni amɛye amɛbua amɛ yɛ bei ni amɛtaoɔ yelikɛbuamɔ mli.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Kaselɔi baakɔ oti pɔtɛɛ ko yɛ ni tsɔɔ nifeemɔ kpakpa (ejurɔfeemɔ, suɔmɔ, anɔkwayeli), jeŋshikpamɔ, tɛkinɔlɔji, tsɔji ahe nilee kɛ akuntaabuu ni blɔfo tsɛɔ lɛ (STEM), Gender Equality and Social Inclusion (GESI), hewalɛkpakpanamɔ, akutsoŋ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe ni amɛkɛwo shwɛmɔ mli.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ baaha sanebimɔi komɛi ni baakudɔ ni eye ebua sanegbaa lɛ.
Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ
Akwɛ ni abo shwɛmɔ lɛ toi ni ahara wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu nii yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ.
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii krokomɛi afata wiemɔkulibii ni aha yɛ nitsumɔ loo yitso lɛ eko fɛɛ eko lɛ he.
Ani wielɔi lɛ kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsu nii yɛ amɛsanegbaa lɛ mli fɛɛ?
Kaa Oti: DoK Ŋɛlɛ 2 – Kuu Nifeemɔi Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni afeɔ abifao kpojiemɔ yɛ okutso mli ahaa.
Ŋmalamɔ wiemɔkulibii ni okɛbaatsu nii yɛ onitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni wiemɔkulibii ni ohara lɛ he hiaa kɛha onitsɔɔmɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni wiemɔkulibii lɛ aaanyɛ atsake kɛji oŋmɛ gbɛ kɛha sanebimɔi.
Tsɔɔmɔ klasi lɛ.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli Note
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa MliMlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli OTSI 2 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi
Ajie hesalɛ yɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ‘turn taking’ kpo yɛ sanegbaa mli
Adamɔ wiemɔkulibii ni ja nɔ kɛgba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ akutso kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 3: Atsɔɔ otii pɔtɛɛ kɛjɛ sanegbaa krɛdɛɛ ko mli.
Nifeemɔ kpakpa (mɔbuu, ekpakpafeemɔ), sukuuyaa, gbekɛbii amɔmɔ, GESI, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Agbala gbee mli, ni akwɛ ehenɔi lɛ amli (nɔ ni yɛ ŋwɛi, nɔ ni yɛ shishi, nɔ ni miiba shi, nɔ ni miiya ŋwɛi kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) kɛ amɛnitsumɔi
Agba gbee nitsumɔi komɛi ahe sane.
(nɔ ni kɛ tsakemɔ baa wiemɔkulibii kɛ sanemuu teŋ).
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ I: MƐƐ YƐ SANEGBAA MLI Eji beiaŋ ni sanegbalɔi lɛ tsakeɔ mɔ ni wiemɔ lɛ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli, ni haa gbɛjianɔtoo kɛ shishinumɔ ko baa sanegbaa lɛ he.
Wielɔi lɛ ekome wieɔ ni mɛi ni eshwɛ lɛ feɔ dioo.
Enɛ haa sanegbaa kɛ jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ yɛ sanegbalɔi lɛ ateŋ lɛ yaa nɔ yɔɔ ni eyeɔ emuu hu.
Ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) he: Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (Turn taking) he nifeemɔ lɛ yɛ srɔto yɛ kusumii kɛ nifeemɔi amli.
Nɔ hewɔ ji akɛ, akutsoŋ nifeemɔi ni kwɛɔ be, bɔ ni wiemɔ lɛ yaa lɛ yɛ lɛ kudɔɔ sanegbaa lɛ.
Bei komɛi lɛ, yɛ sanegbaa mli lɛ, sanegbalɔi lɛ fɔlɔɔ amɛnaa ni ekrokomɛi hu tsɔɔ bulɛ kɛ gbɛi kpakpai anɔ.
Wiemɔkulibii komɛi ni akɛtsuɔ nii kɛji ootao ofo mɔ ko wiemɔ mli lɛ ji ‘miiba fioo’ , ‘manyɛ mawie nɔ ko’ kɛ sanebimɔi ni akɛtaoɔ niashishinumɔ.
Yɛ nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, esa akɛ eyɔse ni okj woo aha bei ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ lɛ.
Wiemɔkulibii komɛi tamɔ ‘mɛni ji osusumɔ?
‘mitsɔɔ mijwɛŋmɔ, obaanyɛ owie kɛ okaditoo nibii komɛi tamɔ yitso hosomɔ tsɔɔ be mli ni eshɛ sanegbalɔ ko nɔ ni ebaawie.
Esa akɛ ona tsui ni oye ohe nɔ be mli ni omɛɔ lɛ.
Kɛha anyɛ aha mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli aya hiɛ lɛ, esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ anyiɛ kaselɔi ahiɛ kɛyoo beiaŋ ni eshɛ mɔ kroko nɔ ni eewie ni amɛkafo mli.
Tsɔɔlɔi baanyɛ amɛtsɔɔ okaditoo loo mutɔɔ wiemɔi kɛtsɔɔ beiaŋ ni eshɛ mɔ ko nɔ ni ewie.
Esa akɛ atsɔse kaselɔi ni amɛŋmɛ amɛtsui shi ni amɛye amɛhe nɔ beiaŋ ni amɛmɛɔ ni eshɛ amɛnɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔlɔ baanyɛ ekɛ betsɔɔlɔ hu akai kaselɔi lɛ kɛji mɔ ko ewie eta ni eshɛ mɔ kroko nɔ ni ebaawie.
Nɔ hewɔ ni amɛɔ yɛ sanegbaa mli: Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli haa sanegbalɔi lɛ naa be ni sa kɛkudɔɔ sanegbaa lɛ.
Ehaa sanegbalɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko naa be kɛtsɔɔ ejwɛŋmɔ.
Ehaa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ kɛ ehe woɔ sanegbaa lɛ mli.
Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli hu kɛ tsakpaa kpakpa baa ni ehaa naanyobɔɔ ni yɔɔ sanegbalɔi lɛ ateŋ lɛ yaa hiɛ kɛji aŋmɛ kaselɔi agbɛ ni amɛkɛ amɛhe shara yɛ bulɛ gbɛi anɔ.
Mɛɛ yɛ samegbaa mli haa sanegbalɔi lɛ tsɔɔ nɔ pɔtɛɛ ni amɛyɛ he miishɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli.
Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli haa sanegbaa naa hewalɛ yɛ amɛdaadaa sanegbaa mli.
Yɛ kaselɔi ateŋ lɛ, mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli haa amɛtoiboo yaa hiɛ kɛtsɔ kaselɔi
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli atsɔɔmɔ ni amɛbo wiemɔ ni yɔɔ sanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ toi jogbaŋŋ ni amɛnu shishi dani amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ yɛ he.
Ehaa amɛhe hu saa yɛ sanegbaa mli kɛji atsɔɔ kaselɔi ni amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ ni gbɛi ni sa nɔ kɛtsɔ sanegbaa ni mɔ fɛɛ mɔ miitsɔɔ ejwɛŋmɔ lɛ nɔ.
Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli lɛ haa gbekɛbii mɔɔ mlai ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ akutso mli lɛ tamɔ tsuishitoo, nijaa, niiashishinumɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Ekoŋŋ lɛ, ehaa kaselɔi nyɛɔ amɛyeɔ amɛhe nɔ: beiaŋ ni amɛmɛɔ ni eshɛ amɛnɔ lɛ tsɔɔ gbekɛbii lɛ tsuishitoo kɛ henɔyeli, ni ji nibii komɛi ni ahe hia jogbaŋŋ yɛ adesa walashihilɛ mli.
M ɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli kpojiemɔ: Tsɔɔlɔi asha mfoniri ko ni kaselɔi lɛ kɛwo shwɛmɔ mli.
(Kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛgba yitso ko he kadaa, ni akɛmɛ ni eshɛ amɛnɔ ni amɛkɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ ni amɛjie nibii komɛi ni ejaaa gbɛ ni awie lɛ anaa).
Nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi ji:
Ani esa akɛ aŋmɛ sɛkɔndre sukuu kaselɔi agbɛ ni amɛkɛ tɛlifonu aya sukuu?
Naagbai komɛi ni baa niyenii saji ahe kɛji kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ mli tsake.
(Kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko atsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ)
Afa bo ni okwɛ sanegbaa yɛ yitso nɛɛ nɔ;
“Agu rɔba nibii yɛ wɔsukuui lɛ amli nɔŋŋ”.
Kɛ nifeemɔ nɛɛ awo shwɛmɔ mli.
(Nibii komɛi ni sa akɛ okadi: kwɛmɔ bɔ ni kaselɔi lɛ kɛ amɛhe sharia yɛ heshibaa mli, bɔ ni amɛbiɔ saji ni kɛ hɔɔ amɛ atrɔma kɛ bɔ ni amɛfeɔ kɛji aheee amɛjwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ lɛ awooo mli, kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ amɛjwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔi ŋmɛɔ jara).
Te obaakwɛ sanegbaa nɛɛ nɔ oha tɛŋŋ?
Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbe He Sanebimɔi Kuuŋ nitsumɔ
Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ sanegbaa
Nyɛtɛŋa mfoniri ko ni shaa otii lɛ fɛɛ ni awie lɛ mfoniri.
Kaselɔi ni bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ jogbaŋŋ lɛ kɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli atsu nii.
Nyɛboa sanegbaa lɛ toi ni nyɛgbaa bɔ ni amɛmɛ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ he sane ekomeiekomei.
Nyɛtsɔɔa hei ni amɛbɔmɔdɛŋ kɛ hei ni amɛtsuuu nii yɛ yɛ sanegbaa ni obo toi lɛ mli.
Kaselɔi kɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa he nilee ni sa atsu nii ni amɛkɛ nifeemɔnɔ pɔtɛɛ ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ baaha lɛ kɛwo shwɛmɔ mli.
Haramɔ sane ko yɛ otii tamɔ subaŋ kpakpa, STEM, GESI, srawa hewalɛ he saji, falefalefeemɔ, akutso kɛ jeŋmaji saji, hewalɛkpakpamɔ he ni okane.
Tsɔɔmɔ oti loo otii ni yɔɔ sanegbaa lɛ mli ni okɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli he mlai lɛ atsu nii kɛgba he sane.
Ofata mɛi ni miigba afiyeli yɛ Ghana he sane.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni okɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli baatsu nii kɛgbala sane lɛ mli (mii 7)
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa MliMlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli
Gbala nibii etɛ komɛi ahewɔ ni akɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli tsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa nɛ mli.
Akɛ mɔ ko nii miikwɛ kadaaŋgbamɔ yɛ osukuu mli lɛ.
tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi enumɔ komɛi anɔ ni obaanyɛ oha kadaaŋgbalɔi lɛ kɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli atsu nii, (mii 5).
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ 2: NAAGBA OTII
Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ naagbai: Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ maŋ, shika gbɛfaŋ koo akutsoŋ naagbai wuji ni ninaa akutsoŋbii ni hiaa akɛ agba he sane, awo mlai loo akutso lɛ afa he naji.
Naagbai nɛɛ kudɔɔ mɛi asusumɔ ni enaa nɔnyɛɛ yɛ amralo lɛ mlai kɛ enifeemɔi amli ni ejieɔ tsakpaa ni ka maŋbii kɛ amralo lɛ teŋ lɛ kpo.
Wiem ɔkulibii ni sa kɛha yitsei pɔtɛɛ komɛi: Eji wiemɔkulibii hee ni mɛi loo nitsulɔi ehe awo mli ni amɛkɛgbaa amɛnitsumɔ loo nifeemɔ lɛ he sane.
Tamɔ, wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ nitsumɔhei komɛi ji;
Galamsey: ɛskaveta, nu, shika tsuru, tsɔnei, nui, ojarawatɛ, shikpɔŋŋ, kɛmika, kɛ nii kɛ nii
Sukuuyaa: Tsɔɔlɔ, nikasemɔ tsuŋ, nikaselɔi apɔɔtaa (student portal), maŋoo oha, nitsulɔi apɔɔtaa (staff portal), kaa, nikasemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo wolo (course outline), kɛ nii kɛ nii.
Nikasemɔ Nitsumɔ Mɛni nitsumɔhe mli akɛ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ?
Wiemɔkuli, sanefa, sanekuku, niŋmaa mli okadii, najiaŋgbɛi
Kɔmpiuta, deetabeesi, softwɛɛ, emeeli Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔi Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-based learning)
Ankroankro kɛ kuu nitsumɔ Kuui amli
Kaselɔi atsɔɔ sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ sanegbaa yɛ akutsoŋ kɛ gbɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ aha nɔyɛɛ kɛ shweremɔ aba akutso lɛ mli.
Kaselɔi aŋmala wiemɔkulibii otii ni jejee kpo yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ.
Kaselɔi adamɔ wiemɔkulibii hee lɛ nɔ kɛwie naagba otii ni yɔɔ akutso lɛ mli lɛ he.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli
Kaselɔi afee nifeemɔ lɛ ekoŋŋ ni amɛkɛwie naagba ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ha lɛ he loo aha kaselɔi ni jwɛŋmɔ mli kwɔ lɛ ahiɛ sanegbaa lɛ hiɛ.
Kaa Oti: DoK Ŋɛlɛ 3 – Kuu Nitsumɔ Haramɔ yitsei lɛ ateŋ eko fɛɛ eko ni aha lɛ.
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa:
Awie tsakemɔ ni jeŋmaji lɛ kɛba Ghanamaŋ lɛ nɔ.
Apɛi tsakpaa ni yɔɔ nɔ ni jɛɔ ohia kɛ hewalɛkpakpanamɔ mli kɛbaa yɛ maji ni miishwere lɛ anɔ.
Aha naamuu yɛ naagbai ni kɔɔyɔɔŋ tsakemɔ kɛbaa okwaayeli nɔ yɛ Ghana kɛ ŋaagbɛi ni abaanyɛ atsɔ nɔ kɛsa naagbaai nɛɛ anaa.
Mlijaa 1 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ ewie wiemɔkulibii ni sa ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa mli, mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni akɛwieɔ naagba otii ahe.
Agbele rɛjista henɔi lɛ ahiɛ atsɔɔ kaselɔi ni atsɔɔ amɛ shishi.
Awie wiemɔkulibii ni sa yɛ sanegbaa mli lɛ he.
Amɛkase ŋaagbɛi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa mli kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli he hiaa yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Ekoŋŋ lɛ, efɛle naagba otii lɛ ahiɛ atsɔɔ kaselɔi ni aha amɛkɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi lɛ tsu nii kɛwie naagba otii lɛ ahe, ni amɛkɛ ŋaagbɛi nɛɛ tsu nii yɛ amɛkɛ amɛnanemɛi adaadaa sanegbaa mli bɔ ni afee ni sanegbaa lɛ aya lɛ yɔɔ.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ, yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ naagbee mli lɛ, amɛkɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi lɛ baatsu nii kɛwie naagba otii wuji ahe, ana nilee ni sa kɛsɔ wiemɔkulibii ni shishinumɔ yɛ he, atsɔɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii shishinumɔ teŋ ni amɛnyɛ amɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa agba sane.
Shigbɛlɛlmɔ trilii hehiaa jogbaŋŋ yɛ otii kɛ saji ni ahe hiaa lɛ akpojiemɔ mli.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ ja, emli ka shi ni ato naa jogbaŋŋ.
Nibii fɛɛ ni ahe hiaa lɛ yɛ mli.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ jieɔ shishinumɔ yɛ abifao kpojiemɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli fitsofitso.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ ja ni ato naa jogbaŋŋ.
Nibii komɛi ni yɔɔ nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ mli lɛ yeee hegbɔ loo anaaa piamɔ kɛha tsakpaai ni awie he lɛ.
Akɛ nibii fɛɛ ni ahe hiaa lɛ woɔ mli.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ tsɔɔ abifao kpojiemɔ lɛ shishinumɔ fitsofitso.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ fioo ko pɛ yɔɔ ni piaa otii ni atsi ta lɛ sɛɛ.
Wiemɔi lɛ ekomɛi ejaaa, atsɔɔɔ shishi jogbaŋŋ ni ekomɛi hu bɛ faŋŋ.
Presentation Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ mɔ lɛ nuuu abifao kpojiemɔ nifeemɔ lɛ shishi (abifao kpojiemɔ mligbalamɔ) Wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu nii yɛ abifao kpojiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ bɛ shifimɔ ko.
Wiemɔi lɛ yeee emuu.
Nibii ni ahe hiaa lɛ elaaje ni afutu lɛ hu.
Nitsɔɔmɔ (Gbɛjianɔtoo) Ato slaidii lɛ anaa jogbaŋŋ, eyɛ fɛo ni akɛ, ato niŋmaa lɛ naa jogbaŋŋ, mfonirii ni niŋmaai lɛ hu faa tsɔ.
Akɛ suui kɛ mfonirii etsu nii bɔ ni sa.
Tɔmɔi komɛi tamɔ (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) bɛ slaidi lɛ mli.
Ato slaidii lɛ anaa jogbaŋŋ ni sa hiŋmɛiaŋ, niŋmaai lɛ faaa tsɔ yɛ slaidi lɛ nɔ.
Akɛ sui kɛ mfonirii etsu nii jogbaŋŋ.
Tɔmɔi lɛ faa tsɔ (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) Afutu mfonirii kɛ niŋmai lɛ ni akɛ sui kɛ nibii krokomɛi tsuuu nii jogbaŋŋ.
Tɔmɔi komɛi yɛ mli (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) yɛ slaidi lɛ mli.
Slaidi lɛ efee haŋtsii ni niŋmaai lɛ efa tsɔ ni mfonirii ko kwraa bɛ mli.
Akɛ sui hu tsuuu nii jogbaŋŋ.
