Kɛji akɛ miidia (mfonirii) tsu nii lɛ, atsɔɔɔ he ni ana kɛjɛ yɛ slaidi lɛ nɔ.
Gbɛjianɔtoo ko kwraa pɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ he.
Tɔmɔi lɛ faa (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) yɛ slaidi lɛ nɔ.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli Nitsɔɔmɔ (Wiemɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ) Etsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ni ato he gbɛjianɔ yɛ be kukuoo mli (minitii 20).
Kuŋbii lɛ eko fɛɛ eko wiemɔ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Efee faŋŋ akɛ amɛsaa amɛhe.
Etsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ mli ni ejieɔ hesalɛ yɛ wiemɔ mli yɛ be kukuoo ni akɛha kuu mli (minitii 20).
Kuŋbii lɛ eko fɛɛ eko wie ni eje kpo faŋŋ akɛ amɛsaa amɛhe.
Ebɔɔɔ mɔdɛŋ tsɔ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli ni tsɔɔ akɛ ehe esako tsɔ yɛ wiemɔ mli.
Eho be ni ato lɛ he fioo.
Kuuŋbii lɛ ekomɛi wie fe ekomɛi.
Amɛmiihia hesaamɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Ebɔɔɔ mɔdɛŋ kwraa yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli ni tsɔɔ akɛ ehe esako kwraa yɛ wiemɔ mli.
Amɛnitsɔɔmɔ lɛ ho be ni ato lɛ he kwraa ni jeee kuuŋbii lɛ fɛɛ ni wie.
Eje kpo faŋŋ akɛ amɛsaaa amɛhe.
OTSI 2 NIKASEMƆ HE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ 1 1.
Kɛha mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) atsu nii lɛ, feemɔ nibii nɛɛ:
Kɛ wiemɔ he gbɛjianɔtoo atsu nii
Wo onine nɔ kɛtsɔɔ akɛ ootao owie
Wo naadaalɔ ko ni ekudɔ sanegbaa lɛ
Wo sanegbalɔi lɛ ahewalɛ ni amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi kpawo ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ fɛɛ mli lɛ - mii 7
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ ekpaa mli lɛ - mii 6
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ enumɔ mli lɛ - mii 5
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ enumɔmli lɛ - mii 4
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ etɛmli lɛ - mii 3
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ mli lɛ - mii 2
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ eko mli lɛ - mi 1
Otii nɛɛ ahewɔ:
Ehaa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ kɛ ehe woɔ mli.
Ehaa akɛ he woɔ mli jogbaŋŋ.
Ehaaa mɔ kome too ahe sanegbaa lɛ kɛfɔ etsitsi.
Kɛji eŋma otii etɛ ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ mli lɛ - mii 3
Kɛji eŋma otii lɛ mli enyɔ lɛ - mii 2
Kɛji eŋma otii mli ekome lɛ - mi 1
Kwɛmɔ hesalɛ ni atsi ta yɛ sabimɔ 1a mli lɛ ni oha hesalɛi ni ajie kpo yɛ sukuu kadaaŋgbamɔ lɛ mli lɛ mii OTI HE NI AWIEƆ LƐ 2 (mii 16) Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
Wiemɔi lɛ mli ka shi ni ehe hia kɛha sanegbaa lɛ.
Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
Wiemɔi lɛ mli ka shi ni ehe hia sanegbaa lɛ hu shi shishinumɔ ntai komɛi yɛ mli.
Wiemɔi lɛ bɛ faŋŋ tsɔ ni wiemɔi lɛ ekomɛi ahe ehiaa yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli.
Wiemɔi lɛ bɛ faŋŋ ni wiemɔi ni ahe hiaa sanegbaa lɛ faaa.
Wiemɔkulibii hee kɛ niŋmaa mlai Akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ja tsu nii.
Akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa kɛha yitso lɛ he wiemɔ tsuɔ nii.
Tɔmɔi yɛ niŋmaa mlai lɛ mli lɛ yɛ mlɛo.
Akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ja tsu nii.
Tɔmɔi bibiibii komɛi yɛ niŋmaa mlai lɛ mli ni bei komɛi lɛ eji tɔmɔ oti.
Bei komɛi lɛ akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsuuu nii.
Mɛi komɛi bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ amɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni wieɔ yitso lɛ he lɛ tsuɔ nii.
Tɔmɔ bibii ni bei komɛi lɛ eji tɔmɔ otii.
Wiemɔkulibii lɛ ewieee oti lɛ he.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii bibioo yɛ ni wieɔ yitso wiemɔkulibii lɛ he.
Tɔmɔi bibii kɛ wuji fɛɛ jeɔ kpo faŋŋ.
Oti: SANEGBAA Oti Mlijaa: Fɔnɔlɔji (Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi) Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi: Akɛ nilee ni ana kɛjɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli lɛ atsu nii yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ shishinumɔ kpo yɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi yɛ Ga mli.
Niahefɛlemɔ Ha kaselɔi nitsumɔ ni amɛfee yɛ kuui amli yɛ Otsi 3 lɛ nɔ ni oha amɛ be ni sa ni amɛkɛfee.
Kɛ SS app lɛ afee sanebimɔi kɛ mlai lɛ.
Adiki kaselɔi anitsumɔ lɛ kɛji amɛgbe naa nɔŋŋ ni akɛ amimii lɛ awo SAP lɛ nɔ.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ tsaa fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni wɔkase yɛ afii ni eho lɛ amli.
Kaselɔi baakase fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi, amɛbaakase blawa henɔi kɛ blawa jebɔ yɛ Ga mli.
Nilee ni amɛbaana yɛ mli lɛ baaye ebua amɛ yɛ wiemɔkulibii atsɛmɔ, nikanemɔ, kɛ wiemɔkulibii ashishinumɔ mli jogbaŋŋ.
No ji akɛ, nilee ni amɛbaana yɛ nikasemɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ baaha amɛnyɛ amɛna hesalɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Ekoŋŋ lɛ, ebaaha kaselɔi ana wiemɔkulibii hee babaoo ni esaa eha amɛhiɛ aba amɛhe nɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii lɛ ahe.
Agbɛnɛ hu lɛ, fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ashishinumɔ lɛ baawa kɛgbala fɔnɔlɔji mlai kɛ gbɛjianɔtooi ni kudɔɔ wiemɔ lɛ kɛ tsakemɔi ni baa wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hia kaselɔi jeee yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli pɛ, shi ekɛ tsakpaa ko baa nikasemɔi tamɔ Blɔfo, kɛ maji krokomɛi anɔ wiemɔi lɛ hu.
Mlijaa nɛɛ waa kaselɔi ni amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛna lɛ atsu nii kɛnu fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ shishi.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ abɔ mɔdɛŋ ni eha mɔ fɛɛ mɔ kɛ ehe awo nikasemɔ lɛ mli, ekɛ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii ni eha kaai ni sa beiaŋ ni nikasemɔ lɛ yaa nɔ lɛ bɔ ni afee ni eye ebua kaselɔi lɛ.
Otsii kɛ gbɛkpamɔi ni yɔɔ mlijaa nɛɛ mli ji:
Otsi 3: Agbala fɔnɔlɔji gbɔjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli (tamɔ, gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi)
Otsi 4: Awie fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli (blawa jebɔi) NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nikasemɔ ŋaagbɛ ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ kɛ Ga wiemɔ kasemɔ kɛ etsɔɔmɔ lɛ yeɔ hegbɔ.
Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) ji ŋaagbɛi ni akɛ jeŋ naagbai tsuɔ nii kɛtsɔɔ kaselɔi anii.
Ŋaagbɛi komɛi ni yɔɔ mli ji ankroankro nikasemɔ, kuu enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ,
Mlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi kuu ni afutu kaselɔi lɛ kɛ klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ kɛ klasi lɛ nitsɔɔmɔ.
Nɛkɛ ŋaagbɛi nɛɛ baanyɛ eha kaselɔi ayiŋkaa aya hiɛ, amɛhe asa yɛ naagbai anaamuu mli, ni amɛna hesalɛ yɛ wiemɔ mli.
Ekɛ hegbɛi tamɔ, nitsumɔ mli ekomefeemɔ, kuuŋ nitsumɔ kɛ hiɛnyiɛmɔ hesalɛ fɛɛ baa.
Kaselɔi baakase bɔ ni amɛbaana wiemɔ ni amɛkɛtsu nii.
Kaselɔi ni le nii fe nine lɛ, ahaa amɛ nitsumɔ lɛ eko kɛfataa he ni ahaa amɛnyiɛɔ amɛnanemɛi lɛ ekomɛi ahiɛ ni amɛyeɔ amɛbuaa amɛ kɛna shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ yɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ yɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ he lɛ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ akadi kaselɔi ni yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔ naagbai lɛ ni etsɔ ŋaagbɛ nɔ kɛku nakai naagba lɛ naa.
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaa gbɛ ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ mɔ fɛɛ mɔ baana hesalɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ, kaselɔi lɛ niiashishinumɔ kɛ amɛsusumɔi baaya hiɛ.
Yelikɛbuamɔ kpitiokpitio ni tsɔɔlɔ kɛhaa kaselɔi beiaŋ ni nitsumɔ lɛ miiya nɔ lɛ yeɔ bua kaselɔi lɛ kɛnaa shidaamɔ kpakpa yɛ nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Kaa ŋaagbɛi lɛ kɛ ŋaagbɛi ni kadiɔ kaselɔi aniiashishinumɔ kɛ amɛnɔyaa yɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ lɛ he.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 henɔ sanebimɔi lɛ ekomɛi ji saneŋmaa kuku loo mɔnaa wiemɔ nitsɔɔmɔ ni akɛna kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ.
Ahaa amɛ ŋɛlɛ 3 yiŋkaa sanebimɔi kɛtsɔ saneŋmaa loo nii amlipɛimɔ kɛna bɔ ni kaselɔi susumɔ kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ahe.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi aka kaselɔi lɛ yɛ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ beiaŋ ni nikasemɔ lɛ miiya nɔ lolo kɛ nikasemɔ lɛ naagbee hu kɛna kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko amɔdɛŋbɔɔ kɛ mii ni amɛna kɛ hei ni esa akɛ aye abua amɛ yɛ.
LI.1 Gbalamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli.
LI.2 Gbalamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli.
Mlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi OTSI 3 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Gbalmɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ mli.
Gbalamɔ Ga vaoli kpojee lɛ mli.
(e.g., wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee)
Gbalamɔ Ga kɔnsonanti kpojee lɛ mli (e.g., wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee) Afi 2 Otsi 2: Gbalamɔ gbee nitsumɔ amli (e.g., sanemuu amli, wiemɔkulibii amli kɛ wiemɔi amli) OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: FƆNƆLƆJI GBƐJIANƆTOOI (GBƐƐMƆ NƆYELI GBƐJIANƆTOOI) Fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi mligbalamɔ: Fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ji ŋaagbɛi ni haa gbɛɛmɔi ni wielɔi kɛtsuɔ nii lɛ tsakeɔ bɔ ni afee ni wiemɔ afee mlɛo.
Abaanyɛ agbala mli hu akɛ eji mlai ni akɛtsuɔ ni kɛha wiemɔkulibii atsɛrɛmɔ afee mlɛo.
Ajara fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ awo gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli kɛ blawa shikamɔ mli.
Anaa gbɛjianɔtooi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ yɛ wiemɔ mli pɛ.
Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi Henɔi Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi: ji fɔnɔlɔji ŋaagbɛ ni baa kɛji gbɛɛmɔ ko kɔ gbɛɛmɔ ni ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ su ni ekɛ tsakemɔ baa esɔɔmɔ kɛ efeemɔ mli.
Gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ baanyɛ ekɛ tsakemɔ aba bɔ ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ ni ehaa anaa alofon yɛ hei ni gbɛɛmɔ lɛ jieɔ kpo yɛ lɛ.
Gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ waa kɛgbalaa gbɛjianɔtooi ni kudɔɔ wiemɔ, kɛ tsakemɔi ni eba wiemɔi amli yɛ bei pɔtɛɛ ko mli.
Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli jieɔ ehe kpo yɛ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ.
Naabu kokloo feem ɔ: Enɛ ji gbɛ nɔ ni kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ naa naabu kokloo su ko kɛfataa he ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ yɛ lɛ.
Enɛ baa kɛji vaoli kokoloo tamɔ /u, o, ɔ/ loo glaadi /w/, nyiɛ kɔnsonati ko sɛɛ kpaakpa yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ mli.
Okadi ni akɛyoɔ naabu kokloo feemɔ ji [w].
Anaa naabufemɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli;
Daaŋ agbafeemɔ: Eji gbɛjianɔtoo ko ni kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ ko naa lilɛi lɛ henɔwomɔ kɛninaa daaŋ agba lɛ kɛfataa he ni asɔɔ yɛ lɛ.
Anaa gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ kɛji hiɛ vaoli /i, e, ɛ/ nyiɛ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ ko sɛɛ kpaakpa.
Okadi ni akɛyɔseɔ lɛ ji [j].
Nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi ji;
Gugɔŋjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔfeemɔ: Eji beiaŋ ni naabu gbɛɛmɔ ko feɔ gugɔjiaŋ kɛji eje kpo yɛ he ni gugɔŋ vaoli ko yɔɔ.
Bei pii lɛ, vaoli titri ni tsɔɔ nɛkɛ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli shi bei komɛi lɛ, naabu kɔnsonantii komɛi hu nyɛɔ etsɔɔ gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ.
No hewɔ lɛ, ayɛ gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ henɔi enyɔ.
Nomɛi ji vaoli gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ kɛ kɔnsonanti gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ.
Mlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi Vaoli gugɔjiaŋgbɛɛmɔ ji nɔ ni afɔɔ naa yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Okadi ni akɛyɔseɔ lɛ ji [ ̃].
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ komɛi ji: nu [nũ], ma [mã], duŋ [dũŋ].
Nikasemɔ he nitsumɔ
Gbalamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Gbakamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni baa nɛɛ amli:
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii etɛtɛɛtɛ yɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Kaa bɔ ni atsɛɔ wiemɔkulibii lɛ okwɛ ni oha ohiɛ ama fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ nɔ.
Bibioo Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) – (ankroankro kɛ kuu)
Kuu ni afutu mɛi ni bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ kɛ mɛi ni ebɔɔɔ mɔdɛŋ tsɔ lɛ.
Kuui lɛ agbala fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amɛ ni amɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ eko fɛɛ eko he.
Kuui lɛ aka amɛyiŋ kɛgba gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli henɔi tamɔ naabu kokloo feemɔ, daaŋagba feemɔ, gugɔjiaŋgbɛɛmɔ feemɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he lɛ he sane.
Kaselɔi yɛ kuui amli aha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli henɔi ni awie he lɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi ni amɛpɛi mli.
Apɛi wiemɔkulibii ni aha amɛ lɛ mli ni amɛtsɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli henɔ ni akɛtsu nii lɛ (tsɔɔlɔ aha nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi yɛ Ga mli)
Baatsɔɔ klasi lɛ onitsumɔ lɛ.
Gbalamɔ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi etɛ ni yɔɔ Ga mli ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii etɛtɛɛtɛ yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ kɛ gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ teŋ ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ yitsei komɛi Yɛ okutso lɛ mli lɛ, ani oyɔse akɛ sanegbaa kpakpa fɛɛ lɛ, toiboo kɛ mɛɛ yaa nɔ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli.
Nyɛtsua nii yɛ kuui amli
Kɛtsɔɔ bɔ ni mɛi mɛɔ yɛ sane ko he wiemɔ mli yɛ shiai loo yɛ sukuui amli.
Atsɔɔ wiemɔ kɛ toiboo subaŋ kpakpai.
Kɛsaa he kɛjie shwɛmɔ kpo kɛtsɔɔ bɔ ni mɛi kɛ wiemɔi ni bulɛ yɛ he gbaa sane kɛtaoɔ akutso lɛ naagbai lɛ atsabaa.
Mlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi OTSI 4 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Agbala fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ (blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi) Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 2
Gbalamɔ Ga kɔnsonanti kpojee lɛ mli (e.g., wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee) Afi 2 Otsi 2: Gbalamɔ gbee nitsumɔ amli (e.g., sanemuu amli, wiemɔkulibii amli kɛ wiemɔi amli) OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: FƆNƆLƆJI GBƐJIANƆTOO (BLAWA SHIKAMƆ GBƐJIANƆTOOI) Blawa Shikamɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi Blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ kɛ tsakemɔ baa blawa shikamɔ lɛ mli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ekomɛi ji vaoli gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ, gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ, gbɛɛmɔ gbɛhe tsakemɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ wiemɔkulibii lɛ tsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ ni wiemɔkulibii tamɔ wiemɔkulibii ni ama lɛ kɛ Ga blawa shikamɔ lɛ yeɔ hegbɔ.
Gbɛɛmɔ Laajemɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ ji fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo ni haa gbɛɛmɔ ko laajeɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii amli yɛ he pɔtɛɛ yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ mli.
Nɛkɛ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ haa tsɛrɛmɔ feɔ mlɛo ni edamɔɔ liŋwistiki nibii komɛi anɔ tamɔ ehaa gbɛɛmɔ lɛ sɔɔmɔ lɛ feɔ mlɛo yɛ fonɛtisi shihilɛ mli.
Ejieɔ ehe kpo titri yɛ wiemɔ mli, ni ahaa gbɛɛmɔi komɛi laajeɔ bɔ ni afee ni wiemɔ lɛ aya lɛ oya.
Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jieɔ ehe kpo yɛ wiemɔkulibii komɛi amli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli kɛ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ama kɛba Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli hu.
Anyɛɔ akɛtsuɔ nii hu kɛsaa blawa shikamɔ ni ejaaa gbɛ hu, titri ji wiemɔkulibii ni ama.
Enyɛɔ ekɛ tsakemɔ baa wiemɔkulibii ni abaanyɛ ana kɛjɛ nakai wiemɔkuli lɛ mli ni esaa mɔfɔlɔji he hu.
Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ haa wɔnuɔ tsakemɔ ni baa wiemɔi amli ni ejeɔ tsakemɔ ni akutso loo kuu ko kɛbaa gbɛɛmɔ lɛ tsɛrɛmɔ mli.
Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jieɔ ehe kpo yɛ hei srɔtoi yɛ wiemɔkuli mli
Shishijee gbɛhe Ebaa yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ shishijee ni klɛŋklɛŋ gbɛɛmɔ yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ mli laajeɔ.
Naagbee gbɛhe Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ lɛ jieɔ ehe kpo yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ naagbee.
Anaa enɛ titri yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ama ejaakɛ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi enyiɛɛɛ amɛsɛɛ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli;
hewɔ lɛ atsɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ nɔ kɛsaa kɔnsonanti ni nyiɛ amɛsɛɛ lɛ naa.
Gbɛɛmɔ Mlihɔlɔmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ ji fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo ni haa akɛ gbɛɛmɔ hɔlɔɔ wiemɔkuli mli bɔ ni afee ni anyɛ atsɛ loo kɛsaa eblawa shikamɔ lɛ.
Bei pii lɛ, akɛ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ hɔlɔɔ wiemɔkulibii amli bɔ ni afee ni ehɔlɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi ni tsara nɔ lɛ koni anyɛ atsɛ.
Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ lɛ haa wɔnuɔ bɔ ni fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ tsuɔ nii wiemɔ mli ni eha esɔɔmɔ afee mlɛo.
Tamɔ gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ lɛ, gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ yɛ hei srɔtoi yɛ wiemɔkulibii amli.
Gbɛɛmɔ gbɛhe tsakemɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ gbɛhe tsakemɔ ji liŋwistiki gbɛjianɔtoo ko ni haa atsakeɔ gbɛɛmɔi loo blawai ashikamɔ yɛ wiemɔkuli mli ni kɛ tsakemɔ baa etsɛrɛmɔ kɛ fonɛtisi gbɛjianɔtoo mli.
Tsakemɔ ni baa wiemɔ lɛ he lɛ haa gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ jieɔ ehe kpo ni ekɛ tsakemɔ hu baa fɔnɔlɔji mlai ni kudɔɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ.
Gbɛɛmɔ gbɛhe tsakemɔ tsɔɔ bɔ ni wielɔi tsakeɔ wiemɔ lɛ hiɛ bɔ ni afee ni etsɛmɔ afee mlɛo loo anyɛ anu shishi ni etsuɔ nitsumɔ kple yɛ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ nɔyaa kɛ wiemɔ lɛ tsakemɔ yɛ bei amli.
Tamɔ bɔ ni atsakeɔ tsɛrɛmɔi ahiɛ yɛ wɔdaa daa wiemɔi amli.
Wiemɔkulibii ni atsɛrɛɛɛ lɛ jogbaŋŋ lɛ ekomɛi ji kɔshikɔshi/ shikɔshikɔ, tokota/kotoka, lajeko/lakoje.
Tsɔɔmɔ ni ogbala blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo henɔi ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli:
Tsɔɔmɔ blawa jebɔ ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli
Tsɔɔmɔ blawa jebɔ henɔ ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii ni jeɔ jebɔi nɛɛ akpo Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) - (ankroankro kɛ kuu)
Agbala fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli titri blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ (gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ, gbɛɛmɔ gbɛhe tsakemɔ, gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ).
Ha henɔi nɛɛ eko fɛɛ eko he nɔkwɛmɔnii ni opɛi mli.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ Apɛi wiemɔkulibii ni aha amɛ lɛ mli ni amɛtsɔɔ blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo henɔ ni akɛtsu nii lɛ ni koni klasi lɛ agba he sane (tsɔɔlɔ aha nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi yɛ Ga mli)
Mlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi Kaa Otii: DoK Ŋɛlɛ 3 – Niiamlipɛimɔ Sane Yɛ okutso lɛ mli lɛ, oyɔse akɛ, srɔto ko yɛ bɔ ni atsɛɔ wiemɔkulibii komɛi kɛ bɔ ni aŋmalaa lɛ teŋ.
Mlijaa 2 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ baakwɛ nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni akase yɛ otsii enyɔ ni eho lɛ mli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, agbala fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli atsɔɔ kaselɔi.
Wɔkase gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli kɛ blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli.
Agbala henɔi bibii ni yɔɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ ashishi lɛ ni akɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii fi sɛɛ.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ kɛji kaselɔi gbe mlijaa nɛɛ naa lɛ, amɛbaajie nilee yɛ nɛkɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ amli lɛ atsɛtsɛɛ wiemɔkulibii jogbaŋŋ ni amɛkane nii jogbaŋŋ hu.
Gbɛɛmɔnɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ji naabu kokloo feemɔ, daaŋagba feemɔ, gugɔŋgbɛɛmɔ feemɔ.
Mii 3 kɛha gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko kɛ ehe nɔkwɛmɔnii Mii 2 kɛha gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ kɛ ehe nɔkwɛmɔnii Mi 1 kɛha gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ateŋ ekome kɛ ehe nɔkwɛmɔnɔ
Srɔtoi otii lɛ ji:
Gbɛjianɔtoo: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔ kɛ eko fataa he shi gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jeɔ eko yɛ mli.
Nɔnyɛɛ: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ tsakeɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ jebɔ loo eshishinumɔ shi gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ tsakeɔ tsɛrɛmɔ lɛ shi bei pii lɛ etsakeee wiemɔkulibii lɛ ashishinumɔ lɛ.
Nifeemɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ kɛ gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jeɔ ehe kpo yɛ liŋwiskiti gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ, tamɔ mɔfɔlɔji, fɔnɔlɔji loo sintasi.
Mii 3 kɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii srɔtoi ni atsi ta lɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi Mii 2 kɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii enyɔ yɛ srɔtoi ni atsi ta lɛ shishi Mi 1 kɛha nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kome yɛ srɔtoi ni atsi ta lɛ shishi.
OTSI 4 Bɔ ni abaadiki sane ko mlikwɛmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli I.
Hiɛgbelemɔ faŋŋ yɛ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yitso lɛ ahe.
Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli he sɛɛnamɔi yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ he sanebimɔi kɛ gbɛkpamɔi (kɛyashi mii 4)
Shigbɛlɛmɔ sanebimɔ ni yɛ faŋŋ.
Gbɛkpamɔi krɛdɛɛ ni he hiaa shigbɛlɔmɔ lɛ.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ hesɛɛnamɔ (kɛyashi mii 3)
Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli shishinumɔ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ he sɛɛnamɔ
Bɔ ni akɛbaatsu nii kɛ esɛɛ saji II.
Nibii ni aŋmala yɛ yitso lɛ he (mii 15)
Shigbɛlɛmɔ he ŋaagbɛi lɛ nɔmimaa (kɛyashi 5)
Fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ashishinumɔ
Bɔ ni akɛ ŋaagbɛi ni sa lɛ tsuɔ nii
Nifeemɔ ni damɔ wiemɔ lɛ nɔ (mii 5)
Ga wiemɔ lɛ jebɔ
Shigbɛlɛmɔi krokomɛi ni atsɔ hiɛ afee yɛ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli
Nibii ni aŋmala yɛ yitso lɛ he lɛ mliphimɔ
Gbɛi ni ashimɔ yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ mli ni abaanyɛ atsu he nii wɔsɛɛ lɛ yopomɔ III.
Saji lɛ aharamɔ (kɛyashi mii 5)
Saji haramɔ ŋaagbɛi lɛ amligbalamɔ (tamɔ, sanegbaa, mɔnaa wiemɔ lala)
Nɔ hewɔ ni okɛ ŋaagbɛ lɛ tsu nii lɛ.
Sane lɛ mlipɛimɔ (kɛyashi mii 5)
Sane mlipɛimɔ ŋaagbɛi lɛ mlitsɔɔmɔ
Nɔ hewɔ ni ŋaagbɛi lɛ ja kɛha shigbɛlɛmɔ sanebimɔ lɛ
Esa akɛ oyoo shimɔhei komɛi ni aaanyɛ eje kpo.
Ŋaagbɛi ni akɛbaasa naatsii lɛ anaa IV.
Saji ni je kpo lɛ amlitsɔɔmɔ (mii 25)
Gbalamɔ saji ni je kpo lɛ amli (mii 15 marks)
Jiemɔ saji lɛ akpo yɛ gbɛ faŋŋ nɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ni sa ni akɛtsu nii
Mlipɛimɔ kɛ Shishitsɔɔmɔ (mii 10)
Damɔ shigbɛlɛmɔ ni afee lɛ nɔ kɛtsɔɔ saji ni je kpo lɛ amli.
Nɔnyɛɛ ni enaa yɛ wiemɔ lɛ tsɔɔmɔ, ekasemɔ loo mlawoo he lɛ mligbalamɔ.
Naamuu kɛ jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ (mii 25)
Saji lɛ anɔdoomɔ (mii 5)
Saji ni jejee kpo lɛ anɔdoomɔ
Naamuu ni ja ni wieɔ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ fɛɛ he.
Jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔi kɛha wɔsɛɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ, wiemɔ lɛ tsɔɔmɔ loo wiemɔ lɛ he mlawoo
Naatoo, Jebɔ kɛ woji ni ana yelikɛbuamɔ yɛ (mii 10)
Naatoo kɛ tsakpaa (mii 5)
Bɔ ni ato niŋmaa lɛ naa yɛ ŋaa gbɛ nɔ.
Niŋmaa faŋŋ kɛ tsakpaa yɛ niŋmaa mli.
Mɛi ni eŋmala nii yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ he ni okɛtsu nii lɛ atatsii jogbaŋŋ.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ
OTI: SANEGBAA Oti Mlijaa: Nikanemɔ Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ nilee ni ona kɛjɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mlitsɔɔmɔ lɛ ajie otii akpo yɛ niŋmaa mli.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Jiemɔ shishinumɔ kpo yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ he yɛ niŋmaa mli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Niahefɛlemɔ Feemɔ Mid-Semester Kaa yɛ otsi 6 lɛ nɔ.
Esa akɛ edamɔ otsi 1 kɛyashi otsi 5 nɔ.
Adiki kaselɔi aniŋmaai lɛ ni akɛ nɔ ni amɛna lɛ awo SAP lɛ mli.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ tsaa otii ni wɔtsɔ hiɛ kɛkase yɛ afii enyɔ lɛ mli lɛ anɔ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, kaselɔi baakai amɛhe yɛ susumɔi otii ni yɔɔ niŋmaa loo sanegbaa mli he nikasemɔ lɛ nɔ.
Wɔbaatsa nɔ ni wɔwie sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ he sane ni wɔya nɔ ni wɔtsɔɔ hei ni amɛyɔɔ yɛ niŋmaa loo nikanemɔ ko mli.
Nilee yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ he lɛ baaye ebua kaselɔi ni amɛhe akpɔ yɛ niŋmaa kɛ sanegbaa mli.
Amɛbaanyɛ amɛha jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔi ni amli kwɔ hu yɛ sanegbaai amli.
Ekoŋŋ lɛ, ebaawa kaselɔi ni amɛtsɔɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ susumɔi ni ahe hia jogbaŋŋ kɛ nɔ ni he ehiaaa tsɔ yɛ sanegbaai amli.
Enɛ baaha amɛnyɛ amɛdoo nikanemɔ nɔ ni eha amɛniiashishinumɔ hu aya hiɛ pam.
Aakpa tsɔɔlɔi agbɛ akɛ amɛkɛ nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa kaselɔi taoɔ saji anaa, kɛ klasi muu fɛɛ nitsumɔ atsɔɔ yitso nɛɛ.
Otsi 5: Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli
Otsi 6: Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli lɛ ahara otii yɛ niŋmaai amli.
NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐ NƆDOOMƆ Akɛ ŋaagbɛi babaoo tsu nii yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ kɛ nikasemɔ ŋaagbɛi ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ tsɔɔmɔ mli.
Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) ji ŋaagbɛ ni akɛ jeŋ saji ni ale lɛ
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ tsuɔ nii yɛ nikasemɔ mli kɛwaa kaselɔi ashishinumɔ ni nifeemɔ aya hiɛ.
Nifeemɔ nɛɛ baanyɛ eha hesalɛ yɛ yiŋkaa, saji anaataomɔ kɛ sanegbaa kɛ wiemɔ mli aya hiɛ pam.
Gbɛkpamɔi DOK ŋɛlɛ Kaa Ŋaagbɛ/ henɔ Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli 2 Sanebimɔ Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli lɛ ahara otii yɛ niŋmaai amli.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti WalɔMlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ OTSI 5 Nikasemɔ gbɛkamɔ: Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli Kaimɔ Afi1 Otsi 3
Daamɔ nilee yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii nɔ kɛkane niŋmaa ko ni oha sanebimɔi ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ ahetoo.
Wiemɔ nikanemɔ vii ŋaagbɛi lɛ ahe (hiŋmɛi loo waobii tsimɔ, wiemɔkulibii loo sanekukuji anaa, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) Afi 2 Otsi 3
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sanegbaa ko mli.
Gbalamɔ oti wuji ni yɔɔ sanegbaa mli.
OTSII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU OTI K Ɛ SANEMUU OTI WALƆ
Sanemuu oti mligbalamɔ: Sanemuu oti ji sanemuu ko ni jieɔ susumɔ oti ni yɔɔ kuku ko mli kpo.
Bei pii lɛ, sanemuu oti lɛ ji wiemɔ ni jeɔ kuku lɛ shishi (shi jeee be fɛɛ be).
Esa akɛ sanemuu oti lɛ mli wiemɔ lɛ afee kuku yɛ be mli ni ehaa kuku lɛ mli susumɔ oti lɛ mli ka shi faŋŋ lolo, ni etsɔɔ oti ni niŋmalɔ lɛ miisumɔ ni ewie yɛ nakai sanemuu oti lɛ he lɛ hu.
Esa akɛ eje kpo yɛ kuku lɛ mli klɛŋklɛŋ wiemɔ lɛ mli loo ebɛŋkɛ.
Agbɛnɛ hu, ehe miihia ni ekɛ sanemuu oti muu lɛ fɛɛ ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli loo niŋmaa lɛ mli lɛ abɔ he koni ekwɛ akɛ ekɛ ehe kpaa gbee.
Tamɔ ɔkwɛmɔnɔ nɛɛ, sanemuu oti ni yɔɔ kuku ko mli yɛ saneŋmaa ko ni yitso ji “Sɛɛnamɔ yɛ sukuuyaa he” lɛ baanyɛ afee “Nitsumɔ ni awoɔ nyɔmɔ jogbaŋŋ ni anaa lɛ ji sɛɛnamɔi ni he hiaa fe fɛɛ ni yɔɔ sukuuyaa he lɛ ateŋ ekome.”
Sanemuu oti walɔ mligbalamɔ: Sanemuu oti walɔ ni yɔɔ kuku ko mli lɛ maa oti ni sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ nɔ mi.
Esaa egbalaa oti ni sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ mli.
Eji sanemuu henɔ ni haa nɔmimaa kɛ piamɔ wiemɔi kɛkɔ nɔ ni sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ.
Sanemuu oti walɔ lɛ haa kanelɔi lɛ naa shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ yɛ sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ ni etsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni nɔ fɛɛ nɔ he ni awieɔ he lɛ yɛ mli lɛ.
Bei pii lɛ, anaa sanemuu oti walɔi nɛɛ yɛ kuku lɛ teŋ.
No ji akɛ, enyiɛɔ sanemuu oti lɛ sɛɛ ni ebaa dani naamuu wiemɔi lɛ baa yɛ kuku lɛ mli.
Bei komɛi hu lɛ, sanemuu oti walɔ lɛ nyɛɔ ejeɔ kuku lɛ shishi, Kɛ ehooo kwraa lɛ, sanemuu ni feɔ sanemuu oti walɔi yɛ kuku mli lɛ afee sanemuji enyɔ kɛyashi ejwɛ yɛ kuku kome mli.
Esa akɛ ato amɛ naa jogbaŋŋ ni ekawie yitso hee loo susumɔ hee kpo he.
Kɛji oonyiɛ kaselɔi ahiɛ kaŋmala sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ, maa nɔ mii akɛ ehe hia akɛ tsakpaa aba sanemuu oti lɛ kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ ateŋ.
Wiemɔkulibii kɛ sanekukuji ashishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ hu haa tsakpaa lɛ jeɔ kpo faŋŋ.
Ha amɛkɛ nibii ni ahe hia pɛ awo mli ni amɛkɛ kanelɔi lɛ aniishishinumɔ ŋɛlɛ hu awo amɛjwɛŋmɔ mli.
Esa akɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ atsa nɔ ni awieɔ lɛ nɔ ni eko fɛɛ eko hu awa ni shɛɛwiemɔ muu ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ aŋmɛ jara nɔ.
Sanemuu oti walɔi komɛi ni baanyɛ ahi sanemuu oti nɛɛ shishi ji: Sukuyaa he hiaa jogbaŋŋ.
Ehaa mɛi naa nilee kɛjɛɔ nikasemɔi tamɔ akuntaabuu, jeŋshikpamɔ he saji, Ghanamaŋ wiemɔ kɛ adebɔɔ nibii amli.
Eyeɔ ebua maŋ lɛ nɔyaa hu kɛtsɔ nitsulɔi ni he esa tamɔ tsɔɔlɔi, datrɛfoi, maŋhebulɔi kɛ onukpai yɛ akutsei amli.
Mɛi kpanaa ni yɔɔ wɔkustei lɛ amli ni wɔnaa lɛ fɛɛ etsɔmɔ sukuu tsɔsemɔ lɛ mli da.
Sanemuji nɛɛ eko fɛɛ eko wieɔ sanemuu oti lɛ he ni ehaa kanelɔi lɛ nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni baaye ebua amɛ kɛnu shɛɛwiemɔ lɛ shishi.
Niŋmalɔ ko baanyɛ ekɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ ateŋ eko fɛɛ eko atsu nii.
Sanemuu oti walɔi ni ja fe fɛɛ haramɔ lɛ damɔɔ nɔ ni niŋmalɔ lɛ taoɔ ekɛŋmɛ jara nɔ yɛ kuku lɛ mli lɛ.
Sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yoomɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli.
Daamɔ sanemuji lɛ asui anɔ kɛtsɔɔ nɔ ni ji sanemuu oti kɛ nɔ ni ji sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa loo kuku ko mli.
Nikasemɔ he kaai
Gbalamɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli.
Kanemɔ sanemuu walɔi ni baa nɛɛ ni ojwɛŋ nɔ ni ji oti ni awieɔ he lɛ he.
Ŋmaa sanemuu oti kɛha nikanemɔ ni baa nɛɛ he.
Kɛji misusu afi 1990 lɛ, mikaiɔ nibii kpakpai ni kɔɔ TV nɔ nifeemɔi ahe lɛ.
Yɛ afi 1990 mli lɛ, minaa Akan shwɛmɔ ni ajieɔ yɛ GTV nɔ lɛ kwɛmɔ he miishɛɛ waa daa Hɔgbaa gbɛkɛ.
Misumɔɔ bɔ ni Adadzewa jieɔ egbɛfaŋ nifeemɔ lɛ akpo eha lɛ waa - miyɛ nɔmimaa akɛ mɔ fɛɛ ni kwɛ yɛ nakai beaŋ lɛ hu sumɔɔ enifeemɔ lɛ.
Adadzewa nitsumɔi lɛ yɛ miishɛɛ waa ni ekɛ nifeemɔ babaoo fata shwɛmɔ lɛ he.
Mikaiɔ nifeemɔi kɛ nibii fɛɛ ni Adadzewa kɛ mɛi krokomɛi ni fata he lɛ jie lɛ kpo lɛ.
Ehaa miyeɔ hejaramɔ akɛ Akan shwɛmɔ lɛ he ehiaaa dɔŋŋ, shi eji TV nɔ nifeemɔi ni Ghanabii babaoo sumɔɔ waa yɛ nakai beiaŋ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni oŋma no akɛ sanemuu oti lɛ.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Kaai Wiemɔ kɛha nikasemɔ he ŋaagbɛi
Klasi muu sanegbaa Agba sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ he sane.
Kanemɔ nɔ ko yɛ otii ɛɛɛ ahe;
nifeemɔ kpakpa, tɛkinɔlɔji, tsofa, gbɛfaa, kɛ nii kɛ nii.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti WalɔMlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti ni yɔɔ nikanemɔ lɛ mli lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti walɔi ni nikanemɔ lɛ mli lɛ.
Baatsɔɔ yɛ klasi muu lɛ hiɛ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ atsɔmɔ klasi lɛ teŋ kpitiokpitio ni ebɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko kɛ amɛhe miiwo nifeemɔ lɛ mli ni eye ebua amɛ yɛ beiaŋ ni amɛhe efi amɛ.
Kanemɔ nɔ ni baa nɛɛ ni otsɔɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ.
(mii 4) Shikwɛɛ wiemɔ nɔyaa ji shishijee sukuu otii lɛ ateŋ ekome ni he hia jogbaŋŋ.
Bei pii lɛ, eshishinumɔ lɛ yɛ mlɛo: akɛ naawiemɔ tsuuu nii akɛ nikasemɔ gbenei krokomɛi amli yɛ sukuu mli, eji nɔ kome ko hu ni he hia kɛha hewoloŋ, jwɛŋmɔŋ kɛ akutsoŋ dalɛ hu mli.
Bei pii lɛ, gbekɛ ni eye afii ejwɛ naa tseɔ yɛ emaŋwiemɔ mli ni enuɔ shishi wiemɔ lɛ shishi ni ekɛtsuɔ nii.
Etsɔɔ nɔ kɛnaa saji kɛtsɔ sanebimɔ nɔ.
Enyɛɔ egbalaa, etaa adesa, etiɔ wiemɔ mli loo ebiɔ he sane, etsɔɔ kɛji eheɔ oti ko ewoɔ mli loo eheee ewooo mli.
Etsɔɔɔ nɔ kɛgbaa esusumɔi ahe sane shi etsɔɔ nɔ kɛnuɔ nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ lɛ shishi.
Mɛni osusuɔ akɛ eji yitso ni yɔɔ kuku ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ mli lɛ?
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi etɛ komɛi anɔ ni sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ yeɔ buaa sanemuu oti lɛ yɛ kuku mli.
Ŋmaa kuku yɛ oti nɛɛ he;
‘sukuyaa ji samfee ni gbeleɔ shweremɔ agbo lɛ’ ni otsɔɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ.
Dikimɔ sanemuu oti lɛ kokloo ni ofo sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ ashishi.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ OTSI 6 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli lɛ ahara otii yɛ niŋmaai amli Kaimɔ Afi1 Otsi 3
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU OTI K Ɛ SANEMUU OTI WALƆ
Susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sanemuu mli mligbalamɔ: Susumɔ oti ji oti ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ.
Eji susumɔ ni he hiaa fe fɛɛ yɛ yitso lɛ he.
Dani obaanyɛ otsɔɔ susumɔ oti lɛ, bi ohe saji nɛɛ: Mɛni awie kɛkɔ mɔ lɛ, nɔ lɛ, loo susumɔ (yitso lɛ) he lɛ?
Anaa susumɔ oti lɛ yɛ hei srɔtoi yɛ kuku lɛ mli.
Bei pii lɛ, susumɔ oti lɛ yɛ sanemuu lɛ mli, ni eji klɛŋklɛŋ sanemuu lɛ.
Niŋmalɔ lɛ kɛ kukuji ni eshwɛ lɛ piaa susumɔ oti lɛ.
Sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yoomɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli: Daamɔ sanemuji lɛ asui anɔ kɛtsɔɔ nɔ ni ji sanemuu oti kɛ nɔ ni ji sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa loo kuku ko mli.
Nyɛkanea ni nyɛgbaa nɔ ni akase yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ he lɛ ni nyɛkɛkaia nyɛhe.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ komɛi ni akwɛɔ kɛji aatsɔɔ sanemuu oti yɛ niŋmaa ko mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ komɛi ni akwɛɔ kɛji aatsɔɔ sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa ko mli.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Kaa
Kɛ kaselɔi atsɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi lɛ amli.
Tswiamɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mligbalamɔ lɛ mli ekoŋŋ.
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ oti ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko mli lɛ shishi.
Kuu ni afutu mɛi ni bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ kɛ mɛi ni ebɔɔɔ mɔdɛŋ tsɔ lɛ:
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti WalɔMlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni odamɔ onilee yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ nɔ kɛpɛi mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ oti lɛ, sanemuu oti lɛ kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ.
Nɔ ni akɛyoɔ gbekɛ ko akɛ gbekɛ kpakpa ji egbɛnaa nitsumɔ.
Gbekɛ fɛɛ ni tsuɔ egbɛnaa nii lɛ atsɛɔ lɛ gbekɛ kpakpa.
Gbekɛ fɛɛ gbekɛ yɛ nitsumɔi komɛi yɛ shia, akutso mli ni ey ɔɔ kɛ sukuu.
Y ɛ shia mli l ɛ gbekɛ kpakpa gb ɛnaa nitsum ɔi lɛ ekomɛi ji kɛji ote shi leebi lɛ osaa osaa ni oka nɔ lɛ dani ojeɔ kpo, Kɛji oje kpo lɛ ŋa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ, onukpa jio, otipɛŋfonyo jio.
Agbɛna ogbɛnaa ji owa ni asaa shia lɛ tamɔ tsu mli bɛɛmɔ, nibii ahetsuumɔ kɛ agbo naa saamɔ.
Eji onitsumɔ hu akɛ kɛ atsu bo lɛ oya.
Yɛ akutso lɛ mli hu eji ogbɛnaa akɛ okɛ woo aha mɔ fɛɛ mɔ titri lɛ mɛi ni ye bo onukpa.
Esa ni ole bɔ ni akɛɔ ofainɛ kɛ oyiwaladɔŋŋ.
Klasi lɛ agba he sane.
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni otsɔɔ susumɔ oti ni yɔɔ mli.
(mii 2) Yɛ yinɔsaji amli lɛ, nibii babaoo ena akutseiaŋbii akui ni yɔɔ Ghana lɛ lalai anɔ hewalɛ.
Wiemɔ, shihilɛ, kusumii srɔtoi, maŋkuramɔ, kɛ gbɔmɛi ayibɔ ni yaa hiɛ lɛ ena lalai lɛ anɔ hewalɛ, ni enɛɛmɛi fɛɛ kɛ amɛhe efutu.
Akutseiaŋbii akuu fɛɛ akutso jɛ maŋ lɛ nɔ heko srɔto.
Enɛ tsɔɔ akɛ mɛi ni jɛ akutsei srɔtoi lɛ amli lɛ yeɔ niyenii srɔtoi, amɛkɛ kɔɔyɔŋ tsakemɔi srɔtoi kpeɔ, ni amɛkɛ gbɔmɛi ni jɛ akutsei srɔtoi amli lɛ kpeɔ.
Kuu fɛɛ kuu faa kɛyaa hei srɔtoi fe ekrokomɛi, ni no hewɔ lɛ gbɔmɛi kɛ shihilɛi srɔtoi na eko fɛɛ eko nɔ hewalɛ.
Agbɛnɛ, jeee akɛ akutso fɛɛ akutso tsuuu nii yɛ nɔyeli kome shishi, ni no hu ha amɛna lalai ahe gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ nɔ hewalɛ waa.
Enɛɛmɛi kɛ nibii krokomɛi eha lalai ahe kusumii ni yɔɔ Ghana lɛ anɔ hewalɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni ona susumɔ otii lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni sanemɔuu oti walɔi lɛ yeɔ buaa susumɔ oti ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko mli lɛ.
Kaa Ni Afeɔ Yɛ Nikasemɔ Lɛ Teŋ Lɛ Jebɔ Mlijaa A: Sanebimɔi ni aharaa yɛ hetooi ateŋ = Sanebimɔi 20 Mlijaa B: Nikanemɔ – Sanebimɔ 1 Mlijaa C: Niŋmaa he sanebimɔ – 1 Question Bɔ ni afeɔ mlijaa A kɛ B he sanebimɔi lɛ Otsi Oti he ni asusuɔ lɛ Sanebimɔ henɔi DoK Ŋɛlɛi Fɛɛ 1 2 3 4 Sanebimɔi ni sa yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Mlijaa 3 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ wie sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ahe.
Yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, wɔkase nɔ ni sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ ji, amɛsui kɛ bɔ ni anaa amɛ yɛ nikanemɔ ko mli kɛ bɔ ni aŋmaa amɛ hu.
Wɔkɛ nilee ni wɔna lɛ tsu nii yɛ kukuji lɛ amli kɛna sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ.
Aakpa gbɛ akɛ kɛji kaselɔi tsɔ mlijaa nɛɛ nikasemɔ nɛɛ mli kɛho lɛ, amɛbaajie nilee kɛ shishinumɔ kpo yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ahe kɛna susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ niŋmaai amli.
Amɛhe baakpɔ yɛ niŋmaa, wiemɔ, nikanemɔ kɛ yiŋkaa mli.
Sanemuu oti lɛ ji
Sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ waa kɛnaa sanemuu oti lɛ yɛ gbɛi nɛɛ anɔ:
Otii ni fiɔ sɛɛ
Tsakpaa baa sanemuji lɛ ateŋ
Otii ni piaa sanemuji lɛ asɛɛ
Ehaa niŋmaa lɛ feɔ faŋŋ
Nibii ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ ateŋ etɛ - mii 3 Nibii ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ - mii 2 Nibii ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ ateŋ ekome - mi 1
(Supporting sentences) Sanemuu oti lɛ kpojiemɔ - mi 1 Sanemuu oti walɔ kpojiemɔ - mii 3 Abaanyɛ aha mii kɛyashi 3 yɛ niŋmaa jebɔ, niŋmaa mla kɛ wiemɔkulibii hee ni akɛtsu nii lɛ.
OTI: WIEMƆ KƐ ENITSUMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Ga Niŋma He Mlai Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo he nilee lɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie shishinunmɔ kpo yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ mli.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, abaatsɔɔ kaselɔi wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ.
Klɛŋklɛŋ lɛ, amɛbaakase wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi.
Abaagbele wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ ekomɛi ahiɛ atsɔɔ amɛ dani abaatsɔɔ amɛ nɔ ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ.
Nilee nɛɛ baaye ebua kaselɔi kɛyɔse wiemɔkulibii hee oya ni amɛtsɔɔ wiemɔ mli ni amɛjɛ.
Otsi 7: Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he)
Otsi 8: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee NITSƆƆMƆ ŊAAGBƐ NƆDOOMƆ Aakpa akɛ tsɔɔkɔi kɛ nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa kaselɔi muɔ naagbai anaa, kuuŋ nitsumɔ kɛ klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ atsu nii kɛtsɔɔ yitso nɛɛ.
Nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ kroko ni aakpa gbɛ akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii ji akɛ amɛbaama nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ lɛ nɔ mi.
Biɛ lɛ, esa akɛ aŋmɛ kaselɔi agbɛ kɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔi yɛ gbɛ kpakpa nɔ yɛ nɔ ni amɛnanemɛi ahetoo loo susumɔi ahe.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ anyiɛ kaselɔi ahiɛ kɛgba wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ he sane.
Esa akɛ kaselɔi agba wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo henɔi lɛ ahe sane ni amɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ Ga mli.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ baanyɛ kaselɔi lɛ ni ekadi bɔ ni wiemɔkulibii lɛ yaa lɛ lɛ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Kaselɔi lɛ baanyɛ amɛkɛ amɛjwɛŋmɔi asɔɔ wiemɔkulibii he ni shishinumɔ yɛ he ni amɛdiɛŋtsɛ amɛkwɛ mli.
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ otsii 7 kɛ 8.
Eko fɛɛ eko hiɛ ekaa otii ŋɛlɛi kɛ ehe sanebimɔi.
Otsi 7 kaa oti kɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ ni biɔ akɛ kaselɔi ajie shishinumɔ kpo yɛ nɔ ni amɛkase lɛ he ni amɛkɛha sanebimɔi lɛ ahetoo.
Otsi 8 kaa lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 4 nɔ ni akpaa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi ajie yiŋkaa hesalɛ kɛ naagba naamɔmɔ hesalɛ kpo kɛ nu naagbai anaa.
Mlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi Gbɛkpamɔi DOK ŋɛlɛ Kaa Ŋaagbɛ/ henɔ Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) 3 Tsuŋ nitsumɔ Kɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee 4 Pɔtufolio
Mlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi OTSI 7 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) Kaimɔ Afi 2 Otsi 5: Akwɛ afisi nitsumɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.
NIKASEMƆ OTI: WIEMƆKULIBII ASƆƆ GBƐJIANƆTOOI Mɛni ji wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjiaɔtooi: Eji ŋaagbɛ ni atsɔɔ kɛnaa wiemɔkulibii hee kwraa kɛbaa wiemɔ ko mli loo akɛtsɔmɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni yɛ momo loo bɔ ni akɛ wiemɔkulibii ekomɛi tsaraa ekomɛi anɔ kɛnaa wiemɔkulibii hee ni shishinumɔ yɛ mli.
Afisifeemɔ: Akɛ afisi tsaa wiemɔkulibii nɔ kɛnaa wiemɔkulibii hee.
Hiɛ afisi ji afisii ni akɛtsaa wiemɔkulitso lɛ yɛ hiɛ gbɛ.
Nɛkɛ afisi henɔ nɛɛ baa dani wiemɔkulitso lɛ baa.
Akɛ sɛɛ afisi tsaa wiemɔkulitso lɛ sɛɛ gbɛ.
Akɛ teŋ afisi woɔ wiemɔkulitso lɛ teŋ ni akɛ awoteŋ afisi bɔleɔ wiemɔkulitso lɛ.
Tsɔɔlɔ (tsɔɔ + lɔ)
Gbekɛbii (gbekɛ + bii)
Wiemɔnta : Wiemɔkulibii enyɔ loo etɛ ni akɛtsaa kɛfeɔ wiemɔkuli hee.
Wiemɔnta (wiemɔ + nta), gbɛiwiemɔ (gbɛi + wiemɔ), lamlu (la + mlu)
Afoatsa : Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛnaa wiemɔkulibii he kɛtsɔ wiemɔkulibii fagbɛ ni akɛtsaraa eko nɔ.
Afɔlɔnɔ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfoɔ wiemɔkul nɔ kuku kɛnaa wiemɔkuli hee.
Nɔtoonɔtoo : Yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ henɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, atiɔ wiemɔkulibii muu lɛ loo fa mli.
Be komɛi lɛ, tsakemɔ fioo ko baa wiemɔkulibii hee ni asɔ lɛ mli.
Wiemɔkulibii ni ama: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni amaa wiemɔkulibii kɛjɛɔ wiemɔ krokomɛi amli kɛbaa Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Wiem ɔkulibii ni asɔ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛsɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee kɛhaa nibii hee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Yɛ henɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, anaa wiemɔkulibii he kwraa loo akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni yɛ momo ni akɛtsuuu ni lɛ ha wiemɔkulibii ko.
Bei pii lɛ, asɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee nɛɛ kɛtsimɔ wiemɔkulibii afɔji komɛi ni bɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ Nɔkwɛmɔnii
Sekpe, Maŋ ko ta maŋ ko nɔ, Tsale wɔte, Kwashiorkor Wiemɔkulii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ahe sɛɛnamɔi Wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ahe hia jogbaŋŋ beiaŋ ni wiemɔi tsakeɔ lɛ.
Amɛhaa wielɔi sɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee kɛtsɔɔ nibii, tɛkinɔlɔji kɛ susumɔi komɛi ashishi ni haa wiemɔ lɛ naa wiemɔkulibii babaoo.
Wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ ashishinumɔ lɛ baaha onyɛ onya owiemɔ lɛ shikamɔ kɛ ejebɔ lɛ he.
Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi mli.
Haramɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ Ga mli ni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ ahe sɛɛnamɔi.
Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Sanebimɔi
Nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa amuɔ naagbai anaa: Klasi muu fɛɛ
Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he)
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii kɛ fi sɛɛ
Baawie ohetoo lɛ he.
Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni baa nɛɛ amli (mii 8)
Wiemɔkulibii ni ama
Wiemɔkulibii ni asɔ
Kɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii enumɔ agbala wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli (mii 5)
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo he hiaa yɛ Ga wiemɔ mli.
Daamɔ ohetoo yɛ sanebimɔi 2 kɛ 3 lɛ nɔ ni okɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo yɛ Ga wiemɔ mli lɛ ato wiemɔ krokomɛi ahe.
Mlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi OTSI 8 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee Kaimɔ Afi 2 Otsi 5: Akwɛ afisi nitsumɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.
NIKASEMƆ OTI: WIEMƆKULIBII HEE ASƆƆ Kanemɔ otsi 7 nikasemɔ lɛ mli Nikasemɔ he Kaai
Kɛ nilee ni ona kɛjɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ asɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee.
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni osɔ lɛ afee sanemuji ni shishinumɔ yɛ he.
Baatsɔɔ yɛ klasi lɛ hiɛ ni awie he.
Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ
Wiemɔ nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wie lɛ he.
Jwɛŋmɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ahe.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ: Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi amli lɛ asɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee.
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii hee ni osɔɔ lɛ atsɔɔ klasi lɛ ni awie he.
Tsɔɔlɔ aha wiemɔkulibii ni eha kaselɔi kɛ nilee ni amɛna kɛjɛ asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo mli lɛ atsɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni yɔɔ amɛwiemɔ lɛ mli.
Kaa Oti: DoK Ŋɛlɛ 4 – Pɔtufolio Feemɔ liŋwistiki pɔtufolio ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ewa kɛna wiemɔkulibii hee kɛba Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Mlijaa 4 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi amli.
Atsɔɔ kaselɔi wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi fɛɛ ni ayɔɔ lɛ ni atsɔɔ amɛ emli nɔ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ Ga mli.
Enɛ wa kaselɔi kɛna wiemɔkulibii ni ejɛɛɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Aakpa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔ baanu nikasemɔ lɛ shishi ni amɛha wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko he nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ Ga mli.
OTSI 8 Bɔ ni abaadiki sane ko mlikwɛmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli I.
Gbɛi ni ashimɔ yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ mli ni abaanyɛ atsu he nii wɔsɛɛ lɛ yopomɔ
OTI: WIEM KƐ ENSITSUMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Wiemɔ Mlai Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Apɛi sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ shishinumɔ yɛ sanemuji henɔi lɛ amli.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, abaatsɔɔ kaselɔi sanemuu jebɔ yɛ Ga mli.
Amɛbaapɛi sanemuu lɛ mli ni amɛtsɔɔ nibii ni yɔɔ mli kɛ sanemuu ni amɛfee lɛ henɔ.
Nilee nɛɛ baawa kaselɔi kɛhara sanemuji ni ja yɛ amɛdaadaa sanegbaa kɛ niŋmaai amli.
Otsi 9: Gbalamɔ nibii ni feɔ sanemuu lɛ mli (tamɔ, sanekuku kɛ sanefa)
Otsi 10: Gbalamɔ sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli.
Otsi 11: Adamɔ sanemuu lɛ nitsumɔi anɔ kɛpɛi sanemuu mli.
NITSƆƆMƆ ŊAAGBƐ NƆDOOMƆ Aakpa tsɔɔlɔi agbɛ akɛ amɛkɛ nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa kaselɔi haa naajiemɔ kɛhaa naagbai kɛ klasmuu fɛɛ nitsumɔ atsɔɔ yitso nɛɛ.
Kaselɔi yɛ kuui amli aha sanemuji, amɛpɛi sanemuji lɛ amli ni amɛtsɔɔ nibii ni feɔ sanemuu lɛ.
Kuui lɛ baabatsɔɔ klasi lɛ.
Kaselɔi lɛ baaha sanemuji ekomeiekomei ni amɛjara mli.
Kaselɔi lɛ ekomeiekomei hu baabatsɔɔ klasi lɛ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ awaje kaselɔi ni amɛhe amɛnanemɛi lɛ amligbalamɔi lɛ awo mli ni amɛbibii saji yɛ hei ni amɛnuuu shishi.
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ otsii 9, 10 kɛ 11.
Otsi 9 kaa otsi lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ, otsi 10 kaa lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 4 nɔ ni otsi 11 he kaa lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ.
Nɛkɛ DoK ŋɛlɛi lɛ akpaa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi ajie yiŋkaa hesalɛ kɛ naagba naamɔmɔ hesalɛ kpo kɛ nu naagbai anaa.
Gbɛkpamɔi DOK ŋɛlɛ Kaa ŋaagbɛ/ henɔ Gbalamɔ nibii ni feɔ sanemuu mli (Tamɔ, sanekuku, kɛ sanefa) 2 Tsuŋ nitsumɔ Gbalamɔ sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli 4 Tsuŋ nitsumɔ/ Adamɔ sanemuu nitsumɔi lɛ anɔ kɛpɛi sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli.
Mlijaa 5: Sanemuu OTSI 9 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Gbalamɔ nibii ni feɔ sanemuu mli (Tamɔ, sanekuku, kɛ sanefa) Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 10: Daamɔ mlai ni akɛŋmaa gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛi lɛ anɔ kɛha sanemuji.
Afi 2 Otsi 6: Kɛ tsalɔi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ atsu nii kɛha sanemuji nɛɛ.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU KƐ NIBII NI YƆƆ MLI Sanemuu: Sanemuu ji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ kɛyaa ni akɛtsa ni shishinumɔ yɛ he ni akɛjieɔ oti ko he loo jieɔ shɛɛwiemɔ ko kpo.
Sanemuu jieɔ susumɔ ni eye emuu kpo
Sanemuu nyiɛɔ jebɔ pɔtɛɛ ko nɔ, tamɔ feelɔ-feemɔwiemɔ-helɔ (SVO)
Sanemuu kɛ niŋmaa mli okadii lɛ tsuɔ nii.
Bei pii lɛ akɛ omashi (.), sanebimɔ okadi (?), loo boo okadi (!) lɛ muɔ sanemuu naa.
Kɛɛmɔ sanemuu: Eji sanemuu henɔ ko ni kɛɔ sane loo oti ko.
Bei pii lɛ, akɛ omashi okadi lɛ muɔ nɛkɛ sanemuu henɔ nɛɛ naa.
nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ji Adote tee sukuu.
Sanemuu ni biɔ sane: Eji sanemuu henɔ ko ni biɔ sane.
Akɛ sanebimɔ okadi lɛ muɔ sanemuu henɔ nɛɛ naa.
nɔkwɛmɔnii ji namɔ ji ofɔlɔi?
Famɔ Sanemuu: Eji sanemuu henɔ ko ni akɛfaa mɔ.
Ŋa shinaa lɛ.
Boo sanemuu: Eji sanemuu henɔ ni jieɔ henumɔ kple ko kpo.
Bei pii lɛ, akɛ boo okadi lɛ muɔ sanemuu henɔ nɛɛ naa.
Nibii ni yɔɔ sanemuu mli: Anaa nibii babaoo yɛ sanemuu mli ni waa kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ lɛ kpo lɛ.
Nibii nɛɛ ekomɛi ji:
Feelɔ: Sanemuu lɛ feelɔ ji gbɛiwiemɔ loo najiaŋgbɛi loo gbɛiwiemɔ kuku ni sanemuu lɛ wieɔ he lɛ.
Eji mɔ ko, he, nɔ ko, loo yiŋtoo ko ni feɔ nɔ he ni awieɔ he yɛ sanemuu lɛ mli lɛ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ji: Oblanuu kakadaŋŋ lɛ ehe tsɔne hee.
Feemɔwiemɔ: Feemɔwiemɔ lɛ ji nifeemɔ ni yaa nɔ yɛ sanemuu lɛ mli lɛ.
Eji wiemɔkuli ni tsɔɔ wɔ nɔ ni feelɔ lɛ feɔ loo nɔ ni ninaa feelɔ lɛ.
Feemɔwiemɔ ni yɔɔ sanemuu ni yɔɔ (a) lɛ mli ji he.
Helɔ/nɔnalɔ: Helɔ loo nɔnalɔ ji gbɛiwiemɔ loo najiaŋgbɛi ni naa nɔ lɛ loo nifeemɔ ni yɔɔ sanemuu lɛ mli lɛ yaa shiɔ lɛ lɛ.
Helɔ/nɔnalɔ tuuntu kɛ helɔ/
Mlijaa 5: SanemuuMlijaa 5: Sanemuu nɔnalɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ ninaaa lɛ tuuntu.
Mɛni feelɔ ni yɔɔ (a) lɛ mli lɛ he lɛ?
… tsɔne heeFeelɔ tuuntu lɛ ji mɔ ni nifeemɔ ni yɔɔ feemɔwiemɔ lɛ ninaa lɛ tuunutu lɛ (tamɔ, Ehe tsɔne.).
Nifeemɔ ni yɔɔ feemɔwiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ ninaaa helɔ/nɔnalɔ henɔ kroko lɛ tuuntu.
Bei pii lɛ akɛ gbɛhegbɛi ni yoɔ helɔ/nɔnalɔ henɔ nɛɛ.
(Tamɔ, Kojo he atade ha mi).
Biɛ lɛ, atade lɛ ji helɔ ni naaa nifeemɔ lɛ he sɛɛ tuuntu lɛ.
Mligbalalɔi: Mligbalalɔi ji wiemɔkulibii ni wieɔ feelɔ, feemɔwiemɔ loo helɔ lɛ he.
Enyɛɔ egbalaa wiemɔkulibii krokomɛi ashishinumɔ yɛ sanemuu lɛ mli.
Mɛni wolo henɔ ohe lɛ?
Nɔ ni sɛɛ asrasu lɛ…
Yigbelɔ: Yigbelɔ ji wiemɔkuli loo sanekuku ni kɛ gbeɔ shishinumɔ ni sanemuu mli lɛ yi.
Ebaanyɛ efee gbɛiwiemɔ, sutsɔɔlɔ loo sanekuku ni wieɔ feelɔ loo helɔ lɛ he.
Nkwɛmɔnɔ, Olokwe he yɛ fɛo.
Enɛ ji feelɔ yigbelɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Tsɛrɛmɔ sanemuji nii baa nɛɛ amli.
Kwei he atade diŋ ko Feelɔ: Kwei Feemɔwiemɔ: he Helɔ: atade Sutsɔɔlɔ: diŋ Kɛji onuɔ nibii ni yɔɔ sanemuu mli lɛ ashishi lɛ, obaanyɛ afee sanemuu kpakpa ni shishinumɔ yɛ he.
Sanekuku: Eji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ loo etɛ yɛ sanemuu mli ni bɛ feemɔwiemɔ.
Esa akɛ ale akɛ, sanekuku bɛ shishinumɔ.
Sanekuku nyɛɔ etsuɔ nii akɛ gbɛiwiemɔi, sutsɔɔlɔi loo mligbalalɔi ni wkɛ wiemɔi komɛi fataa sanemuu lɛ he.
Gbɛiwiemɔ sanekuku: Etsuɔ nii akɛ gbɛiwiemɔ yɛ sanemuu ko mli.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji: nuu, googa ko, gbee agbo lɛ.
Feemɔwiemɔ sanekuku: Etsuɔ nii akɛ feemɔwiemɔ yɛ sanemuu mli: Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji: ba, baaye, baabajo.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji: agbo, miishɛɛ tɔ, eyɛŋ futaa.
Mligbalamɔ sanekuku: Egbalaa feemɔwiemɔ, sutsɔɔlɔ loo mligbalamɔ kroko mli.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi ji: leebi maŋkpa, nyɛ gbɛkɛ, ŋmɛji etɛ,
Gbɛhegbɛi sanekuku: Gbɛhegbɛi ji wiemɔ oti ni yɔɔ mli ni egbalaa gbɛiwiemɔ loo feemɔwiemɔ mli.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi ji: okplɔ lɛ shishi, kpaa lɛ nɔ.
Sanefa Eji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ loo etɛ ni hiɛ feelɔ kɛ helɔ.
Eji sanemuu shishijee henɔ ni jieɔ susumɔ ni eye emuu loo eyeko emuu kpo.
Sanefa Oti: Eji sanefa ni hiɛ feelɔ kɛ helɔ ni jieɔ susumɔ ni eye emuu kpo.
Ekome enyɛɔ edamɔɔ shi kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ ko kpo.
Miyahe omɔ kɛ shitɔ
Sanefa kpasalɔ: Eji sanefa ni hiɛ feelɔ kɛ feemɔwiemɔ shi ejieee shishinumɔ ni eye emuu kpo.
Ekome enyɛɛɛ edamɔ shi yɛ kɛfee sanemuu.
Akɛ hɔmɔ miiye lɛ hewɔ lɛ (ehiaa sanefa oti dani ebaaye emuu)
Ejaakɛ ebɛ hewalɛ (ehiaa sanefa oti dani ebaaye emuu) Sanefafai ahe nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ Sanemuu mli
Sanemuu ganee: Ehiɛ sanefa oti kome Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Misumɔɔ omɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Sanemuu nta: Eji sanefa otii enyɔ loo etɛ kɛyaa ni akɛ tsalɔ etsa amɛ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Misumɔɔ omɔ jogbaŋŋ shi minyɛmi yoo lɛ sumɔɔ banku kɛ shitɔ.
Sanemuu haŋtsii: Henɔ nɛɛ hiɛ sanefa oti kome kɛ sanefa kpasalɔi ekome loo enyɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Mihaye omɔ ejaakɛ misumɔɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Sanefa he sɛɛnamɔi Sanefafai waa:
Kɛjieɔ susumɔ ni eye emuu kpo.
Ehaa akɛ sanemuu henɔi srɔtoi ŋmɛɔ jara.
Ehaa niŋmalɔi kɛ wielɔi kɛ gbee kɛ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ tsuɔ nii yɛ niŋmaa kɛ wiemɔ mli.
Sanefa he shishinumɔ lɛ waa ni onaa hesalɛ yɛ niŋmaa kɛ wiemɔ mli ni.
Kɛji onu sanefafai ashishi lɛ ehaa ohe saa yɛ oniŋmaa kɛ wiemɔ mli ni onyɛɔ ogbaa sane jogbaŋŋ.
Gbalamɔ sanemuu he sui lɛ amli.
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ sanemuu ganee, sanemuu nta, kɛ sanemuu haŋtsii ashishi.
Jiemɔ osusumɔi lɛ akpo otsɔɔ klasi lɛ ni agba he sane.
Mlijaa 5: SanemuuMlijaa 5: Sanemuu Nikasemɔ He Kaa
Nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa anaa naajiemɔ kɛha sanebimɔi: Klasi muu fɛɛ nitsumɔ Gbalamɔ sanemuu mli
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ atsu nii yɛ kuu mli kɛgbala sanemuu kɛ ehe nibii lɛ amli ni amɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii.
Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ afee ekome kɛgbala sanekuku kɛ sanefa kɛ amɛhenɔi lɛ amli.
Gbalamɔ nɔ ni ji sanemuu mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔtoi enumɔ ni yɔɔ sanekuku kɛ sanefa teŋ.
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii ni ja yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Tsɛrɛmɔ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ amli.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ye omɔ
Abifao lɛ miifo
Bɔɔlu lɛ ka okpɔlɔ lɛ shishi
Atade diŋ lɛ egbee shi.
Mlijaa 5: Sanemuu OTSI 10 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Gbalamɔ sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 10: Daamɔ mlai ni akɛŋmaa gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛi lɛ anɔ kɛha sanemuji.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU HEN ƆI
Sanemuu haŋtsii Sanemuu: Sanemuu ji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ kɛyaa ni akɛtsa ni shishinumɔ yɛ he ni akɛjieɔ oti ko he loo jieɔ shɛɛwiemɔ ko kpo.
Nikasemɔ he kaa
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu henɔ ni akɛtsu nii yɛ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ amli
Hulu lɛ nyɔɔ shi yɛ anai.
Ghanamaŋ lɛ mli yɛ fɛo hewɔ lɛ gbɔ babaoo basaraa wɔ daa afi.
Feemɔ sanemuji yɛ henɔi nɛɛ anɔ:
Sanemuu ganee ni wieɔ Ghana niyenii he
Sanemuu nta ni wieɔ afiyeli he
Sanemuu haŋtsii ni wieɔ sukuuyaa he sɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Pɛimɔ sanemuji nɛɛ amli ni otsɔɔ ejebɔ
Nugbɔ miinɛ moŋ nɛ shi okwaafoi lɛ miitsu nii lolo
Saneyelihe wulu lɛ yɛ Ga
Gamɛi lɛ kɛ amɛfiyeli lɛ shwɛɛɛ kwraa.
Feemɔ sanemuu ganee nɛɛ sanemuu haŋtsii
Ghana maŋ lɛ mli yɛ fɛo naakpa.
Kaselɔi lɛ miikase nii waa kɛha amɛkaa lɛ.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Kaa Klasi muu nitsumɔ
Klasi lɛ agba sanemuu henɔi lɛ ahe sane (ganee, nta kɛ haŋtsii)
Kaselɔi ekomeiekomei aha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ sanemuu henɔ lɛ anɔ (ganee, nta kɛ haŋtsii) Kaselɔi atsɔɔ sanemuu henɔi ni akɛtsu nii yɛ niŋmaa ko mli.
Kaa Oti: Dok Ŋɛlɛ 4 – Tsuŋ Nitsumɔ Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni otsɔɔ sanemuu henɔi srɔtoi ni ni ŋmalɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii lɛ (ganee, nta kɛ haŋtsii).
Kaselɔi babaoo kɛ intanɛti lɛ tsuɔ nii daa gbi.
Amɛkɛkaseɔ nii ni amɛtsɔɔ nɔ ni amɛkɛ amɛnanemɛi hu gbaa sane.
Woloŋlelɔi komɛi heɔ yeɔ amɛ gbekɛbii babaoo kɛ amɛhiɛ fɔɔ intanɛti nɛɛ nɔ tsɔ.
Amɛsusuɔ akɛ kaselɔi ni feɔ amɛshigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ yɛ woji kɛ niŋmaai amli lɛ shwereɔ fe ekrokomɛi lɛ ejaakɛ nikanemɔ hiaa yiŋkaa jogbaŋŋ.
Ani okɛ amɛ kpaa gbee?
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni sanemuu henɔi nɛɛ waa kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ ni yɔɔ nikanemɔ lɛ mli lɛ kpo.
(mii 6) Kɛ nilee ni ona lɛ afee sanemuji etɛtɛɛtɛ yɛ sanemuu henɔi nɛɛ amli.
Mlijaa 5: Sanemuu OTSI 11 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Daamɔ sanemuu lɛ nitsumɔi anɔ kɛpɛi sanemuu mli Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 10: Daamɔ mlai ni akɛŋmaa gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛi lɛ anɔ kɛha sanemuji.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU HENƆI
Sanemuu ni biɔ sane
Sanemuu ni akɛfaa Sanemuu: Sanemuu ji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ kɛyaa ni akɛtsa ni shishinumɔ yɛ he ni akɛjieɔ oti ko he loo jieɔ shɛɛwiemɔ ko kpo.
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu henɔ (kɛɛmɔ, sanebimɔ kɛ famɔ) ni akɛtsu nii yɛ sanemuji nɛɛ amli lɛ:
Mɛni maŋ nɔ ojɛ?
Nugbɔ miinɛ yɛ Osu.
Kɛɛmɔ sanemuu ni wieɔ kusum he.
Sanebimɔ sanemuu yɛ Ghana blema saji ahe.
Famɔ sanemuu yɛ mɔ wamɔ he.
Tsakemɔ sanemuji ni baa ɛɛ kɛya sanemuu henɔ ni yɔɔ akrabatsa lɛ mli lɛ:
Mɛni ji Ghana maŋ lɛ hiɛnyiɛlɔ lɛ gbɛi?
Jaramɔ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ owo ehenɔi lɛ amli ni bo diɛŋtsɛ oha nɔkwɛmɔnɔ yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Shia lɛ mli ejɔ.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ:
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ yɛ kuui amli awie sanemuu nitsumɔi kɛ nibii ni yɔɔ mli lɛ ahe.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ yɛ kuu mli aha nɔkwɛmɔnii sanemuu nitsumɔi lɛ ahe.
Kaselɔi aha amɛdiɛŋtsɛ amɛnɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ henɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko he.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ sanemuji henɔi lɛ amli: kɛɛmɔ, famɔ, kɛ sanebimɔ sanemuu ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Jaramɔ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ amli owo amɛnitsumɔi lɛ ashishi ni otsɔɔ bɔ ni ejebɔ, niŋmaa mli okadii kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni okɛtsu nii yɛ mli lɛ yeɔ buaa enitsumɔ lɛ.
Kaata stoovi lɛ he.
Mlijaa 5: Sanemuu Mlijaa 5 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ wie sanemuu henɔi lɛ ahe.
Aja henɔi lɛ amli kɛwo jebɔ kɛ nitsumɔ amli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ shishinumɔ lɛ baawa kaselɔi ni amɛfee sanemuji kpakpai kɛji amɛmiiŋma nii loo amɛmiiwie.
Enɛ baaha kaselɔi anaa atse yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ejee yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli pɛ shi yɛ Blɔfo kɛ nikasemɔi krokomɛi tamɔ Arabic kɛ French.
Gbɛi ni ashimɔ yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ mli ni abaanyɛ atsu he nii wɔsɛɛ lɛ yopomɔ OTSI 10 (18 marks) Bɔ ni abaadiki sane ko mlikwɛmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli I.
Bɔ ni akɛbaatsu nii kɛ esɛɛ saji OTSI 11 I.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ
OTI: WIEMƆ KƐ NITSUMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Saneŋmaa Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he lɛ adoo/ afo sane loo niŋmaa ko nɔ kuku.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ kpo yɛ nɔdoomɔ/ kukufoo ŋmaa he.
OTI: WIEMƆ KƐ NITSUMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Sanenaajiemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi kɛ ehe mlai lɛ atsu nii kɛtsɔɔ niŋmai komɛi ashishi.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ kpo yɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he.
Esani okɔ kɛjɛ nibii ni atsɔɔ kɛjɛ Afi 1 kɛyashi 3.
Kwɛmɔ specification kɛ structure of the paper ni yɔɔ mlijaa nɛɛ naagbee lɛ nɔ kɛha yelikɛbuamɔ.
Kadimɔ kaselɔi awoji oya ni otswa mii lɛ owo SAP lɛ nɔ.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ wieɔ bɔ ni akɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii ŋaagbɛi lɛ tsuɔ nii kɛdoɔ loo foɔ niŋmai kakadaji anɔ kuku.
Akɛ kaselɔi baatsɔ ŋaagbɛi ni haa nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii yeɔ emuu lɛ mli bɔ ni afee ni amɛnyɛ amɛyɔse ni amɛna otii pɔtɛɛ kɛ otii krokomɛi ni yɔɔ niŋmai amli.
No sɛɛ lɛ, amɛkɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he nilee lɛ baadoo saji anɔ.
Akɛ kaselɔi baatsɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai amli hu.
Abaaha amɛtsɔɔ wiemɔi, sanekukuji, sanefai, sanemuji kɛ kukujiaŋ aniŋmai ashishi kɛjɛ Blɔfo wiemɔ lɛ mli kɛba Ghana shikwɛɛ wiemɔi srɔtoi ni akaseɔ lɛ amli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hiaa kaselɔi jogbaŋŋ jeee yɛ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ akaseɔ lɛ amli pɛ shi moŋ amɛbaale nikasemɔ tsakpaa ni ka Shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ kɛ nikasemɔi krokomɛi tamɔ Blɔfo kɛ ekrokomɛi lɛ ateŋ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ woɔ kaselɔi ahewalɛ ni amɛnaa nilee yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii kɛha nɔdoomɔ emuuyeli.
Eyeɔ ebuaa kaselɔi hu kɛ ŋai kɛha saneshishitsɔɔmɔ emuuyeli.
Aawo tsɔɔlɔi lɛ ahewalɛ bɔ ni afee ni amɛkɛ nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai ni haa
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kaselɔi kɛ amɛhe woɔ nikasemɔ kɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli kɛ yelikɛbuamɔ nibii srɔtoi atsu nii koni ewa nitsɔɔmɔ kɛ nikasemɔ fɛɛ.
Otsii kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ni kɔɔ mlijaa nɛɛ he nɛ:
Otsi 12: Kɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he nilee ahara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko ni abaadoo nɔ mli.
Otsi 13: Awie sanehishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ ahe.
(N.k., wiemɔkulibii asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ, Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ, kn.kn)
Otsi 14: Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mlai lɛ ahe (n.k., nilee yɛ wiemɔi ni abaatsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛya kɛ wiemɔi ni atsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi yɛ lɛ, kusum he nilee, wiemɔ mli akpɔi kn.kn) NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
Sanenaataomɔ Nikasemɔ: yɛ ŋaagbɛ nɛɛ mli lɛ, akɛ jeŋshihilɛ mli saji toɔ nikasemɔ lɛ he ni no haa kaselɔi nuɔ nibii ashishi faŋŋ fe nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ ni awieɔ nibii ahe tamɔ bɔ ni amɛji lɛ.
Ŋaagbɛi tamɔ mɔ kome nikasemɔ, ku nitsumɔ, hii kɛ yei amlijaramɔ loo amɛfutumɔ kɛ nitsumɔ kɛ klasi muu fɛɛ nifeemɔ fɛɛ hɔ sanenaataomɔ nikasemɔ nɛɛ shishi.
Ŋaagbɛii nɛɛ yeɔ ebuaa niiahesusumɔ vii, hetootamɔ kɛha saji anaamɔmɔ kɛ naatselɛ.
Ehaa kaselɔi ahegbɛ ni amɛtsuɔ loo amɛkaseɔ nii yɛ kui amli ni amɛkaseɔ hiɛnyiɛmɔ hu.
Kaselɔi baanyɛ ayɔse naagba ko hu ni amɛtao hetoo kɛ tsɔɔ shishi fitsofitso.
Kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛdamɔ nibii ni mɛi krokomɛi ewieɔ lɛ anɔ ni amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ yɛ he, kɛji amɛwieee kɛshiii lɛ, amɛbaafi wiemɔ lɛ sɛɛ kpɛŋŋ ni amɛbaawie he jogbaŋŋ.
Kaselɔi ni nikasemɔ shɛɔ shɔŋŋ lɛ, abaaha amɛ gbɛnaa krokomɛi tamɔ ku hiɛnyiɛmɔ, tipɛŋfoi anitsɔɔlɔi koni amɛye amɛbua amɛnanemɛi krokomɛi lɛ koni amɛnu nɔ ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ etsɔɔ lɛ shishi jogbaŋŋ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi leɔ kaselɔi ni yɔɔ nitsɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔi amli naagbai koni amɛtsɔɔ ŋaagbɛi anɔ kɛku susumɔi ni ejaaa fɛɛ anaa yɛ klasi lɛ mli.
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaagbɛ yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ shishi yeɔ ebuaa niiashishinumɔ kɛ niiahesusumɔ vii yɛ kaselɔi agbɛfaŋ.
Kaa kpitiokpitioo haa anaa kaselɔi ashidaamɔ kɛ amɛ nɔyaa.
Kaa ŋaagbɛ ni akɛtsuɔ nii lɛ yeɔ ebuaa ni ayɔseɔ kaselɔi aniiashishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ shikwɛɛ wiemɔ ni atsɔɔ lɛ mli.
Niŋmaa kukuji loo mɔnaa wiemɔ fata Ŋɛlɛ 2 sanebimɔ he ŋaa lɛ he, ni no nɔ atsɔɔ aleɔ bɔ ni kaselɔi wieɔ nibii ahe amɛhaa.
Yɛ Ŋɛlɛ 3 lɛ, abiɔ saji ni haa asusuɔ niibii ahe vii kɛtsɔɔ niŋmaa kukuji loo kakadaji anɔ kɛkaa kaselɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mlikwɔlɛ kɛ shishinumɔ ni amɛyɔɔ sanegbaa gbɛjianɔtoo he kɛ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔi ni esani amɛkɛtsu nii yɛ maŋ kɛ jeŋjeŋ saji amli lɛ ahe.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii koni amɛkɛna kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ, mii ni amɛnaa kɛ hetoo kɛha amɛ nikasemɔ nɔyaa.
Nɔdoomɔ kɛha kaa kɛ amɛ gbɛkpamɔ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Gbɛkpamɔ DoK Ŋɛlɛ Kaa ŋaagbɛ Akɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he nilee lɛ ahara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko ni abaado nɔ mli 2 Practice paper Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ ahe (N.k., wiemɔkulibii, shishinumɔ saneshishitsɔɔ, kn.
Kn.) 4 Sukuu nitsumɔ Awie saneshitsɔɔmɔ mlai lɛ ahe 3 Ku nitsumɔ
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ OTSI 12 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he nilee atsu nii kɛhara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko ni abaadoo nɔ mli Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 4: Akɛ nilee ni oyɔɔ yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he lɛ akane nii ni akɛtao hetoo aha jeŋshihilɛ mli naagbai Afi 2 Otsi 4: Awie nikanemɔ gbajaa gbeji lɛ ahe (nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ , nikanemɔ vii, kn.) OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: NIKANEMƆ HIƐHIƐ KƐ NIKANEMƆ VII Nikanemɔ Hiɛhiɛ: Yɛ enɛ shishi, akwɛɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli oyaoyai kɛnaa nɔ pɔtɛɛ he ni niŋmaa lɛ wieɔ, otii gbajaa kɛ niŋmaa lɛ jebɔ.
Nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa niŋmaa lɛ mli otii, ehaa anuɔ ŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo lɛ shishi ni efiteee be hu.
Nikanemɔ Vii: Yɛ enɛ shishi lɛ, akpaa niŋmaa lɛ mli kɛtaoɔ otii pɔtɛɛ komɛi tamɔ wiemɔkulibii hee, sanefafai loo sanebimɔi ahetoo kɛha shigbɛlɛmɔ emuuyeli.
Nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii hesɛɛnamɔi Nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii ŋaagbɛi lɛ ahe hiaa jogbaŋŋ kɛha niiashishinumɔ kɛ nikanemɔ.
Kɛji mɔ ko he kpɔ yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii mli lɛ, eyeɔ ebuaa enikanemɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Nikasemɔ He Nitsumɔ Kanemɔ shiɛmɔ niŋmaa ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ
Kwɛmɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli ni ohara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli.
Kpaa niŋmaa lɛ mli ni otao (i) mɔ ni to nitsumɔ lɛ shishi (ii) maji krokomɛi ni naa nitsumɔ lɛ he sɛɛ (ii) okwaafoi abɔ ni na he sɛɛ.
Doomɔ niŋmaa lɛ nɔ yɛ bo diɛŋtɛ wiemɔ mli.
Mɛi fe 3,000 ni ji okwaafoi bibii kɛ amralofoi ni yɔɔ ku ni kwɛɔ okwaayeli nɔyaa lɛ ena shika ni ana kɛjɛ Diversification Pathways yɛ Afrika kɛtsɔ Circular Agriculture Innovation (DVIA-GRI) nitsumɔ lɛ he sɛɛ.
Afii ejwɛ nitsumɔ ni European Union to shishi lɛ kwɛ gbɛ ni ni abaatsɔ ni shweremɔ aba okwaafoi anidumɔ kɛ nikpamɔ mli kɛtsɔ shikataomɔ gbɛjianɔtoo komɛi amli kɛha okwaafoi ni yɔɔ Sub-Saharan Afrika lɛ.
Mɛi ni na hegbɛ lɛ na tsɔsemɔ yɛ bɔ ni atsɔɔ ŋoo nu kɛyaa nuŋma mli, nitsumɔi srɔtoi yɛ hei ni nu yɔɔ, nidumɔ sui kɛ nidumɔi ŋaa srɔtoi, Biogas, Integrated biorefinery (mlɛ namɔ, mu kpakpa namɔ, black soldier fly technology kɛ nii kɛ nii) SLECI irrigation system kɛ Biochar
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ
Yɛ Ghana sɛɛ lɛ, aje nitsumɔ nɛɛ hu shishi yɛ South Africa, Namibia, Botswana kɛ Mozambique.
Ati nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii nikasemɔ lɛ mli ekoŋŋ
Awie nɔdoomɔ niŋmaa he
Akɛ nilee ni ana yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he lɛ ayɔse susumɔi otii ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ni adoo nɔ lɛ mli.
Kaa Oti: Nitsumɔ Wolo l Kaa ni afeɔ be ni nikasemɔ miiya nɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 2- Shia Nitsumɔ
Gbalamɔ mli ni otsɔɔ srɔto enyɔ ni yɔɔ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii teŋ.
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛii etɛ anɔ ni abaatsɔ kɛyɔse susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko mli.
Akɛ nilee ni ana yɛ susumɔ otii ahe lɛ adoo saji nɛɛ anɔ yɛ kukujiaŋ shi ekafa fe sanemuji kpaanyɔ:
Mɛni ofeɔ yɛ odekanamɔ mli?
Mɛni woɔ bo hewalɛ kɛ feɔ nakai nɔ lɛ?
Ani okane nɔ ko yɛ nibii ni mɛi feɔ yɛ amɛdekanamɔ mli?
Be ni mɔ ko naa kɛjɔɔ ehe ni etsuuu nii ji dekanamɔ be.
Yɛ nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, gbɔmɔ nɛɛ feɔ nibii ni lɛ diɛŋtsɛ esumɔɔ yɛ be ni esumɔɔ mli.
Ekɛ mɛi ni esumɔɔ yeɔ be lɛ, ni eyeɔ ebe lɛ bɔ ni lɛ diɛŋtsɛ esumɔɔ.
Mɛi komɛi hu jɔɔ amɛhe yɛ amɛdekamɔ be mli.
Enɛ yeɔ ebua amɛ kɛnaa hewalɛ hee.
Gbɔmɔtso lɛ kɛ jwɛŋmɔ naa hegbɛ kɛjɔɔ amɛhe kɛnaa hewalɛ hee ejaakɛ akpaaa gbɛ akɛ wɔbaatsu nitsumɔ ko kwraa yɛ dekanamɔ be lɛ mli.
Esa akɛ mɔ fɛɛ naa deka kɛji etsu nɔ akpaa gbɛ yɛ edɛŋ lɛ egbe naa.
Dekanamɔ be he hiaa jogbaŋŋ ejaakɛ ewaa wɔ koni wɔkatsu nii fe nine.
Wɔ dekanamɔ be lɛ haa wɔjɔɔ wɔjwɛŋmɔ he ejaakɛ wɔjoɔ tɔlɛ nitsumɔ naa foi.

Daamɔ nibii ni akɛsusuɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi lɛ ni okɛsusu Ga kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi lɛ (n.k.
gbeekpaa shikamɔ, he ni asɔ yɛ kɛ bɔ ni kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ jeɔ kpo ehaa)
Gbalamɔ vaoli kpojee mli yɛ Ga mli (n.k wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee)
Tsɔɔmɔ blawa henɔi (vaoli naagbee kɛ kɔnsonanti naagbee) ni ayɔɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.
Gbalamɔ Ga blawa jebɔ mli yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ mli.
Gbalamɔ gbee kɛ ehenɔi lɛ amli (n.k.
gbee ni yɛ ŋwɛi, gbee ni yɛ shishi, gbee ni miiya ŋwɛi kɛ gbee ni miiba shi) kɛ enitsumɔi.
Gbalamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni ayɔɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.

Jaramɔ mligbalalɔi owo amɛhenɔi lɛ ashishi.

Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ GA NIŊMAA MLAI
Daamɔ mlai ni akɛŋmaa gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛii anɔ lɛ kɛfee sanemuji.
Daamɔ mlai ni akɛŋmaa sutsɔɔlɔ nɔ lɛ kɛfee sanemuji.
Gbalamɔ Ga wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli (wiemɔnta, afɔlɔnɔ, wiemɔmaa, afisifeemɔ)
Tsɔɔmɔ niŋmaa mli okadii ahe (omashi, alɔgɔ, boo okadi, omashi alɔgɔ)
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ mli lɛ.
Daamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ anɔ ni okɛsɔ wiemɔkulibii hee.
Gbalamɔ wowɔi agbɛii amli ni otsɔɔ bɔ ni amɛbabɔ ade kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni amɛba lɛ.
Gbalamɔ kpokuafoi hetsuu nifemɔ lɛ amli (kusum ni afeɔ dani hetsuu lɛ jeɔ shishi, nbe mli ni nifeemɔ lɛ miiya nɔ kɛya nifeemɔ lɛ sɛɛ)
Kɛ Ga kpokuafoi ahetsuu nifeemɔ lɛ ato kusum krokomɛi anɔ lɛ he.
Gbalamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ni yaa nɔ yɛ gbalamlibotemɔ mli kɛ esɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Tsɔɔmɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ tsakemɔi ni eba gbalamlibotemɔ he ni naagba eba tsutsu gbalamlibotemɔ lɛ he.
Kɛ Ga weku tɔkpaai lɛ ato kusumi krokomɛi aweku tɔkpaa lɛ he.
Gbalamɔ Ga obɔade afiyelii lɛ amli ni otsɔɔ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi.
Kɛ Ga afiyeli lɛ ato kusumi krokomɛi yɛ Ghana afiyeli lɛ he.
Kɛ Ga yarafeemɔ kusum lɛ ato kusum krokomɛi yɛ Ghana ayarafeemɔ lɛ he.
Tsɔɔmɔ shikwɛɛ saneyli jebɔ lɛ he (tsɛ, weku nukpa, weku tɔkpaa yitso)
Tsɔɔmɔ shikwɛɛ saneyli jebɔ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ saneyeli yɔɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ shikwɛɛ/blema saneyeli kɛ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ saneyeli teŋ.
Gbalamɔ Ga ŋkpaiyeli jebɔ lɛ mli (gbɛgbelemɔ, wiemɔ, naamuu)
Gbalamɔ yarawoo lalai amli (enitsumɔ kɛ ehe sɛɛnamɔi)
Pɛimɔ ajenu jebɔ kɛ ehenɔi lɛ amli.
Gbalamɔ alabaaloo jebɔ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nitsumɔ lalao, tayaa lalai, shwɛmɔ lalai kɛ abifaowɔlemɔ lala 1 1
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ WOJIAŊ SAJI 1.
Gbalamɔ adesa otui lɛ amli (n.k.

Nya adesa ko he (yitso lɛ, otii, wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsi nii, wiemɔheŋɔɔlɔwolɔi, k.n.k.n)
Gbalamɔ shwɛmɔ otui lɛ amli.

Tsɔɔmɔ ni ogbala lalawiemɔ otui lɛ amli (n.k.
otii, wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu nii, kukuji, liamɔ, wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, shitiimɔ k.n.k.n.)
Nya lalawiemɔ ko he.

Abaaha sanesɛɛkɔmɔ ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii 300-350 yɛ adesa nɔ ni sanebimɔi nyɔŋma yɛ eshishi.
Aakpa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi aha fɛɛ ahetoo.
Abaaha kaselɔi sanebimɔi enyɔ ni amɛha hetoo.
Abaadiki sanebimɔ lɛ eko fɛɛ eko mii nyɔŋma.
Abaaha sane ko ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii 250 yɛ Blɔfo mli ni kaselɔi atsɔɔ shishi kɛba Ga mli.
Abaaha saneŋmaa he sanebim ɔi ejwɛ ni esa ak ɛ kaselɔi aŋma sanŋmaa ni hi ɛ wiemɔkulibii aaafee 300.
Abaahara kaa sanebim ɔi lɛ yɛ henɔi ni baa n ɛɛ ashishi.
Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, abaaha sanebimɔi etɛ yɛ jeŋ saji henɔi lɛ ashishi (adesa, shwɛmɔ kɛ lalawiemɔ) koni kaselɔi akɔ emli ekome.
Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli lɛ ajie susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ nikanemɔi amli lɛ akpo.
