Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ OTSI 13 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Agba saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ ahe sane (Nk, wiemɔkulibii ekomeiekomei asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ, Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ, kn.kn) Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 6: Agba mɔnaa sanenaajiemɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve, shishinumɔ kɛ shisitsɔɔmɔ, kn.kn.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANESHISHITSƆƆMƆ
Nɔ ni saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ji.
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sɛɛnamɔi
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ: Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛtsakeɔ niŋmaa aloo wiemɔ kɛjɛɔ wiemɔ ko mli kɛyaa wiemɔ kroko mli ni shishinumɔ, yiŋtoo krɛdɛɛ kɛ shɛɛ wiemɔ ni yɔɔ klɛŋklɛŋ wiemɔ lɛ mli tsakeee.
Saneshihitsɔɔmɔ he sɛɛnamɔi : Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ tsakpaa ko baa wiemɔi ateŋ, ehaa shishinumɔ baa jeŋ nilee he, ni ekwɛɔ ni shramɔ kɛ sanegbaa feɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Naa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kasemɔ he sɛɛnamɔ Ehaa maji kɛ amɛhe shraa: Ekɛ shishinumɔ baa mɛi ni miiwie wiemɔi srɔtoi ateŋ.
Kusum krokomɛi ahe nilee: Ehaa hegbɛ ni mɛi kɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ, saji kɛ nilee ni kɔɔ mɛi krokomɛi akusum he.
Nitsumɔ kɛ Jarayeli: Ehe hiaa jogbaŋŋ kɛha maŋsɛɛ nitsumɔ, jarayeli kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ ashɛɔ loo amajeɔ nibii.
Sukuuyaa kɛ Shigbɛlɛmɔ: Ehaa nilee kɛ sukuuyaa he nibii baa sɛɛnamɔ yɛ jeŋ.
Sharamɔ ni ka maji maji ateŋ: Ehe baa sɛɛnamɔ kɛha maŋnɔkwɛlɔi kɛ maji maji ateŋ sharamɔ kpakpa.
Ehaa anaa nibii ni yaa nɔ: Ehaa hegbɛ ni anaa nibii ni yaa nɔ, kɛ egbeleɔ nitsumɔ gbɛ kɛha mɛi fɛɛ ni yɔɔ jeŋ.
Eyeɔ ebuaa shishinumɔ kɛ henumɔ: Ehaa maji maji nuɔ amɛhe shishi, ni ewoɔ ekomefeemɔ mli hewalɛ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Henɔi
Yɛ henɔ nɛɛ shishi lɛ, atsɔɔ wiemɔkuli lɛ aŋkroaŋkro ashishi yɛ niŋmaa lɛ mli, akwɛɛɛ yiŋtoo, ŋaa loo wiemɔ ni atsɔɔ niŋmaa lɛ shishi lɛ kɛyaa lɛ kusum.
Nɛkɛ henɔ nɛɛ haaa anu sane lɛ shishi jogbaŋŋ.
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ
Yɛ enɛ shishi lɛ, ataoɔ niŋmaa muu lɛ fɛɛ shishinumɔ fee wiemɔkulibii aŋkroaŋkro ashishitsɔɔmɔ.
Nɛkɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔ nɛɛ kwɛɔ kusum, wiemɔ kɛ jeŋshihilɛ saji ni miiya nɔ nakai ŋmɛlɛtswaa lɛ mli ni akɛtsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi bɔ ni eyɔɔ ni anaa shishitsɔɔmɔi ni ja.
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ Nɛkɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔ nɛɛ gbɛi kroko ji “overly free loo loose translation” nɛkɛ ŋaa nɛɛ jeɔ niŋmaa lɛ jebɔ loo eshishinumɔ lɛ ŋlɛ nɔ kwraa.
Eyɛ mli akɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ bɛ kpɛŋŋ moŋ nɛ shi kɛji akwɛɛɛ ni ahi ni akɛ henɔ nɛɛ tsu nii fe nine lɛ, shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ eyafeŋ faŋŋ loo oti komɛi baalaje yɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Abaanyɛ akɛtsu nii yɛ ŋaa niŋmaa ni ji ‘creative writing’ mli shi kɛji akɛmii tsu nii trilii yɛ agwaseiaŋ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli lɛ sane baaba.
Nikasemɔ He Nitsumɔ
Yɛ kui etɛtɛɛtɛ amli lɛ, nyɛkaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ yɛ nifeemɔi srɔtoi amli tamɔ (yɛ jara lɛ nɔ, helatsamɔhe loo sukuu) nyɛtsɔa wiemɔi loo sanekukuji komɛi ni nyɛfɔɔ nuu lɛ ashishi.
Yɛ jara lɛ nɔ (mɔ kome baanyɛ afee nihɔɔlɔ, mɔ kroko hu gbɔ ni eba shishramɔ kɛ mɔ kome hu ni miitsɔɔ gbɔ lɛ sane shishi kɛmiiba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli)
Yɛ helatsamɔhe yɛ Amɛrika (mɔ kome baafee datrɛfonyo, mɔ kome hu baafee onukpa ni bɛ hewalɛ, kɛ mɔ kome hu ni miitsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛmiiha helatsɛ lɛ kɛ datrɛfonyo lɛ kɛmiijɛ Blɔfo kɛmiiba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli)
Yɛ Ga wiemɔ nikasemɔ tsu lɛ mli (mɔ kome baafee tsɔɔlɔ, mɔ kome baafee nikaselɔ hee ni ejɛ Ablotsiri eba etsɛko, mɔ kome hu baatsɔɔ nɔ ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ wieɔ shishi eha kaselɔ hee lɛ) Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ He Ŋai Gbaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ehenɔi lɛ ahe sane (N.k., wiemɔkulibii asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ, Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ, kn.kn)
Tii saneshishitsɔɔmɔ yitso lɛ mli ekoŋŋ
Tii saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he mlai lɛ amli ekoŋŋ (nu wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ ahishi, nilee yɛ rɛjista ni ja kɛ nitsumɔ he yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ mli, kn.kn)
Tii saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ amli (wiemɔkulibii asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, shishinumɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ, sanegbaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he lɛ)
Kanemɔ niŋmaa ko ni kɛlɛ aashɛ wiemɔkulibii 300 loo nɔ ni fe nakai ni otsɔɔ shishi kɛjɛ wiemɔ ni niŋmaa lɛ yɔɔ lɛ kɛja wiemɔ kroko mli.
Too gbɛjianɔ kɛha ehe wiemɔ.
Gbalamɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli ni oha gbɛi etɛ komɛi anɔ ni ehe hiaa kɛha owiemɔ lɛ nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ.
Akɛ saneshitsɔɔmɔ nikaselɔ lɛ, aha bo shɛɛ wiemɔ ni tsɔɔlɔnukpa lɛ baawie yɛ osukuu lɛ Naawiemɔ kɛ Nikee nifeemɔ lɛ shishi ni esani atsɔɔ shishi.
Tsɔɔmɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔ ni okɛbaatsu nii kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni ohara nakai henɔ lɛ?
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ OTSI 14 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli mlai lɛ ahe (Nk.
Nilee yɛ wiemɔ ni abaatsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛya kɛ wiemɔ ni atsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi yɛ lɛ, kusum he nilee, wiemɔ mli mlai / akpɔi kɛ natselɛ kn.kn) Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 3: Agba mɔnaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve kn.kn.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANESHISHITSƆƆMƆ HE MLAI Tii Saneshishitɔɔmɔ Mli Ekoŋŋ Kwɛmɔ otsi 13 kɛha saneshitsɔtsɔɔmɔ mligbalamɔ, ehenɔi kɛ ehesɛɛnamɔi lɛ.
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he mlai : Kɛji saneshishitsɔɔlɔ ko kɛ nɛkɛ mlai nɛɛ tsu nii jogbaŋŋ lɛ, ebaanyɛ etsɔɔ sane fɛɛ sane shishi ni shishinumɔ ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli baafee faŋŋ no ji shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ baaye emuu.
Nilee yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ ahe: Esa akɛ saneshishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ he akpɔ yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ amli jogbaŋŋ bɔ ni afee ni ekɛ rɛjista ni ja atsu nii kɛji etsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi.
Esa akɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ aja: Tsɔɔmɔ sane lɛ shishi ni ekɛ wiemɔ ni eyɔɔ mli momo lɛ shihinumɔ lɛ aye hegbɔ.
Anɔkwayeli: Kaatsake shishinumɔ, jebɔ loo gbee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ/ niŋmaa momo lɛ mli kɛji ootsɔɔ shishi kɛmiiya wiemɔ kroko lɛ mli.
Yɛ niŋmaa / wiemɔ momo lɛ anɔkwa.
Esa akɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ afee faŋŋ: Bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ ni sane / niŋmaa ni otsɔɔ shishi lɛ afee faŋŋ ni ekahiɛ shishinumɔi srɔtoi.
Kusum he nilee: Esa ni okwɛ wiemɔ mli ni otsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛyaa lɛ kusum kɛ amɛnifeemɔi koni otsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi nakai
Esa ni ole yiŋtoo ni hɔɔ sane/niŋmaa l ɛ sɛɛ: Esa ni onu sane lɛ shishi jogbaŋŋ dani obɔi shishitsɔɔmɔ’
Esa ni oniŋmaa /wiemɔ lɛ afee ekome: Esaaa ni otsakeɔ wiemɔkulibii otii kɛ ŋaa ni okɛtsuɔ nii lɛ.
Fɔŋwiemɔi: Tsɔɔmɔ fɔŋwiemɔi, abɛi kɛ wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi lɛ akɛtsu nii lɛ ashishi jogbaŋŋ.
Toibolɔi/ Nikanelɔi: Kwɛmɔ toibolɔi loo kanelɔi lɛ awiemɔ, kusum kɛ nibii ni he hiaa amɛ.
Ŋaa kɛ gbee: Kaatsake wiemɔ/ niŋmaa momo lɛ ŋaa kɛ gbee lɛ.
Kanemɔ oniŋmaa lɛ mli ekoŋŋ ni osaa emli tɔmɔi fɛɛ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Nikasemɔ He Nitsumɔ
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni esa akɛ atsɔɔ sane shishi ni eja lɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ lɛ
Mɛni haa shishinumɔ eyabaaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni ejaaa he lɛ?
Tsɔɔmɔ niŋmaa kuku nɛɛ shishi kɛba Ga mli Ale lɛ akɛ “overly free loo loose translation”, nɛkɛ ŋaa nɛɛ jeɔ niŋmaa lɛ ejebɔ loo eshishinumɔ lɛ ŋlɛ nɔ kwraa.
Eyɛ mli akɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ bɛ kpɛŋŋ moŋ nɛ, shi kɛji akwɛɛɛ ni ahi ni akɛ henɔ nɛɛ tsu nii fe nine lɛ, shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ eyafeŋ faŋŋ loo oti komɛi baalaje yɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Abaanyɛ akɛtsu nii yɛ ŋaa niŋmaa ni ji ‘creative writing’ mli shi kɛji akɛmiitsu nii trilii yɛ agwaseiaŋ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli lɛ sane baaba.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ He Ŋai
Daamɔ nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ anɔ: Klasi muu fɛɛ nifeemɔ
Wiemɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mlai lɛ ahe.
(n.k., nilee yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ amli, kusum he nilee, wiemɔ mli mlai/ akpɔi kɛ naatselɛ, kn.kn)
Kanemɔ niŋmaa ko ni emli wiemɔkulibii shɛɔ 300 loo nɔ ni fe nakai ni otsɔɔ shishi.
Kɛ sane ni otsɔɔ shishi lɛ aba klasi ni nyɛ fɛɛ nyɛgbaa he sane.
Kwɛmɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he mlai enumɔ lɛ, yɛ osusumɔ naa lɛ, too amɛnaa osusumɔ naa yɛ nɔkwɔlɛ naa.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni oto naa nakai lɛ.
Kɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he mlai lɛ atsu nii ni otsɔɔ niŋmaa nɛɛ shishi kɛba owiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ale Ghana jogbaŋŋ yɛ ekusum nifeemɔ lɛ ahewɔ.
Afiyeli ji Ghana kusum ni he yɔɔ sɛɛnamɔ jogbaŋŋ, ebuaa mɛi anaa ni ekɛ ekomefeemɔ baa.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ, Afi ni Gamɛi yeɔ ni ji Hɔmɔwɔ lɛ yafeɔ miishɛɛ kɛ mlifimɔ be.
Yɛ afiyeli lɛ mli lɛ, ahoɔ shikwɛɛ niyenii tamɔ kpokpoi, ni mɛi feɔ ekome amɛkɛ tsui jurɔ daa shi yɛ nikpamɔ babaoo lɛ hewɔ.
Anyɛŋ atsi afiyeli hesɛɛnamɔi lɛ fɛɛ ata.
Ehaaa mɛi kɛ amɛmaŋ ana tsakpaa kɛkɛ shi moŋ ejieɔ nibii kɛ kusum srɔtoi ni maŋ lɛ yɔɔ kɛtsɔɔ je lɛ.
Kɛtsɔ lalai, joo, shikwɛɛ hesaamɔ mli lɛ, Ghanabii jieɔ amɛkusum kpo.
Kɛji yinɔbii bibii ni baa lɛ kɛ amɛhe wo afiyeli kɛ kusum nifeemɔi amli lɛ, amɛbaakase blema saji kɛ kusum he nibii ni baawa kɛbaa shikwɛɛ nibii nɛɛ ayi kɛha wɔsɛɛ yinɔbii krokomɛi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Note
Mlijaa 6 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛ nibii ni akase yɛ otsii 12, 13 kɛ 14 lɛ mli.
Awie bɔ ni nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii yeɔ ebuaa yɛ nɔdoomɔ mli.
Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ehe saji ahe titri lɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai srɔtoi ni baaye ebua kɛji aatsɔɔ sane ko shishi kɛmiiba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ, be ni akɛ kaselɔi baatsɔɔmɔ yitsei nɛɛ amli ata lɛ, amɛbaaanyɛ amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛna kɛjɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii ŋai lɛ amli lɛ adoo saji anɔ ni amɛnyɛ amɛtsɔɔ saji ashishi kɛba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ pɔtɛɛ ko mli hu.
Wiemɔ nikanemɔ vii ŋaagbɛi lɛ ahe (hiŋmɛi loo waobii tsimɔ, wiemɔkulibii loo sanekukuji anaa, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) Afi 2 Otsi
OTSI 13 Susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sanemuu mli mligbalamɔ : Susumɔ oti ji oti ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ.
Nitsɔɔmɔ kɛ nikasemɔ ŋaagbɛ he kaa
Gbekɛ fɛɛ gbekɛ yɛ nitsumɔi komɛi yɛ shia, akutso mli ni eyɔɔ kɛ sukuu.
Yɛ shia mli lɛ gbekɛ kpakpa gbɛnaa nitsumɔi lɛ ekomɛi ji kɛji ote shi leebi lɛ osaa osaa ni oka nɔ lɛ dani
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ojeɔ kpo, Kɛji oje kpo lɛ ŋa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ, onukpa jio, otipɛŋfonyo jio.
OTI: KUSUM NIFEEMƆI KƐ MAŊKURAMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Kusum Nifeemɔi Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Ale sɛnaamɔi ni yɔɔ afii srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ kui srɔtoi ni yɔɔ Ghana lɛ amli.
Oti gbajaa: Atsɔɔ nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ afii srɔtoi lɛ ahe HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ.
Ewieɔ shikwɛɛ afii kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Kaselɔi baakase oti nɛɛ kɛjɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kusum srɔtoi lɛ amli ni amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛbaana lɛ baawie bɔ ni kui srɔtoi lɛ yeɔ amɛfii lɛ amɛhaa.
Kɛji kaselɔi kase enɛ lɛ, amɛbaale amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛmaŋ kusum kɛ kusumi krokomɛi ni yɔɔ Ghana maŋ lɛ mli lɛ.
Ebaawa kɛbaa kusum yi.
Kaselɔi baanu kusum he nibii ashishi ni amɛbaanya kusumi krokomɛi ahe hu ni ekɛ ekomefeemɔ baaba.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hiaa kaselɔi jogbaŋŋ jeee yɛ shikwɛɛ lɛ kasemɔ mli pɛ kɛkɛ shi moŋ tsakpaa ka ekɛ jeŋ saji kɛ blema saji anikasemɔ lɛ teŋ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ wajeɔ kaselɔi ni egbeleɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ mli ni amɛnuɔ amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛ kusum shishi kɛ mɛi krokomɛi anɔ hu.
Aawo tsɔɔlɔ lɛ hewalɛ ni ekɛ sanegbaa nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai srɔtoi, yelikɛbuamɔ nibii srɔtoi kɛ kaahamɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii ni eha nikaemɔ aye emuu.
Otsi 16: Afii srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ.
NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
Mlijaa 7: AfiyeliMlijaa 7: Afiyeli Kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛdamɔ nibii ni mɛi krokomɛi ewieɔ lɛ anɔ ni amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ yɛ he, kɛji amɛwieee kɛshiii lɛ, amɛbaafi wiemɔ lɛ sɛɛ kpɛŋŋ ni amɛbaawie he jogbaŋŋ.
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaagbɛ yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ shishi yeɔ ebuaa niiahishinumɔ kɛ niiahesusumɔ vii yɛ kaselɔi agbɛfaŋ.
Kaa kpitiokpitioo haa anaa kaselɔi lɛ ashidaamɔ kɛ amɛnɔyaa.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi aniashishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ shikwɛɛ afiyeli yɛ Ghana nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Ayɛ ŋɛlɛ 4 kaa ni kwɛɔ niiahesusumɔ vii mli.
Kaa nɛɛ nɔyaa damɔ sanebimɔi, niŋmaa kukuji kɛ mɔnaa wiemɔi anɔ kɛkaa kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ yɛ bɔ ni amɛwieɔ nibii ahe amɛhaa kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛtsuɔ nii.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii koni amɛkɛna kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ, mii ni amɛ naa kɛha amɛ nɔyaa.
Gbɛkpamɔi DoK Ŋɛlɛ Kaa ŋaagbɛ Agba kui kɛ afii ni amɛyeɔ kɛ amɛhesɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe sane 4 Sanebimɔ Kɛ afii srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ atotoi amɛhe 4 Sanebimɔ
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli OTSI 15 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Awie shikwɛɛ afiyeli kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe YITSO OTI NI AKWƐƆ: AFIYELI
Afiyeli shishitsɔɔmɔ: Afiyeli ji kusum nifeemɔi ni ku loo maŋ ko nɔ bii feɔ kɛgbaa blema sane loo mumɔŋ nifeemɔi kɛtsɔ wiemɔi, lalai kɛ jooshwɛmɔi anɔ.
Afiyeli hesɛɛnamɔi: Afiyeli he yɛ sɛɛnamɔi babaoo.
Afiyeli kusum yibaamɔ: Eji nifeemɔi ni damɔ shi ha maŋ lɛ ni atsɔɔ na akɛ woo kaa wowɛi lɛ.
Akɛkaiɔ bɔ ni fee ni atswa maŋ loo akutso ko ama shi, hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ kakalɔi ni damɔ shi kɛha hemɔkɛyelii ni amɛhiɛ mli lɛ.
Akɛ afiyeli nifeemɔi lɛ kaiɔ kakalɔi ni huu kɛshi mɛi kpɔji, ni amɛkpɔkpɔi shikpɔji hee, kwɛ mɛi, wuu kɛshi naagbai ni nina amɛ lɛ.
Afiyeli beiaŋ ji be ni mɛi kɛkaiɔ mumɔi ni he ba sɛɛnamɔ kɛha akutso kɛ maŋ lɛ.
Tsɔsemɔ: Atsɔɔ afiyeli nɔ atsɔseɔ yinɔbii ni baa kɛtsɔ kusum nifeemɔi, mumɔŋ nifeemɔi kɛ akaŋshii kɛ shwɛmɔi anɔ.
Ekomefeemɔ: Afiyeli nifeemɔi lɛ haa mɛi kuɔ amɛsɛɛ kɛyaa amɛmaŋ, ni no tsɔɔ mɛi ni amɛji.
Mɛi nɛɛ feɔ ekome kɛyeɔ naagbai anɔ, ni amɛpleɔ nibii ni baaha amɛ miishɛɛ kɛ ninamɔ he.
Yɛ Shikagbɛfaŋ: Nɛkɛ be nɛɛ ji be ni akutsoŋbii lɛ yeɔ jara kɛbuaa shika naa kɛhaa amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛhe ejaakɛ mɛi babaoo ji mɛi ni baa akutso lɛ mli.
Nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ: Afiyeli beiaŋ lɛ abua shika naa kɛha nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ nibii yɛ akutso lɛ mli.
Yɛ nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ maŋbii lɛ waa kɛ maŋ nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ nibii tamɔ nubu jee, sukuu aloo jara maa, kɛ nibii krokomɛi ni he baahia akutsoŋbii lɛ.
Hiɛtsɛrɛjiemɔ: Afiyeli kɛ nifeemɔi babaoo ni kɛ mishɛɛ baa nyiɛ.
Ehaa mɛi nyaa amɛŋaa ahe yɛ mumɔi kɛ kusum gbɛfaŋ.
Wowɛi anibimɔ: Afiyeli ji be amli ni mɛi kɛ amɛnaagbai kɛ nibimɔi shwieɔ wowɛi lɛ ahiɛ koni amɛna amɛ mɔbɔ ni amɛye amɛbua amɛ.
Afiyeli henɔi: Ayeɔ afii srɔtoi yɛ Ghana maji lɛ fɛɛ anɔ.
Ekomɛi ji nikpamɔ afiyelii kɛ blemabii afiyelii, ni ayeɔ yɛ afi lɛ mli fɛɛ, kɛtsɔɔ kusum agbojee srɔtoi.
Ekomɛi ni ale jogbaŋŋ nɛ: Nikpamɔ afiyelii
Hɔmɔwɔ Afiyeli: Gamɛi ni yɔɔ Ga Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ ni yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ kɛkaiɔ hɔmɔ ni naa wa lɛ naagbee, kɛ shikwɛɛ lalai, joo, kɛ niyenii sr ɔtoi tamɔ kpokpoi.
Gologo Afiyeli: Yiteŋ Boka Kpokpaa nɔ bii kaiɔ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha nikpamɔ babaoo ni amɛtsɔɔ nɔ kɛ biɔ amɛ hebuu hu.
Samanpiid Afiyeli: Builsabii ni yɔɔ Yiteŋ Boka Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ ji mɛi ni yeɔ afi nɛɛ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha maŋbii lɛ kɛ amɛŋmɔshi nibii lɛ ahe buu .
Asogli Yɛlɛ Afiyeli: Afiyeli ni Asoglibii ni yɔɔ Shwilao Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ yeɔ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha yɛlɛ babaoo ni amɛnaa lɛ.
Kundum Afiyeli: Nikpamɔ afiyeli ni Ahanta kɛ Nzemamɛi ni yɔɔ Anai Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ lɛ yeɔ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha niyenii ni ebu lɛ hewɔŋ.
Ngmayem Afiyeli: Boka kpokpaa nɔ bii ni yeɔ afi nɛɛ kɛkadiɔ naagbee nikpamɔ lɛ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha nikpamɔ babaoo.
Odwira Afiyeli: Nikpamɔ afiyeli ni Akropong-Akwapimɛi yeɔ yɛ Boka Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha nikpamɔ babaoo.
Blemabii lɛ Afiyelii
Adae Kese Afiyeli: Ashantemɛi ji mɛi ni yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ kɛkaiɔ tsɛmɛi lɛ ni amɛtsɔɔ nɔ kɛbiɔ jɔɔmɔ kɛjɛɔ amɛdɛŋ kɛtsɔ gwabɔɔ, miyii kɛ jooshwɛmɔi amli.
Akwasidae Afiyeli: Ashantemɛi kɛkaiɔ tsɛmɛi lɛ daa otsii ekpaa kɛtsɔ shikwɛɛ miyii, joo, niyenii ashwamɔ kɛ daji afɔsemɔ mli.
Hogb ɛtsotso Afiyeli: Akɛkaiɔ Ewemɛi agbɛfaa wulu kɛjɛ Notsie ni ji ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ Togo lɛ.
Aŋlɔmɛi ni yeɔ afi nɛɛ yɛ Shwilao Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ.
Fetu Afahye Afiyeli: Nikpamɔ afiyeli ni Fantemɛi ni yɔɔ Cape Coast kɛ Elmina yeɔ kɛkaiɔ wowɔi kɛ tsɛmɛi lɛ.
Aboakyir Afiyeli: Effutumɛi ni yɔɔ Shiŋkpa lɛ ji mɛi ni yeɔ, kɛ adowa gbɔbimɔ lɛ kɛtsɔɔ amɛgbɛfaa wulu lɛ mli sane.
Bakatue Afiyeli: Akɛkadiɔ wuoyaa beiaŋ, Elminabii ni yɔɔ Teŋ Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ bii kɛ lɛlɛ kudɔmɔ kɛ shikwɛɛ jooshwɛmɔ tsɔɔ naa.
Damba Afiyeli: Dagombabii, Gonjabii, Nanaumbabii ni yɔɔ Kooyi Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ ni yeɔ kɛkaiɔ Gbalɔ Muhammed fɔmɔ kɛ egbɛiwoo.
Akwambɔ Afiyeli: Ajumako, Breman Esiam, Enyan Abaasa kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he lɛ ji mɛi yeɔ afi nɛɛ kɛgbaa gbɛ ni amɛkɛtsɔɔ woo ni amɛyɔɔ kɛha tsɛmɛi lɛ.
Asafotufiami Afiyeli: Adamɛi ni yɔɔ Ga Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ bii ji mɛi ni yɛɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ kɛkaiɔ tai ni tsɛmɛi lɛ huu kɛjɛ Ile Ife kɛbashɛ he ni amɛyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Kloyosikplemi Afiyeli: Yilo Krɔbɔbii ji mɛi ni yeɔ kɛkaiɔ amɛgbɛfaa wulu kɛjɛ Kloyomi (Krɔbɔ gɔŋ lɛ) kɛbashɛ he ni amɛyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Nikasemɔ Lɛ He Nitsumɔ
Mɛni ji afiyeli?
Ha afiyeli henɔi etɛ ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ kɛha eko fɛɛ eko.
Tsɔɔmɔ afiyeli ni okutsoŋbii lɛ yeɔ ni otsɔɔ nibii etɛ hewɔ ni amɛbaahiɛ afiyeli lɛ mli.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai
Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ: Agba afiyeli kusum he sane
Ku nitsumɔ Yɛ kui ni hiɛ hewalɛi srɔtoi
Awie afii srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ ahe
Ha sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ afii srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ.
Kwɛmɔ Ghana afiyeli kusum srɔtoi lɛ he vidio
Wiemɔ otii ni yɔɔ vidio lɛ mli.
(Mɛni/Namɔ he afi ayeɔ lɛ?
Namɔ ni yeɔ afi lɛ?
Mɛni hewɔ ayeɔ afi lɛ?
Nɛgbɛ ayeɔ afi lɛ yɛ?
Mɛɛ be mli yɛ afi lɛ mli ayeɔ nakai afi lɛ?
Too gbɛjianɔ ni owie nibii ni oyɔse yɛ vidio lɛ mli lɛ ahe.
Taomɔ mli ni otsɔɔ nɔ ni afi ni ayeɔ yɛ okutso lɛ mli lɛ damɔ shi eha kɛ shishinumɔ ni nyiɛ sɛɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ afiyeli gbɛnaa yɛ maŋ nɔyaa kɛ akɔntaabuu mli.
Mlijaa 7: AfiyeliMlijaa 7: Afiyeli OTSI 16 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ afii srɔtoi ni ayɔɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ atotoi amɛhe OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: AFII SRƆTOI NI AYEƆ GHANA LƐ
Afii ni ayeɔ lɛ Ghana (Mlitii): Afiyeli ji kusum loo mumɔŋ nifeemɔi ko ni maŋ ko efo efɔ shi ni amɛyeɔ kɛkaiɔ blema sane ko.
Ayeɔ afii kɛkadiɔ nifeemɔi, loo akɛgbaa kusum he otii komɛi kɛtsɔ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔi, joi kɛ lalai amli.
Bɔ ni ayeɔ afii yɛ Ghana maji komɛi anɔ: Shikwɛɛ kui srɔtoi yɛ gbɛ srɔto nɔ ni amɛyɛɔ amɛfi.
Kuku nɛɛ baatsɔɔ otii komɛi yɛ afii komɛi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ.
Hɔmɔwɔ Afi lɛ ji afi ni Gamɛi yeɔ.
Ayeɔ afi yɛ manyawalɛ nyɔɔŋ lɛ nɔ daa afi.
Naa bɔ ni ayeɔ afi lɛ.: Hesaamɔi Wulɔmɔi ni ji wowɛi lɛ anaadaalɔi lɛ baa shi.
Amɛduɔ ŋmaa (abele) ni no damɔ shi ha dioofeemɔ kɛ be ni akɛsɔleɔ kɛhaa maŋ lɛ.
Kɛji abaagbele gbɛ ni ji gbi ni akɛ dioofeemɔ kusum lɛ baaba naagbee lɛ afeɔ kusum ko ni atsɛɔ lɛ odadao nɔshwiemɔ.
Abuleɔ ŋshɔ kɛkɛ lɛ akɛ haaji akusum ni ale lɛ akɛ yeeyeyee tsa sɛɛ.
Ayeɔ Hɔmɔwɔ lɛ Hɔɔ ni ji enyɔ nɔ yɛ Manyawalɛ nyɔɔŋ lɛ nɔ.
Awoɔ shikwɛɛ atadei Afiyeli lɛ Kɛji aje afi lɛ shishi lɛ, maŋ lumɛi, hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ maŋbii lɛ toɔ srɛnɛɛ.
Alaa shikwɛɛ lalai ni ajoɔ.
Maŋ lumɛi kɛ maŋbii fɛɛ wulaa amɛhe kɛ shikwɛɛ hesaamɔ nibii.
Ashwaa nii ahaa wowɛi lɛ kɛ hiimeji ni amɛtsɔmɔ amɛsɛɛ lɛ.
Maŋ lumɛi lɛ shwaa kpokpoi, niyenii ni akɛyeɔ afi lɛ.
Wekui srɔtoi hu hoɔ kpokpoi nɛɛ eko ni amɛjaa.
Ahaa hii fɛɛ yeɔ nii yɛ ka kome mli kɛtsɔɔ ekomefeemɔ.
Niyeli kɛ mlifimɔ: Ajaa shikwɛɛ niyenii, ni mɛi feɔ ekome kɛnyaa.
Shikwɛɛ lalai kɛ joo: Ashwɛɔ joi srɔtoi kɛjieɔ Ga kusum lɛ kpo kɛtsɔɔ je lɛ.
Kusum nibii akpojiemɔ: Ajieɔ shikwɛɛ ŋai, nitɛŋmɔi kɛ nifeemɔi akpo.
Amuɔ afi lɛ naa kɛ shidaa ni ji ŋɔɔ wala (eji be ni akɛbaa shade kpakpa) kɛ tsese bulemɔ (aŋmɛɔ nibii fɛɛ ni eho lɛ fɛɛ ahe)
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli Sɛɛnamɔ Hɔmɔwɔ afi ni Gamɛi yeɔ lɛ tsɔɔ nikpamɔ wulu be ni amɛŋɔɔ shidaa kple amɛhaa wɔwɛi lɛ nikpamɔ babaoo lɛ hewɔŋ.
Ekɛ ekomefeemɔ baa ni ekɛ tsakpaa baa maŋbii lɛ ateŋ.
Odwira Afiyeli Lɛ Maŋtsɛmɛi kɛ mɛi ni jɛ Fanteakwa yɛ Boka kpokpa lɛ nɔ yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ.
Mɛi lɛ jɛ Akrokpong- Akuapim, Aburi, Larteh kɛ Mamfe kn.kn yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ.
Ayeɔ afi lɛ yɛ Gbo kɛ Antɔŋ nyɔɔŋ lɛ nɔ.
Ayeɔ afi nɛɛ yɛ nikpamɔ beiaŋ be mli ni niyenii ebu ni maŋbii lɛ tsɔɔ no nɔ kɛdaa hiimeji ni etsɔmɔ amɛ sɛɛ lɛ ashi.
Akɛni eji yɛlɛyeli afi lɛ, ahaa hiimeji lɛ niyenii kɛtsɔɔ akɛ niyenii ebu.
Ato Odwira afi lɛ naa kɛjɛ blema ni yinɔbii lɛ feɔ lɛ nakai pɛpɛɛpɛ.
Aguɔ hoofeemɔ kɛ yarafeemɔ fɛɛ naa gbii nyɔŋmai ejwɛ yɛ Akuapem dani ayeɔ Odwira lɛ.
Mɛi ni kuɔ nɛkɛ mla nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, agbalaa amɛtoi.
Odwira gbi Ju Yɛ Akan kusum naa lɛ, hemɔkɛyeli ko yɛ akɛ maŋtsɛmɛi gbooo, moŋ amɛyahiɔ akrowa ko.
Odwira gbi ju lɛ, agbaa gbɛ kɛyaa akrowa nɛɛ.
Nɛkɛ teemɔŋ nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, agbaa gbɛ lɛ kɛjɛɔ Akropɔŋ maŋ lɛ teŋ ni ji Okuapeman maŋ wulu lɛ kɛyaa lumɛi agɔɔ ni atsɛɔ lɛ Amanprobi lɛ.
Maŋ lɛ blafo lɛ kɛ amɛ maŋtsɛ lɛ ji mɛi ni nyiɛɔ nɛkɛ blema kusum nɛɛ hiɛ.
Nɛkɛ gbɛgbamɔ nɛɛ tsɔɔ akɛ hiimeji (Nananom) lɛ baabafata amɛhe kɛye Odwira lɛ yɛ Okuapehene maŋtsɛwe lɛ.
Odwira gbi Jufɔ Hemɔkɛyeli lɛ ji akɛ Odwira ji mumɔŋ nikeenii ni hiimeji lɛ kɛhaa maŋtsɛyeli weku lɛ.
Yɛ nɛkɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, Gyaasehene – niji maŋtsɛ ni fata maŋtsɛmɛi bibii lɛ enumɔ lɛ ahe yɛ Akuapem kɛ lumɛi krokomɛi lɛ tsɛɔ Banmuhene (Lumɛi afuuhe kɛ hiimeji lɛ anaadalɔ) lɛ kpee.
Gyaasehene lɛ woɔ Banmuhene lɛ toi nɔ akɛ Okuapehene ni ale lɛ akɛ Omanhene lɛ efee kralo akɛ ebaaye Odwira.
Gyaasehene lɛ faa Banmuhene lɛ koni eya koo lɛ mli, he ni gɔɔ lɛ yɔɔ lɛ, koni ekɛ hiimeji (Nananom) lɛ ayawie ni ekɛ Odwira lɛ ajɛ Nananom lɛ aŋɔɔ kɛbaha Okuapehene.
Ekoŋŋ yɛ Odwira da Jufɔ lɛ, sɛi shiai kpawo lɛ naadaalɔi lɛ baajieɔ yɛlɛ hee ni afa lɛ kpo.
Afeɔ enɛ yɛ Kubri shia lɛ mli.
Dani nɛkɛ gbi kɛ be nɛɛ baashɛ lɛ, aahi akɛ mɔ ko baasa yɛlɛ hee nɛɛ naa yɛ Akuapem maŋ.
Okuapehene lɛ taa maŋjaranɔ ni emɛɔ Baamuhene kɛ ewebii lɛ koni ekɛ Odwira jɔɔmɔ lɛ ajɛ hiimeji lɛ aŋɔɔ kɛba koni anyɛ aye Odwira lɛ.
Kɛji Banmuhene lɛ bashɛ pɛ, etsiɔ ebɛŋkɛɔ Okuapehene lɛ, ni akɛ mama haa amɛyiteŋ, hemɔkɛyeli lɛ ji akɛ Banmuhene kɛ Okuapehene pɛ naa nɔ ni hiimeji lɛ keɔ amɛ lɛ.
No sɛɛ lɛ, agbeleɔ hoofeemɔ lɛ naa.
Mlijaa 7: AfiyeliMlijaa 7: Afiyeli Odwira gbi Shɔ Gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, Akropɔŋ maŋ muu lɛ fɛɛ yeɔ awerɛho.
Awulaa kɛ nidiŋ kɛ nitsuru, wekui bua amɛhe naa kɛfoɔ mɛi ni etsɔmɔ amɛsɛɛ lɛ.
Okuapehene lɛ yasraa lumɔyeli shiai ni yɔɔ Akropɔŋ lɛ – Aboasa, Asona, Twafo, Benkum, Kyeame kɛ Akrahene ni ekɛ amɛyafoɔ.
Ajieɔ shishi maŋkɛ, akaiɔ hiimeji (Nananom) kɛ maŋbii fɛɛ ni amɛgboi yɛ afi ni eho lɛ mli yɛ shia fɛɛ shia ni yɔɔ Akropɔŋ lɛ mli.
Hiimeji (Nanamom) ni agba amɛgbɛ lɛ, aheɔ ayeɔ yɛ mumɔŋ gbɛfaŋ akɛ amɛbafata Odwira afiyeli lɛ he.
Odwira gbi Soo Okuapeman heɔ Nyɔŋmɔ nɔ amɛyeɔ ni be mli ni amɛdaa lɛ shi lɛ, amɛjajeɔ amɛwoo kɛ bulɛ amɛhaa Omanhene – mɔ ni ta Ofori Kuma sɛi lɛ nɔ lɛ.
Nɛkɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ.
Okuapehene woɔ atade fɛɛfɛo, etaa maŋ jara nɔ ni hereɔ mɛi kɛ gbɔi ni amɛba eŋɔɔ lɛ.
Nɛkɛ beiaŋ lɛ, oblayoo fioo, ni ji omahene sɛi he yei lɛ ateŋ mɔ kome kpaa maŋ lɛ gbɛjegbɛi lɛ anɔ ni ekɛ niyenii srɔto ‘Eto” bahaa ewu lɛ.
Gbɛkɛnaashi lɛ, Banmuhene kɛ ewebii lɛ kɛ eto yaa Nsurem – klɛŋklɛŋ omanhene lɛ toohe lɛ.
Maŋtsɛmɛi ni jɛ lumɛi ashiai krokomɛi lɛ nyiɛɔ sɛɛ.
Gbɛkɛ ŋmɛji kpawo mli lɛ, Okuapehene kɛ maŋtsɛmɛi bibii enumɔ lɛ – Gyaasehene, Benkumhene, Nifahene, Adontenhene kɛ Kurontihene yaa sɛi tsuji lɛ mli ekomeiekomei ni amɛyawajeɔ amɛkita kɛha Ofori Kuma Sɛi lɛ.
Enɛ ji kusum nifeemɔ ko ni tsɔɔ Akuapem ekomefeemɔ.
Kɛji atswa gbɛkɛ ŋmɛji 10 kɛyaa lɛ, esaaa ni ajieɔ kpo, enɛ kadiɔ duŋ nifeemɔ.
Akropɔŋ fɛɛ feɔ dioo be mli ni blafoi woɔ sɛii kɛ Okuapeman hewulamɔ nibii kɛtsɔɔ Akropɔŋ gbɛi lɛ anɔ kɛyaa Ademi mu (blema faa) kɛha amɛhe tsuumɔ.
Kɛji amɛku amɛsɛɛ amɛba lɛ, amɛkɛ amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛhe, sɛii kɛ hewulamɔ nibii lɛ fɛɛ ni atsuu amɛhe lɛ baahaa Okuapehene.
Ayeɔ aheɔ akɛ esaaa ni maŋnyo ni fataaa ku lɛ he lɛ naa amɛ yɛ nɛkɛ teemɔŋ nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli.
Yɛ kusum nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, Okuapehene lɛ jieɔ ehe flo ni akɛ mama bihii komɛi fiɔ lɛ ni etswaa tu shii etɛ kɛtsɔɔ akɛ, akɛ kusum kɛ gbi lɛ eba naagbee.
Enɛ hu tsɔɔ akɛ Omanhene ena hegbɛ ni ebɔ gwa ni no ji nɔ ni afeɔ gbi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ.
Odwira gbi Sohaa Gbi nɛɛ he hiaa jogbaŋŋ ejaakɛ no nɔ ayeɔ afi lɛ diɛŋtsɛ lɛ.
Maŋtsɛmɛi, onukpai kɛ Okuapemanbii yakpeɔ yɛ Mpeniase – (tso klɛŋklɛŋ ni adu be mli ni Akropɔŋ batsɔ Okuapeman maŋ wulu lɛ.
Mpeniase nɛɛ ebatsɔ okadi ni damɔ shi kɛha Okuapeman mumɔ) Maŋ hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ mɛi krokomɛi fɛɛ baa.
Okuapehene lɛ tsɔɔ nibii ni etee nɔ yɛ maŋ lɛ mli afi ni eho lɛ mli ni etsɔɔ nibii ni abaafee afi hee lɛ mli ni kɛ nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ baaba.
Okuapehene lɛ heɔ shɛɛwiemɔi fɛɛ ni jɛ maŋ onukpai kɛ ewebii ni yɔɔ maŋsɛɛ kɛ emaŋ lɛ fɛɛ adɛŋ.
Okuapehene lɛ kɛ enaawiemɔ haa Okapemanbii.
Afii ni eho lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, gbɔi babaoo baa maŋ lɛ mli.
Kɛ je na lɛ, ashwɛɔ shwɛmɔi pii.
Nitsumɔhei wuji hu tsɔɔ no nɔ amɛkɛhɔɔ amɛ nibii kɛji amɛpia nifeemɔi lɛ ni amɛ hu amɛkɛ nibii hee ba jaraŋ.
Kusum nifeemɔi eyi Odwira afiyeli lɛ mli tɔ, ŋaa, lalai, hesaamɔ kɛ niyenii.
Kusum nifeemɔi nɛɛ gbeleɔ gbɛi hei ehaa maŋbii kɛ gbɔi ni baa afiyeli lɛ fɛɛ.
Wɔsumɔɔ wɔkusum lɛ, wɔbaa wɔnibii ayi ni wɔtsɔɔ shishi yɛ ŋlɛ fɛɛ nɔ ejaakɛ ejra wa ha wɔ.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli yɛ amɛ wiemɔ ni yɔɔ srɔto lɛ hewɔ lɛ, enɛ ji hegbɛ ni oyasra amɛ.
Wɔmiibashɛ wɔhe pam akɛ wɔbaakpee bo ni wɔjie wɔkusum lɛ kpo wɔtsɔɔ bo kɛ je lɛ.
Odwira gbi Hɔɔ Kɛ Hɔgbaa Hɔɔ titri lɛ, nɔ ko feemɔ bɛ tsɔ shi nifeemɔi krokomɛi ni ji shwɛmɔi tamɔ bɔɔlutswaa, kɛ shwɛmɔi krokomɛi yaa nɔ ni oblahii kɛ oblayei lɛ kɛshɛjeɔ amɛmii yɛ maŋ lɛ mli.
Hɔgbaa lɛ, Akuapem Krontihene lɛ bɔɔ gwa srɔto ko ni akɛmuɔ Odwira afiyeli lɛ naa.
Fetu Afahyɛ Fetu Afahyɛ ji afi ni Oguaamɛi kɛ amɛmaŋtsɛmɛi lɛ yeɔ yɛ Teŋ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ.
Ayeɔ kakaiɔ loo babaoo ashamɔ yɛ ŋshɔ lɛ mli, ni amɛfeɔ kusum kɛdaa wowɛi 77 ni yɔɔ Oguaamaŋ lɛ shi, ni amɛtsɔɔ no nɔ hu kɛsaa amɛmaŋ lɛ mli.
Hesaamɔ kɛha afi lɛ jieɔ shishi kɛjɛɔ naagbee otsi ni yɔɔ manyawalɛ nyɔɔŋ lɛ mli lɛ.
Nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, amɛkɛhereɔ gbɔi ni jɛɔ je lɛŋ he fɛɛ he kɛbaa amɛmaŋ kɛ amɛmaŋbii ni amɛfafai gbɛ kɛbaa Oguaamaŋ ekoŋŋ.
Ajieɔ afi lɛ diɛŋtsɛ shishi klɛŋklɛŋ Hɔɔ ni yɔɔ Gbo nyɔɔŋ lɛ mli lɛ nɔ.
Dani abaaye afi lɛ diɛŋtsɛ lɛ, akɛ Ɔmahen lɛ woɔ tsuŋ otsi.
Be mli ni awo lɛ tsuŋ lɛ, esɔleɔ ni ebiɔ nilee kɛjɛ bɔlɔ lɛ (Ɔboadze) kɛ hiimeji lɛ adɛŋ, ni ebiɔ hewalɛ yɛ mumɔŋ kɛ heloo gbɛfaŋ fɛɛ koni enyɛ eje kpo yɛ hewalɛ mli ni ejwɛŋmɔ mli hu aka kɛha nifeemɔi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ bɔ ni afee ni afi lɛ aye emuu.
Kɛji akɛ Ɔmanhen tsuŋwoo nifeemɔ lɛ ba naagbee lɛ, ebaa maŋjaranɔ ni eyaa sɛi tsu lɛ mli ayayeɔ ŋkpai ekɛbaa jɔɔmɔ kɛjɛɔ Oguaamaŋ wowɛi 77 lɛ adɛŋ, amɛhemɔkɛyeli ji akɛ, amɛ amɛkwɛɔ Oguaamaŋ lɛ nɔ.
Dani abaaje afi lɛ shishi lɛ, aguɔ hoofeemɔ nifeemɔi fɛɛ anaa ni ahɛɛ Fosu kpaakpo lɛ mli bɔ ni afee jeŋ ajɔ.
Afeɔ enɛ koni mumɔi ni yɔɔ Oguaamaŋ lɛ anyiɛ nifeemɔ lɛ hiɛ ni amɛkudɔ mɛi ni toɔ nifeemɔ lɛ he gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ.
Afeɔ enɛ dani gbi klɛŋklɛŋ ni yɔɔ Gbo lɛ shɛɔ.
Fosu mukpo lɛ nɔkwɛlɔ (Emissafo) ni yɔɔ Oguaa lɛ yeɔ ŋkpai yɛ kpaakpo lɛ naa ni ebaatsɛ mumɔi lɛ koni amɛshwie mumɔi fɔji kɛ oshrai ni baanina gbɔi ni baa afi lɛ kɛya sɛɛ flɛŋŋ.
Ayeɔ ŋkpai lɛ hu kɛbaa loo babaoo, ŋmɔshi nibii babaoo kɛ shweremɔ hu.
Emuntumadadze lɛ – “shishitsɔɔmɔ ji ‘hewalɛ gbi’ – gbi ni oblahii kɛ oblayei kɛ hiimeji kɛ yeimeji kɛsaa akutso lɛ mli.
Amɛjieɔ gɔɔtai amli ni amɛshaa tsui ni yɔɔ akutso lɛ fɛɛ ahe, yiŋtoo lɛ ji akɛ amɛbaasaa akutso lɛ mli ni emli afee fɛo kɛha gwabɔɔ wulu ni ji “Bakatue” lɛ.
Ashiɔ kpe yɛ Fosu mukpo gbatsu lɛ naa daa Ju naagbee ni yɔɔ Manyawalɛ nyɔɔŋ lɛ mli.
Mɛi babaoo babuaa shi yɛ gbatsu lɛ naa kɛkwɛɔ nibii ni wɔŋtsɛmɛi lɛ feɔ yɛ amɛkutso lɛ mli.
Atsuɔ nitsumɔ nɛɛ kɛyashiɔ enɔ jetseremɔ be mli ni ayiɔ mii ni wɔŋtsɛmɛi lɛ joɔ.
Atsɛɔ hiimeji lɛ amumɔi lɛ koni amɛgba nɔ ni baaya nɔ yɛ afi hee lɛ mli.
Enɔ jetseremɔ Jufɔ hu lɛ, afeɔ nibii pii.
\Afeɔ kusum yɛ Fosu gbatsu lɛ naa, ni naagbee lɛ akɛ lɛji daa yɛ Fosu mukpo lɛ mli kɛji Ɔmanhen ye ŋkpai eta.
Akɛni ahaa mɔ ko ahɛ yɛ Fosu mukpo lɛ mli bei nɛɛ amli hewɔ lɛ, Ɔmanhen lɛ ji mɔ klɛŋklɛŋ ni fɔɔ yaa shii etɛ kɛ gbeleɔ mukpo lɛ naa kɛhaa maŋ lɛ.
Loo pii ni Ɔmanhene lɛ shaa lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ amɛbaana loo pii afi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ.
Mɛi bayeɔ nifeemɔ lɛ he odase be mli atswaraa tu.
Mlijaa 7: AfiyeliMlijaa 7: Afiyeli Maŋtsɛmɛi ni yɔɔ Oguaa lɛ kɔɔ Shɔ kɛŋmɛɔ shi ni amɛkɛhereɔ Oguaabii lɛ.
Ayiɔ mii ni Asafo kui kpawo lɛ joɔ.
Akɛ gbi nɛɛ kpataa bei ni mɛi tsɔɔ nɔ amɛleɔ amɛhe hu.
Afeɔ kusum ko hu yɛ Nana Paprata gbatsu lɛ naa Soo gbɛkɛ.
Ajoɔ Adammba kɛtsɛɔ hiimeji lɛ amumɔi lɛ koni wɔŋtsɛmɛi lɛ anyɛ agba.
Afeɔ kusum nɛɛ kɛyashiɔ jetsɛremɔ.
Oti lɛ ji atsuu Oguaamaŋ lɛ he ni ashwie mumɔi fɔji fiaa.
Nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, agbeɔ tsina kɛjuraa Oguaamaŋ.
Dani abaajura maŋ lɛ, akɛ kooloo lɛ yaa Nana Tabir gbatsu lɛ naa ni ayatsuɔ he kɛha afɔleshaa kple nɛɛ.
Ayashaa afɔle lɛ yɛ Papratam.
Akɛtoɔ sliki tso ni Ɔmanhen taa shishi yɛ gwabɔɔ lɛ shishi lɛ he okadi.
Ekɛ emaŋtsɛmɛi bibii lɛ kɛ maŋ onukpai lɛ taa shi yɛ Tabri gbatsu lɛ naa he ni akɛ tsina afimɔ enaji lɛ eŋmɔ lɛ.
Ɔmahen yeɔ ŋkpai ni etsuɔ kusum komɛi, etsɛɔ hiimeji lɛ ni amɛbaawa Oguaamaŋ.
Biɛ lɛ, ekɔɔ kakla ni ekɛgbeɔ tsina lɛ ehaa wɔji lɛ.
Kɛji Ɔmanhen sha afɔle lɛ eta lɛ, Ayeɔ afi lɛ diɛŋtsɛ Hɔɔ klɛŋklɛŋ ni yɔɔ Gbo nyɔɔŋ lɛ nɔ lɛ.
Nɛkɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, Asafo kui lɛ toɔ srɛnɛɛ kɛnyiɛɔ Oguaa kɛjɛ Kotokuraba kɛbaa blohum ni yɔɔ maŋtsɛwe lɛ.
Mɛi jɛɔ hei hei baa amɛbakwɛɔ afi nɛɛ.
Abɔɔ gwa nɛkɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ ni awieɔ saji ni gbaa Oguaa naa ni akpaa Asafo kui kpawo lɛ afai koni amɛbu Oguaamaŋ he.Ayiɔ mii, ajoɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai kɛkpeɔ maŋ kɛbaa afi hee lɛ mli kɛ toiŋjɔlɛ kɛ shweremɔ.
Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ awoɔ afi lɛ hesɔlɛ kɛ Afahyɛ joo, bɔɔlutswaa, atadei kɛ hesaamɔ, kɛ kusum nibii akpojiemɔ kɛ ‘Miss Afahyɛ’ nifeemɔ lɛ.
Kɛji agbe afi lɛ naa lɛ, Hɔgbaa lɛ asafo kui (sɔlemɔi) fɛɛ feɔ ekome ni amɛjaa yɛ Victoria Park kɛdaa Nyɔŋmɔ shi akɛ Eye ebua Oguaa ni afiyeli lɛ efee shweshweeshwe.
Kɛfatahe lɛ, atsɔɔ nɔ akɛtaoɔ shika kɛhaa Oguaamaŋ.
Kɛtsɔ enɛ nɔ lɛ, Ɔmanhen kɛ enukpai lɛ yaa sɔlemɔfeemɔ nɛɛ ni amɛkɛ hegbɛ lɛ tswaa gbi ni abaaye afi lɛ yɛ afi hee ni abaabote lɛ mli lɛ.
Srɔtoi ni yɔɔ Afii ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ: (Biɛ lɛ, esa akɛ kaselɔi akase maji krokomɛi anɔ afii ni amɛtsɔɔ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ amɛteŋ) Nɔkwɛmɔnii nɛ.
Odwira Afiyeli Lɛ
M ɛi ni yeɔ: Akanmɛi, ni ji Akwamumɛi kɛ mɛi ni jɛ Akyem lɛ.
Oti: Koni ajura maŋ lɛ kɛ akutsei lɛ, ni anya ekomefeemɔ kɛ hewalɛ ni maŋ lɛ yɔɔ lɛ he.
Nifeemɔi: Kusumi, mii ayii, joo, lalai kɛ hiimeji kɛ wowɛi lɛ anihamɔ
Be: Afeɔ lɛ otsi Fetu Afahyɛ
Mɛi ni yeɔ: Oguaamɛi ni yɔɔ Cape Coast lɛ.
Oti: Koni akai Oguaamɛi ablema sane, kusum kɛ ekomefeemɔ.
Nifeemɔ i: Kusumi, mii ayii, lalai kɛ lalai kɛ afɔleshaa kɛha Oguaa wowɛi lɛ.
Mɛi pɔtɛɛ ni yeɔ: Akan ku gbajaa yeɔ Odwira, shi Fetu Afahyɛ lɛ Oguamɛi ni yɔɔ Cape Coast lɛ pɛ yeɔ.
Blema saji: Odwira blema saji lɛ damɔ Akan maŋtsɛyelii lɛ anɔ, shi Fetu Afahyɛ lɛ Oguaamɛi ablema saji kɛ kusum pɛ anɔ edamɔ.
Kusum nifeemɔi: Nifeemɔi nɛɛ fɛɛ damɔ kusumi anɔ shi eko fɛɛ eko yɛ srɔto.
Nikasemɔ Nitsumɔ Saamɔ ohe ni oha agba kadaa ni ji awie aha loo awie ashi yitso lɛ: “Eji oblahii kɛ oblayei lɛ agbɛnaa akɛ amɛkɛ amɛhe baawo shikwɛɛ afiyelii lɛ amli bɔ ni afee ni amɛkusum lɛ akalaaje.” Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai
Tii Afiyeli nikasemɔ lɛ mli ekoŋŋ
Agba shikwɛɛ afiyeli srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ, akwɛ henɔi lɛ.
Agba afiyeli srɔtoi lɛ ahe sɛɛnamɔ.
Kuu nitsumɔ/ Afutu kaselɔi lɛ yɛ amɛmɔdɛŋbɔɔ naa
Kui srɔtoi lɛ atsɔɔ srɔtoi kɛ nibii ni haa afii lɛ ekomɛi yeɔ hegbɔ lɛ.
Tsɛ afii enumɔ komɛi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana kɛ mɛi ni yeɔ.
Kɛ omaŋ afi lɛ ato afii ni otsɛ lɛ ateŋ ekome he.
(Nk., afi lɛ gbɛi, mɛi ni yeɔ, bɔ ni ayeɔ lɛ, kusum nifeemɔi komɛi kɛ afi lɛ he sɛɛnamɔ kn.kn.
Mɛi komɛi heɔ yeɔ akɛ shikwɛɛ afii abe eho.
Ŋmaa wolo hiɛgbelemɔ ni otsɔɔ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔ kɛha ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ Ghanamaŋ shihilɛ.
Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ afii srɔtoi ni okase lɛ ahe lɛ atsɔɔ nɔnyɛɛ ni ekɛbaa maŋ ni yeee lɛ anɔ.
Mliijaa 7 Mlikwɛmɔ Otsii nyɔŋma kɛ enumɔ kɛ nyɔŋma kɛ ekpaa lɛ, wɔwie shikwɛɛ afiyelii, amɛhenɔi kɛ sɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Wɔkɔ afii enyɔ ni wɔtsɔɔ nɔ ni feɔ amɛ srɔtoi kɛ nɔ ni haa amɛyeɔ hegbɔ.
Afee enɛ koni kaselɔi ale amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛkusum ni amɛnya mɛi krokomɛi anɔ he hu.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ, be mli ni akaseɔ mlijaa nɛɛ he nii lɛ, kaselɔi baale amɛ kusum koni amɛ baayi.
Ebaawo kaselɔi ahewalɛ koni amɛtsa yaa kɛye afii lɛ.
Amɛbaakase ŋai, amɛbaatɛŋ mfoniri yɛ amɛjwɛŋmɔ mli ni amɛbaana wiemɔi hee ni amɛkɛbaatsu nii yɛ maŋ kɛ jeŋ saji anibii ahewiemɔ mli.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli OTSI 16 Bɔ ni abaadiki sane ko mlikwɛmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli I.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ
OTI: KUSUM NIFEEMƆI KƐ MAŊKURAMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Kusum Nifeemɔi Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ he lɛ atsɔɔ srɔto kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔ kusumi yɛ maji srɔtoi anɔ lɛ yeɔ hegbɔ.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Tsɔɔmɔ nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ he.
Niahefɛlemɔ Feemɔ Practice Paper 2 ni yɔɔ Otsi 18.
Esani okɔ kɛjɛ nibii ni atsɔɔ kɛjɛ Year 1 kɛyashi 3.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ baawie gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ he yɛ kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ yitso lɛ shishi.
Kaselɔi baakase nɔ ni gbele ji, nɔ ni kɛbaa, ehenɔi, nɔnyɛɛ kɛ gbɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ atsɔ kɛku gbele naa.
Amɛbaakase yarafeemɔ, yarafeemɔ henɔi, yarafeemɔ he sɛɛnamɔ kɛ tsakemɔi ni eba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ mli.
Amɛbaatsɔɔ srɔtoi kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔi krokomɛi kɛ amɛkusum nɔ lɛ yeɔ hegbɔ.
Nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ yarafeemɔ he lɛ baaye ebua kaselɔi ni amɛbaale amɛkusum kɛ mɛi krokomɛi anɔ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hiaa kaselɔi jogbaŋŋ jeee yɛ shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ kasemɔ mli pɛ kɛkɛ shi moŋ tsakpaa ni ka ekɛ jeŋ saji kɛ blema saji anikasemɔ lɛ teŋ.
Otsii kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi ni kɔɔ mlijaa nɛɛ he nɛ:
Otsi 18: Abaatsɔɔ srɔtoi kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔi yeɔ hegbɔ yɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kui srɔtoi lɛ ateŋ.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Sanenaataomɔ Nikasemɔ: yɛ ŋaagbɛ nɛɛ mli lɛ, akɛ jeŋshihilɛ mli saji toɔ nikasemɔ lɛ he ni no haa kaselɔi nuɔ nibii ashishi faŋŋ fe nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ ni awieɔ nibii ahe tamɔ bɔ ni amɛji lɛ.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi anishishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ ahe.
Gbɛkpamɔi DoK Ŋɛlɛ Kaa henɔ LI.1.3.3.1 Nibii ni kɔɔ gbele he sanegbaa 4 Sanegbaa LI.2.3.3.1 Abaatsɔɔ srɔtoi kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔ kusum nifeemɔi lɛ ekomɛi yeɔ hegbɔ yɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kui srɔtoi lɛ ateŋ.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ OTSI 17 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Agba saji ni kɔɔ gbele he OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: GBELE Gbele: Gbele ji nɔ ni foɔ adesa wala sɛɛ yɛ shikpɔŋ nɛɛ nɔ.
Eji nɔ ko ni kɛji eba lɛ anyɛɛɛ atsake yɛ adesa walashihilɛ mli: adesa susuma/mumɔ lɛ shiɔ egbɔmɔtso lɛ mli.
Esa ni ale akɛ gbele shishitsɔɔmɔ yɛ srɔtoi yɛ kusumi srɔtoi lɛ amli, mla naa kɛ gbɔmɔtsoŋ kwɛmɔ mli saji.
(Shishitsɔɔlɔi/ tsɔɔlɔi atsɔɔ gbele shishi tamɔ bɔ ni eyɔɔ yɛ amɛkusum lɛ mli lɛ) Nibii komɛi ni kɛ gbele baa
Dalɛ: kɛji mɛi da ni amɛgbɔlɔ lɛ, amɛgboiɔ
Oshrai: oshrai tamɔ tsɔji oshrai, nu miimɔ, loo shigbeemɔ kɛjɛ tsu kakadaŋŋ yiteŋ kn, lɛ ebaanyɛ egbe mɛi.
Helai: Helai tamɔ HIV/AIDS, sikli hela, atridii, musuŋkɔɔ, tsui hela kɛ ekrokomɛi lɛ haa mɛi wala ŋmɛɔ amɛ kɛji amɔɔɔ naa.
Loomɔi: Loomɔi kɛ tsofai gbohii nyɛɔ egbeɔ mɛi.
Adebɔɔ mli oshrai: Adebɔɔ mli oshrai tamɔ la, nugbɔ ni naa wa, tsui ni ekumɔɔ egbeɔ mɛi anɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi hu kpataa mɛi awala hiɛ
Mumɔi/ Ayɛkpɛmɔ: Ayeɔ aheɔ akɛ wɔji nyɛɔ amɛgbeɔ mɛi kɛji amɛtɔ amɛ nɔ loo kɛji amɛkɛ shade fɔŋ ba maŋ mli kɛji amɛtɔ mla nɔ.
(Tsɔɔlɔ agbala enɛ mli yɛ amɛ kusum naa) Gbele Henɔi: Nyɔŋmɔjeŋ Gbele Kɛ Kpatu Gbele
Nyɔŋmɔjeŋ gbele: Gbele ni agboɔ kɛji ada loo hela ye mɔ ni etsɔ mli kɛ gbo.
Kpatu gbele: Enɛ lɛ jeee dalɛ loo hela gbeɔ mɛi lɛ.
Shia gbele: Enɛ lɛ mɔ kome loo mɛi enyɔ gbeɔ mɔ lɛ.
Yɛ mla naa lɛ kɛji aje gbɛ agbe mɔ lɛ atsɛɔ lɛ ‘murder’ ni kɛji ajeee gbɛ lɛ atsɛ lɛ ‘manslaughter”
Oshra gbele: Enɛ lɛ, oshra ni kɛ nɛkɛ gbele nɛɛ baa.
Abuɔ nɛkɛ gbele henɔ nɛɛ akɛ oshra kɛji jeee mɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ fo ewala nɔ loo hela ni gbe mɔ lɛ.
Ebaanyɛ efee tsɔne/lɔre oshra, shigbeemɔ ni naa wa kn.kn.
Nɔ ni gbele kɛbaa mɔ nɔ: Eyɛ mli moŋ akɛ gbele foɔ adesa tɔlɛ sɛɛ, shi gbele diɛŋtsɛ nyɛɔ ekɛ nibii fɔji baa adesa nɔ yɛ gbɛi pii anɔ.
Suɔlɔ ko gbele haa weku muu kɛ nanemɛi fɛɛ awerɛ hoɔ amɛhe ni amɛnaaa miishɛɛ.
Shramɔ mli naagbai: gbele haa akpoɔ mɛi, titri lɛ mɛi ni amɛ pɛ amɛyɔɔ loo mɛi ni kɛ mɛi pii bɛ tsakpaa kpakpa ko.
Mɛi ni ashiɔ yɛ sɛɛ lɛ naaa yelikɛbuamɔ ni mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ kɛhaa amɛ lɛ dɔŋŋ.
Shika gbɛfaŋ naagbai: Gbele kɛ nyɔmɔ baa titri lɛ yarafeemɔ mli nyɔmɔi, helatsamɔ mli nyɔmɔi, mɛi anitsumɔ mli nyɔmɔwoo nyɛɔ eŋmɛɔ amɛ tete po.
Jwɛŋmɔ mli naagbai: Gbele titri lɛ kpatu gbele woɔ mɛi jwɛŋmɔ.
Hewalɛ shibaa: Gbele mli naagbai nyɛɔ ehaa hela mɔɔ mɛi titri lɛ mɛi ni egbɔjɔ momo loo mɛi ni hela yɛ amɛhe momo
Nitsumɔ mli naagbai: Gbɛ baaka nitsumɔi kɛ gbɛhei ni mɛi ni egboi kpatu gbele lɛ hiɛ yɛ nitsumɔhei srɔtoi lɛ amli titri lɛ kɛji gbekɛbii kɛ oblahii kɛ oblayei gboi, ni no yeŋ ebuaŋ maŋ lɛ wɔsɛɛ.
Mumɔŋ naagbai: Kpatu gbele haa mɛi jeɔ amɛhemɔkɛyelii loo Nyɔŋmɔ he ŋwane.
Gbɛi komɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ atsɔ kɛku gbele naa : Ayɛ gbɛi pii anɔ ni adesai baanyɛ amɛtsɔ nɔ kɛku gbele naa.
Niyenii kpakpa yeli: Esa ni aye niyenii ni hewalɛ yɔɔ mli tamɔ aduawai, yelibai kɛ niyenii krokomɛi ni hewalɛ yɔɔ amɛmli.
Kpɔiaŋgbɔlemɔ: Esa akɛ wɔfɔ wɔkpɔiaŋ gbɔlemɔ, wɔ fɔ nyiɛmɔ, wɔ fɔ foijee loo kpɔiaŋgbɔlemɔi nifeemɔi srɔtoi feemɔ.
Akwɛ daa ni wa numɔ: Esa ni akwɛ ni akanu daa fee nine loo akpa numɔ kwraa.
Afɔ helatsamɔhe lɛ sramɔ: Esa akɛ afɔ helatsamɔhe lɛ sramɔ kɛha gbɔmɔtso lɛ mlikwɛmɔ koni kɛji hela ko etee yɛ gbɔmɔtso lɛ mli lɛ, ana mra ni aku naa.
Tsofagbuu : Esa ni wɔgbulɔ ni wɔkɔlɔ tsofa ni sa, no hu yeɔ ebuaa
Hela nɔkwɛmɔ: Esa ni mɛi ni yɔɔ helai komɛi tamɔ sikli hela loo tsui hela lɛ akwɛ amɛhe nɔ jogbaŋŋ ni amɛjwɛŋ nɔ amɛkɔlɔɔ amɛtsofai lɛ.
Nibii ni esa akɛ wɔhiɛ ahi nɔ ni wɔfee
‘Sitibɛɛti woo’: Esa akɛ awo sititbɛɛti kɛji aakudɔ loo ata tsɔji amli.
‘Hɛlmɛtiwoo: Esa akɛ abu hɛlmɛti kɛji aata moto loo baisiko nɔ.
Shigbeemɔ naakuu: Esa ni ato gbɛjianɔ kɛku shigbeemɔ naa, tamɔ abaafee nibii ni abaanyɛ amɔmɔ mli kɛji aanyiɛ loo aakwɔ nii loo anyiɛɛɛ nibii ni shaneɔ anɔ.
Trafiki mla nɔyeli: Kɛji aakudɔ tsɔji loo aafo gbɛ mli lɛ, esa akɛ aye mlai lɛ anɔ koni akɛna oshra loo agboi
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Jwɛŋmɔ shikamɔ mli hewalɛ
Hejɔɔmɔ: Ajɔ he bɔ ni sa ni akajwɛŋ pii
Jwɛŋmɔ mli hewalɛnamɔ yelikɛbuamɔ: Esa akɛ abi yelikɛbuamɔ kɛjɛ jwɛŋmɔ mli hewalɛnamɔ nitsulɔi loo yelikɛbuamɔ kui adɛŋ.
Oshrai anaakuu: Esa ni ato lajee he gbɛjianɔ, akɛ nibii ni woɔ la aka bɛ ŋkɛ nibii ni jeɔ la.
Esa akɛ atsi he kɛjɛ subaŋi fɔji tamɔ juu, daanumɔ trilii kɛ ajwamaŋbɔɔ he.
Abu onukpai, ni aye mlai anɔ.
Yarafeemɔ Kusum Yɛ Ghana Maji Lɛ Ekomɛi Anɔ Note Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ akwɛ yarafeemɔ kusum yɛ Ghana maji lɛ ekomɛi anɔ he niŋmaa lɛ yɛ baafa 56-58 yɛ nikaselɔ gbɛtsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Nikasemɔ Nitsumɔ Apɛi naagbai ejwɛ (4) ni gbele kɛbaa mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ weku kɛ nanemɛi anɔ Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai Sanenaataomɔ Nikasemɔ
Klasi muu fɛɛ sanegbaa: Agba gbele he sane.
Ku nitsumɔ: Awie gbele henɔi lɛ ahe yɛ kusum naa.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ anitsumɔ: Atsɔɔ naagbai ni gbele kɛbaa.
Klasi muu fɛɛ: Awie gbɛi komɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ atsɔ kɛku gbele naa he.
Taomɔ nibii enumɔ ni kɛ gbele baa yɛ akutso lɛ mli ni ataoɔ nibii otii ni kɛ nɛkɛ gbele nɛɛ baa kɛ nɔnyɛɛ ni gbele lɛ yɔɔ yɛ akutso lɛ nɔ
Akɛ naawielɔ lɛ, tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni obaatsɔɔ okutsoŋbii lɛ gbɛ nɔ ni amɛbaanyɛ amɛtsɔ nɔ kɛku gbele naa.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ OTSI 18 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ yɛ Ghana maji srɔtoi lɛ anɔ lɛ atotoo amɛhe OTI HE NI AWIEƆ: SRƆTO NI YƆƆ YARAFEEMƆ KUSUM YƐ GHANA MAJI LƐ SRƆTOI LƐ ANƆ
Gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ: Yarafeemɔ ji gbɛ kpakpa nɔ ni maŋ muu fɛɛ ehe ewo mli akɛ wekumɛi kɛ maŋbii fɛɛ atsɔ nɔ kɛwo mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ gbɛ.
Yarafeemɔ ji naagbee kusum loo nifeemɔ ni mɛi ni yɔɔ wala mli feɔ kɛwaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ kɛkpalaa lɛ kɛjɛ hiɛkalɔi ateŋ kɛyaa gbohiiajeŋ yɛ toiŋjɔlɛ mli.
Yarafeemɔ ji gbɛ ni ahe awo mli akɛ akutsoŋbii lɛ atsɔɔ nɔ amɛkɛma amɛkɛ mɔ ni egbo lɛ tsakpaa lɛ nɔ mi ekoŋŋ.
Yarafeemɔ ji kusum nifeemɔ ni afeɔ kɛfuɔ/ toɔ mɔ ni egbo lɛ.
Kusum nifeemɔ nɛɛ, esoro bɔ ni maŋ fɛɛ maŋ feɔ enɔ ehaa kɛ mlai ni yɔɔ he hu.
Akutsoŋbii lɛ kɛ weku lɛ yeɔ awerɛho ni amɛnyaa shihilɛ hu ni mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ bahi.
Yarafeemɔ kusum lɛ ji kusum ko ni saa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ he- gbonyo lɛ tete po fata he.
Ato nifeemɔ lɛ naa, shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee.
Yarafeemɔ henɔi: Maŋ fɛɛ maŋ kɛ eyarafeemɔ henɔi kɛ bɔ ni amɛfeɔ amɛyara amɛhaa.
Fantemɛi yɛ Eyipa/sikasa, Dɛtseyi/fundahɔ kɛ fea.
(Tsɔɔlɔ kɛ shishitsɔɔlɔi atsɔɔ shishi yɛ amɛkusum naa)
Yarafeemɔ he sɛɛnamɔi: Yarafeemɔ he hiaa jogbaŋŋ yɛ maji amli.
Naa ehe sɛɛnamɔi lɛ ekomɛi:
Ehaa anyaa mɛi awalashihilɛ saji ahe titri lɛ nibii ni mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ bafee.
Kɛji aaye afi lɛ, agbaa mɛi awalashihilɛ mli saji kɛtsɔɔ lalai kɛ wiemɔi amli ni akɛkaŋfoɔ mɛi ni egboi lɛ.
Eji hegbɛ ni anaa akɛ je lɛ jaa loo gbaa nibii ni amɛfee amɛha amɛmaŋ lɛ.
Yarafeemɔ waa ni egbeɔ dɔlɛ ni yɔɔ mɛi amli be mli ni amɛsuɔlɔ lɛ egbo sɛɛ.
Kɛji awo gbonyo lɛ hiɛ nyam yɛ eyarafeemɔ mli lɛ, mɛi kaiɔ eshihilɛ kpakpa.
Yarafeemɔ haa mɛi yeɔ awerɛho yɛ gbɛ kpakpa nɔ ni mɛi nuɔ he ehaa amɛhe.
Ehaa mɛi yeɔ awerɛho kutuu, ni etsɔɔ tsakpaa ni ka wekumɛi ateŋ nakai beiaŋ.
Yarafeemɔ haa wekumɛi kɛ nanemɛi kpeɔ ekoŋŋ.
Yara shishi ji he ko ni mɛi bakpeɔ yɛ, ni etsɔɔ tsakpaa ni amɛyɔɔ, ni no yafeɔ yelikɛbuamɔ nɔ yɛ be ni amɛhiaa amɛhe titri lɛ.
Yarafeemɔ yafeɔ adesa naagbee kusum yɛ shikpɔŋ nɛɛ nɔ.
Srɔto ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ kusum henɔi lɛ ateŋ yɛ Ghana maji lɛ srɔtoi lɛ anɔ: Naa bɔ ni Gamɛi feɔ amɛyara amɛhaa.
(Tsɔɔlɔi atɔɔ bɔ ni afeɔ lɛ yɛ amɛkusum naa ni amɛkɛto bɔ ni Ghana maji krokomɛi lɛ feɔ lɛ lɛ he)
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Gbele He Kusumii Onukpai kpeɔ kɛji mɔ lɛ gbee shi nɔŋŋ.
Amrɔ ni mɔ lɛ gbo lɛ, akɛɛɛ
Ajajeɔ gbonyo lɛ ni ajuɔ ehe.
Ashɛɔ eyi ni ajaleɔ ahe.
Ashaa egbɔmɔtso he ni ajaleɔ ehe (kɛjɛ enane shishi kɛyaa ŋwɛi).
Afɔɔ lɛ abonuɔ ni atsumɔɔ ehe (abonua lɛ gbɔjɔɔ gbonyo lɛ).
Ashaa lɛ sawie ni awoɔ lɛ agblama (Pɔɔda).
Atsɛɔ lɛ tɛklɛ (kɛ yoo ni lɛ – bue, kɛ nuu ni lɛ - agu) ni asaa ehe dani akɛ lɛ fɔɔ shi.
No sɛɛ lɛ aboɔ yaafo.
Sɛɛ mli dani awoɔ lɛ kɛyaa mɔtsire.
Kaimɔ Tsutsu ko lɛ, ataa gbonyo lɛ shi yɛ tsu ko mli.
Aku nu futuɔ kaŋfa ni akɛmaa enaa kɛbɛjeɔ lɛ.
Sisatsɛmɔ (Sisa Kplekemɔ) Wekumɛi yatsɛɔ gbonyo lɛ sisi kɛtao nɔ pɛtɛɛ ni gbe lɛ.
Kɛji loomɔ lɛ, awoɔ nu (baa) kɛshwaa gbonyo lɛ he dani ajuɔ ehe.
Kotsagbamɔ Eji klɛŋklɛŋ kusum ni afeɔ haa gbonyo lɛ dani ajuɔ ehe haa lɛ.
Afeɔ yɛ tsɛmɛi awe.
Wekumɛi ni kpeɔ kɛgbaa kotsa lɛ.
Wu loo ŋa kɛ wekumɛi yɛ nibii ni amɛkɛbaa.
Atoɔ nibii lɛ ashwieɔ saa nɔ.
Atsɛrɛɔ kotsai lɛ amli ni afolɔɔ abonuai lɛ amli ni akɛyaa hejuuhe.
Tsɛsɛɛ haa abonoai kɛ sawie.
Nyɛsɛɛ haa kotsa (taakotsa akwabii ejwɛ) bosaa kɛ abonuai
Ŋa haa kotsa kɛ samlia, sawie kɛ abonuai, krɔbɔ kɛ ayilɔ loo mlɔgo, mama shishi kɛ ŋwɛi (adasa).
Wu haa hɛlɛnii kɛ kuumɔ kpaa, tɛklɛ, adeka kɛ atade.
Kaimɔ Bii ji mɛi ni fuɔ amɛtsɛ adeka yɛ kusum naa.
Ni yoo ni bɛ wu kɛ bii lɛ, etsɛsɛɛ weku fuɔ lɛ adeka
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Gbonyo Hejuu Ayeɔ ŋkpai kɛjeɔ gbonyo lɛ hejuu shishi.
Ahaa okulafoyoo lɛ mɔɔ ewu nane waobii (gɔŋti) ni ajuɔ ahaa lɛ.
Akpaa gbonyo lɛ krɔbɔ kɛ ayilɔ.
No sɛɛ lɛ, ashishwuaa dɛŋ kɛkɛ lɛ, akɛbu gbonyo lɛ musu nɔ (akɛhaa gbonyo lɛ due) kɛkɛ lɛ asaa lɛ afɔ shi.
Kɛtrɛ - Gbɛjianɔtoo ko ni ato yɛ yara nɔ ni atsɔɔ nɔ kɛheɔ fuu (shika)
Faafoo – Kɛji asaa gbonyo he lɛ, wekumɛi foɔ lɛ faa – Ahaa shika nigbonyo lɛ kɛfo faa kɛya gbohiajeŋ.
Tsɛlɛgbamɔ Eji kusum ni akɛtseɔ hiɛkalɔ kɛ gbonyo teŋ.
Ahaa nuu onukpa ni toɔ nɛkɛ kusum nɛɛ.
Agbaa tsɛrɛ mli enyɔ kɛji aaye ŋkpai.
Afɔɔ kɛshwieɔ gbonyo lɛ hiɛ.
Akpaa gbɛ akɛ ekome abu shi ni okome hu ata shi.
Afamɔɔ mɛi ejwɛ (hii sɔŋŋ) kɛjɛɔ tsɛsɛɛ weku mli.
Ahaa hiɛkalɔ lɛ kɔɔ nɔ ni taa shi lɛ ni ekpeɔ.
Akɛ nɔ ni bu shi lɛ haa gbonyo lɛ kɛhaa.
Gbonyo Nɔ Womɔ Awoɔ gbonyo lɛ adeka mli.
Ayeɔ ŋkpai ni awoɔ enɔ (Akɛ adeka lɛ saa shi shii etɛ).
Akɛ daa tɔ kome nyiɛɔ gbonyo lɛ hiɛ ni ayafuɔ lɛ.
Gɔɔŋyaa Atereɔ gbonyo lɛ (eniji yajeɔ hiɛ ni eyitso yajeɔ sɛɛ).
Shianyo ko kɛ enine abɛku saa adeka lɛ najiashi he shii etɛ kɛhaa gbonyo lɛ kpoo.
Abayaŋ Mama bibioo ni yaratsɛmɛi kɛfiɔ amɛnineshi (abɛku gbɛ).
Akɛwooo dɛŋ, akɛŋmiɛɔ shikpɔŋ ni akɔɔ kɛ nine abɛku.
Mɛi ni yara lɛ kɔɔ amɛhe waa ji mɛi ni fiɔ.
Gbele Sɛɛ Kusum
Saisaramɔ: Atsuɔ nɛkɛ kusum nɛɛ gbi ni afu gbonyo lɛ nɔ jetsɛremɔ.
Blema ko lɛ, akɛ daa yaa gɔɔ mli.
Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ, wekumɛi kpeɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai.
Akɔŋtaabuu: Eji kusum ni afeɔ kɛbuɔ gbonyo lɛ he akɔŋtaa.
Kɛji mɔ ko naaa gbɛ ni efu gbonyo lɛ, enyɛɔ efuɔ lɛ nɛ beaŋ.
Saanɔjiemɔ: Eji kusum ni afeɔ haa nuu ni ŋa egbo dani ekɛ yoo kroko hiɔ shi.
Atsɛɔ yoo lɛ susuma ni abiɔ lɛ gbɛ.
Kaimɔ Atsɛɔ yoo lɛ akɛ kuayeliŋa.
Aka shika kɛ abonua kpawo (7) kɛ daa yaa he ni amɛyɔɔ lɛ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai.
Ayaa ŋshɔnaa ni atsuɔ nɛkɛ kusum nɛɛ.
Yarayoo nukpa ni tsuɔ kusum nɛɛ.
Ekaneɔ ŋshɔ kei kpawo ni etsiɔ nuu lɛ ewoɔ ŋshɔ lɛ mli kɛkɛ lɛ nuu lɛ eje kpo.
Afeɔ nifeemɔ nɛɛ shii etɛ, akaɛ abonua lɛ kpaa nuu lɛ he.
Ashiɔ ŋshɔ lɛ naa ni nuu lɛ yajuɔ ehe kɛkɛ lɛ aye ŋkpai kɛmu kusum lɛ naa.
Okulafeemɔ: Kɛji yoo ko wu gbo lɛ, atoɔ be ni afeɔ okula haa yoo lɛ.
Anyɛɔ akɛ yoo lɛ haa nuu lɛ nyɛmi uu ni ekwɛ bii lɛ.
Atsɛɔ kusum nɛɛ kuayeli.
Niaaŋkwɛmɔ: Atsuɔ nɛkɛ kusum nɛɛ Ju, Soo kɛɛ Hɔɔ yɛ tsɛmɛi awe.
Aje shishi shwane aafee ŋmɛlɛ akpaa gbɛkɛ ŋmɛji ekpaa pɛpɛɛpɛ.
Gboshinii Eji kusum ni afeɔ dani ajara mɔ ni egbo lɛ jwetri loo nibii ni eshi yɛ esɛɛ.
Ayeɔ ŋkpai kɛtsɔɔ nɔ kɛbiɔ sisa lɛ gbɛ bɔ ni afee ni eŋmɛ gbɛ kɛha enibii amlikwɛmɔ.
Akɛbaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ nibii ayi ni eka gbɛ.
Ehaa bii naa hiɛnɔkamɔ yɛ jeŋ.
Eyeɔ bua bii kɛnaa amɛdɛŋ nii ni amɛkɛ eko tsuɔ nii.
Okulafo lɛ tsɔɔ nɔ kɛnaa kwɛmɔ kpakpa.
Nibii jaa kɛhaa bi hii titri lɛ haa gboshinii lɛ hiɔ weku lɛ mli nɔŋŋ.
Bii kɛ amɛfɔlɔ hiɛ shi jogbaŋŋ bɔ ni afee ni amɛgboshinii lɛ akɛbɔ amɛ yɛ fɔlɔi gbele sɛɛ.
Naa bɔ ni Fantemɛi hu feɔ amɛnɔ lɛ amɛhaa: Dɛtseyi/fundahɔ Enɛ ji kusum ni afeɔ kɛjɛ be ni mɔ ko baatsɔɔ esɛɛ kɛyashi efuu.
Blɛma beiaŋ lɛ, kɛji mɔ ko gbo lɛ, maŋtsɛ ni yɔɔ maŋ loo akutso lɛ mli lɛ klɛŋklɛŋ toi nɔ ayawoɔ.
No sɛɛ dani akɛɔ maŋbii kɛ mɔ ni egbo lɛ wekumɛi.
Akuraa gbonyo lɛ be fioo koni wekumɛi kɛ nakai be lɛ asaa amɛhe.
Yɛ gbonyo lɛ kuramɔ mli lɛ, anyɛɔ aŋaa amɛmli nu.
Be komɛi hu lɛ, ahaa gbonyo lɛ daa ni naa wa koni emusuŋ nibii lɛ akafite.
Akɛwoɔ lɛ tsofa hu.
Eji gbonyo lɛ bii lɛ agbɛnaa akɛ amɛju amɛha amɛfɔlɔ lɛ shi moŋ, yei onukpai ni yɔɔ weku lɛ hu baanyɛ afee.
Yɛ fɛɛ mli lɛ, esa akɛ bi nukpa lɛ kɛ nu shwieɔ enɔ klɛŋklɛŋ dani bibii lɛ anyiɛ sɛɛ.
Ashɛɔ gbonyo lɛ yitsɔi ni ajieɔ ewaonaa.
Akɛ nu la juɔ ehaa gbonyo lɛ shii etɛ.
Dani abaaju gbonyo lɛ he lɛ, weku lɛ kɛ kotsa, papam, samla, paada kɛ hejuu nibii krokomɛi baa ni akɛjuɔ ahaa lɛ.
Kɛji aju aha lɛ ata lɛ, asaa ehe ni akɛ lɛ fɔɔ saa nɔ.
Mɛi / kui srɔtoi baa ni amɛbashiɔ kpe kɛyashiɔ enɔ jetsɛremɔ be ni sa akɛ ayafu gbonyo lɛ.
Nibii ni akɛfuɔ gbonyo: Fantemɛi heɔ amɛyeɔ akɛ gbele lɛ gbɛfaa ni, ni mɔ ni egbo lɛ yaatsa eshihilɛ lɛ nɔ yɛ gbohiiajeŋ.
Enɛ hewɔ lɛ, amɛhaa lɛ nibii fɛɛ ni ehe hiaa gbɛfalɔ.
Ebii lɛ haa adeka ni akɛfuɔ lɛ.
Ahaa la saa, saanɔbo, sune, pioto, mamai kɛ shika fɛɛ.
Suɔlɔi krokomɛi kɛ nibii srɔtoi baaha gbonyo lɛ ni amɛkɛ lɛ yeɔ shɛɛ.
Akɛ nibii nɛɛ woɔ gbonyo adeka lɛ mli.
Fuu: Kɛji afee kusumi nɛɛ ata lɛ, ayeɔ ŋkpai ni akɛ gbonyo lɛ woɔ adeka lɛ mli koni ayafu lɛ.
Hii ni yɔɔ shia lɛ mli lɛ tereɔ gbonyo lɛ kɛyaa gɔɔ lɛ mli, wekumɛi nyiɛɔ sɛɛ ni ayatoɔ lɛ.
Kɛji afu lɛ ata lɛ, abaa shia.
No sɛɛ lɛ abɔɔ gwa ni mɛi baataa shishi ni akeɔ nii.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Eyipa loo sikasa Kɛji mɔ ko gbo lɛ, gbii nyɔŋmai ejwɛ sɛɛ dani afeɔ eyara.
Afɔɔ yarafeemɔ Hɔɔ kɛ Soo.
Bianɛ lɛ, akɛ okpɔlɔi mamɔɔ shi kɛha nikeehe mɔ.
Ajeɔ jara lɛ shishi shwane ŋmɛlɛ kome mli.
Kɛji ato be kɛha yara lɛ, ashiɔ abalai kɛhaa gbɔi ni baaba yara lɛ shishi lɛ.
Mɛi komɛi kɛ amɛnaji foji nyiɛɔ.
Alaa be mli ni ayiɔ mii.
Weku ni agbo yɛ amɛnɔ lɛ taa hiɛ.
Kɛji mɛi baŋa amɛ eta lɛ, ahaa amɛ he ko amɛtaa ni akpeɔ amɛ kɛ ŋamɔi kɛ daji.
Kɛji aha amɛ daa eta lɛ, amɛkeɔ nii.
Yaratsɛmɛi lɛ yaŋaa amɛ ekoŋŋ kɛtsɔɔ akɛ amɛhiɛ esɔ amɛhe.
Ŋmɛji ekpaa mli lɛ, akpaa yara lɛ.
Enɔ jetsɛremɔ leebi lɛ, amɛbuaa amɛhe naa ekoŋŋ koni amɛhe nikee.
Be komɛi lɛ akɛ nibii komɛi haa mɛi ni ba yara lɛ.
Kɛji mɔ ni egbo lɛ shi eŋa yɛ ɛɛ lɛ, akɛ lɛ tsɔɔ okulafo kusum lɛ mli.
Afeɔ eyipa loo sikasa kusum lɛ kɛji afu gbɔmɔ lɛ momo.
Fea Fea ji yara ni afeɔ ahaa bi klɛŋklɛŋ ni gboɔ yɛ fɔlɔ ko bii ateŋ.
Akɛ tsɛnsi bibioo ko ni maa shi kɛha nikee.
Ashiɛɛɛ nikee lɛ.
Aheɔ ayeɔ akɛ kɛji afee nɛkɛ yara nɛɛ jogbaŋŋ lɛ, gbele mumɔ lɛ mii baashɛ ehe ni ebaabaŋɔ bii krokomɛi ni eshwɛ lɛ hu kɛya, Afooo pii, ayeee nii pii ni anyaaa gbonyo lɛ he hu.
Tsakemɔi ni eba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ mli Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ atsake yarafeemɔ hiɛ yɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kui srɔtoi lɛ babaoo amli, atsake kusum lɛ hiɛ ni afutu lɛ kɛ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ nibii ni ebaye nɔ lɛ.
Yarafeemɔ he nyɔmɔbɔɔ: ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ, yarafeemɔ yara ewa.
Mɛi tsɔɔ nɔ kɛjieɔ amɛhe kpo, shika ni amɛyɔɔ kɛ gbɛnaa ni amɛhiɛ.
Jeee gbonyo lɛ hiɛnyamwoo akwɛɔ dɔŋŋ shi moŋ koni maŋbii ale weku lɛ gbɛnaa yɛ maŋ lɛ mli.
Tɛkinɔlɔji: Akɛ tɛknɔlɔji etsake nibii pii ahiɛ yɛ yarafeemɔ mli, ajieɔ yara lɛ yɛ t.v nɔ, afɔɔ nine atsɛɔ mɛi yɛ intanɛti lɛ nɔ ni ashiɛɔ yara lɛ tete po yɛ nɔ ni mɛi pii naa he anuɔ.
Enɛ hewɔ lɛ, mɛi efaa gbɛ kɛjɛɛɛ shɔŋŋ kɛ amɛhe ebaawooo yarafeemɔ mli dɔŋŋ.
Akɛ kusum kɛ kristofoi ajamɔŋ hemɔkɛyeli efutu yarafeemɔ: Bianɛ lɛ, afutu yara kusum nifeemɔ kɛ kristofoi ajamɔŋ mli hemɔkɛyelii, eyafeɔ sɔlemɔ ahaa gbonyo lɛ yɛ sɔlemɔtsui amli, kpeshii he saji kn.kn.
Nɛkɛ futumɔ nɛɛ tsɔɔ he ni gbonyo lɛ jɛ kɛ jamɔ mli ni Ghanabii pii yɔɔ.
Jarayeli: Bianɛ lɛ mɛi tsɔɔ yarafeemɔ nɔ kɛfeɔ shika.
Nihoolɔi, gbonyo tsu mli saalɔi, lalatswalɔi, gbonyo adeka kpɛlɔi kɛ mɛi ni woɔ mɛi anaa ŋmɔlɔ naa shika waa yɛ yarafeemɔ mli.
Nɛkɛ nifeemɔ nɛɛ yɛ nɔnyɛɛ kpakpa kɛ egbonyo fɛɛ yɛ yarafeemɔ nɔ.
Ŋaa kpojiemɔ: Yarafeemɔ ebatsɔɔ he ni mɛi jieɔ amɛŋaa kpo yɛ, mɛi ni joɔ shikwɛɛ joo, ni amɛyiɔ mii kɛ lalai srɔtoi afolɔi, mɛi ni kɛɔ lalawiemɔ kɛ mɛi ni tɛŋɔ nii tete baa shishi.
Atsɔɔ enɛmɛi anɔ akɛwoɔ gbonyo lɛ hiɛ nyam.
Weku kɛ maŋ lɛ gbɛnaa tsakemɔ: Yarafeeemɔ tsɔɔ maŋ kɛ weku tsakpaa shi moŋ, maŋ kɛ weku gbɛnaa yɛ yarafeemɔ mli etsake yɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ amɛhe woɔ yarafeemɔ lɛ mli kɛ shika gbɛkpamɔ.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Nikasemɔ Nitsumɔ
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔyɛɛ ni ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ nifeemɔi yɔɔ yɛ:
weku kɛ nanemɛi ni agbo yɛ amɛnɔ lɛ.
Gbalamɔ susumɔ nɛɛ mli otsɔɔ akɛ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ nifeemɔi he hiaa yɛ shikwɛɛ kusum nifeemɔi lɛ amli.
Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbɛi
Nyɛtia gbele kɛ ehe saji lɛ amli.
Wiemɔ yarafeemɔ kɛ ehenɔi ni yɔɔ kusum lɛ mli lɛ ahe.
Wiemɔ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ ahaa mɛi nɛɛ: maŋtsɛmɛi, wɔŋtsɛmɛi, yei hɔlɔi, gbekɛbii kɛ mɛi foji.
Wiemɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ tsakemɔi ni eba yarafeemɔi amli lɛ.
Ku nitsumɔ: Nyɛkɛ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ yɛ Ghana maji srɔtoi lɛ anɔ lɛ atotoa amɛhe ni nyɛtsɔa srɔto kɛ nibii ni haa kusumi lɛ kɛ amɛhe yeɔ hegbɔ.
Kwɛmɔ yarafeemɔ henɔi srɔtoi lɛ ahe viidio.
Nyɛkɛ yarafeemɔ kusum srɔtoi lɛ awoɔ shwɛmɔ mli.
Gbaa yarafeemɔ henɔi enyɔ ni afeɔ yɛ okutsoŋ lɛ he sane ni otsɔɔ sɛɛnamɔ ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ kusumi nɛɛ ahe.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ kusumi ni afeɔ ahaa mɛi nɛɛ yɛ okutso lɛ mli: maŋtsɛmɛi, wɔŋtsɛmɛi, yeihɔlɔi kɛ gbekɛbii.
Kɛ yara ni afeɔ yɛ okutso lɛ mli lɛ ato yara ni afeɔ yɛ akutso kroko mli he.
Tsɔɔmɔ naagba kome ni yɔɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ kusum nifeemɔi lɛ ahe yɛ okutso lɛ mli ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni abaanyɛ amɔ naa.
Daamɔ nibii ni akɛsusuɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ ni okɛsusu Ga vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ (N.K., lilɛi shikamɔ, naabufeemɔ, lilɛi henɔwomɔ)
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ GA NIŊMAA MLAI
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ ŊAAŊ SAJI 1.
Tsɔɔmɔ nitsumɔ lalao, tayaa lalai, shwɛmɔ lalai kɛ abifaowɔlemɔ lala 1 1 WOJIAŊ SAJI 1.
Abaaha saneŋmaa he sanebimɔi ejwɛ ni esa akɛ kaselɔi aŋma sanŋmaa ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii aaafee 300.
Abaahara kaa sanebimɔi lɛ yɛ henɔi ni baa nɛɛ ashishi.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, abaaha sanebimɔi etɛ yɛ jeŋ saji henɔi lɛ ashishi (adesa, shwɛmɔ kɛ lalawiemɔ) koni kaselɔi akɔ emli ekome.
Feemɔ agwaseiaŋ woloŋmaa WIEMƆSHISIHTSƆƆMƆ KƐ SANESHISHITSƆƆMƆ
otii, wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu nii, kukuji, liamɔ, wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, shitiimɔ k.n.k.n.) TOTAL 1 10
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ ITEM SPECIFICATION TABLE FOR PAPER THREE-SHS GHANAIAN LANGUAGE Wolo lɛ jebɔ PAPER 3 (Sanegbaa) Kaa nɛɛ hiɛ mlijaai etɛ (3) ni kaselɔi lɛ kɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ sanegbaa hu baahi mli.
Gbalamɔ susumɔi kɛ otii ni ahara kɛjɛ sanegbai mli.
nifeemɔ kpakpai (mɔbuu, toiŋjɔlɛ, suɔmɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi), sukuu tsɔsemɔ, GESI, STEM k.n.k.n) FƆNƆLƆJI Daamɔ nibii ni akɛsusuɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi lɛ ni okɛsusu Ga kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi lɛ (n.k.
gbeekpaa shikamɔ, he ni asɔ yɛ kɛ bɔ ni kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ jeɔ kpo ehaa) NIKANEMƆ 1.

Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Mlijaa 8 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛ nibii ni akase yɛ otsii nyɔŋma kɛ kpawo kɛ nyɔŋma kɛ kpaanyɔ lɛ mli.
Awie gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ kusumi lɛ ahe, amɛhenɔi kɛ sɛɛnamɔi.
Ebaawo kaselɔi ahewalɛ koni amɛfee ekome kɛ nya kusumi nifeemɔi ahe.
Ekɛ tsakpaanamɔ loo naanyobɔɔ baa Kɛji eŋma otii etɛ ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ mli lɛ - mii 3 Kɛji eŋma otii lɛ mli enyɔ lɛ - mii 2 Kɛji eŋma otii mli ekome lɛ - mi 1 Kwɛmɔ hesalɛ ni atsi ta yɛ sabimɔ 1a mli lɛ ni oha hesalɛi ni ajie kpo yɛ sukuu kadaaŋgbamɔ lɛ mli lɛ mii
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ OTSI 18 Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
OTI: JEŊNILEE YƐ GHANA SHIKWƐƐ WIEMƆ LƐ MLI Oti Mlijaa: Ŋaaŋ Saji Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalai ahe sui lɛ asɔɔ lalai Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Atsɔɔ nilee kɛ shishinumɔ ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalai ahe HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ wieɔ obɔade lalai tritri lɛ nitsumɔ kɛ shwɛmɔi lalai ahe.
Ekwɛɔ nɔ ni nitsumɔ lalai ji, ehenɔi kɛ ehe sɛɛnamɔi.
Ekwɛ shwɛmɔ lalai hu, ewieɔ ehenɔi, jebɔ, sɛɛnamɔi kɛ bɔ ni feɔ ni anyaa lalai nɛɛ ahe.
Nilee ni yɔɔ nitsumɔ kɛ shwɛmɔ lalai ahe lɛ baawa kaselɔi ni amɛhebaasa kɛji amɛmiifo amɛdiɛŋtsɛ amɛ lalai.
Nɛkɛ mlijaa nɛɛ he yɛ sɛɛnamɔi kɛha kaselɔi jeee yɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ wiemɔ kasemɔ mli pɔtɛɛ shi ekɛ tsakpaa hu baa lala kɛ jeŋnilee nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Mlijaa lɛ hu haa kaselɔi anilee ni mli kwɔ kɛ shishinumɔ yɛ amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛkusum kɛ ŋaa naa.
Abi ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ana nilee yɛ gbɛjianɔtoo, nibii, tsakemɔi kɛ kaa he ŋai ni yeɔ ebuaa kaselɔi lɛ.
Otsii kɛ nɔkwɛmɔi ni mlijaa lɛ wie he:
Otsi 20: Shwɛmɔ lalai NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
Mlijaa 9: LalaiMlijaa 9: Lalai Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi leɔ kaselɔi ni yɔɔ nitsɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔi amli naagbai koni amɛtsɔɔ ŋaagbɛi anɔ kɛku susumɔi ni ejaaa fɛɛ anaa yɛ klasi lɛ mli.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi anishishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ shikwɛɛ afiyeli yɛ Ghana nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Kaa ni kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo yaa Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi DoK Ŋɛlɛ Kaa henɔ L1.1.4.4.1 Awie nitsumɔ lalai ahe 4 Sanegbaa L1.2.3.4.1 Atao ni anya ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ shwɛmɔ lalai ahe.
Mlijaa 9: Lalai OTSI 19 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi: Awie nitsumɔ lalai ahe OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: NITSUMƆ LALAI
Lalai amligbalamɔ: Lala ji ni wiemɔi ni asɔɔ ni akɛ gbee laa.
Gbee nɛɛ hiɔ mɛlɔdi ni gbee henɔi kɛ dioofeemɔ yɔɔ mli.
Lalai yɛ lalawiemɔ sui lɛ eko.
Wiemɔheŋɔɔwolɔi yɛ fɛɛ mli ni amɛyɛ ‘rhyme’ .
Aŋmalaa lalai yɛ liamɔi kɛ kukuji amli- atiɔ amɛmli yɛ lala lɛ mli.
Liamɔi lɛ asɛɛ kɛlɛɛɛ ni abaanyɛ ati amɛmli.
Wiemɔi ni yɔɔ lalai amli hiɛ shishinumɔi ni mli kwɔ ni amaa amɛ nɔ mi.
Nitsumɔ lala: Nitsumɔ lala ji lalai ni kɛ nitsumɔi hiɛ tsakpaa.
Alaa kɛji aatsu nɔ ko, loo nitsumɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ he lala ni asɔ, loo lala ni wieɔ eshiɔ nibii.
Alaa nitsumɔ lalai kɛji aatsu nii tamɔ wuoyaa, okwaayeli, jarayeli kɛ sɔŋmɛ nitsumɔ koni akɛkanya nitsulɔi ni amɛtsu nii lɛ oya loo amɛtsu kɛtsɛ.
(Nitsumɔ lalai, henɔi kɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii lɛ, soro maŋ fɛɛ maŋ kɛ enɔ.
Saneshishitsɔɔlɔi/tsɔɔlɔi atsɔɔ shishi/atsɔɔ lɛ bɔ ni eyɔɔ yɛ lɛ amɛkusum naa)
Nitsumɔ lalai ahenɔi: Nɔ ni gbaa sane, nɔ ni akɛsusuɔ nibii atsɔɔ, lalai ni akɛwieɔ ashiɔ nibii .
Nitsumɔ Lalai yɛ Ga mli kwaayeli: Kosɛɛ duade Kosɛɛ duade ahuuu eee Kosɛɛ duade ahuuu eeee Shi ebɔ momo 2x Agwao, agwao agwao duade agwao 2x Ha mana eko maye 2x Ataa Kofi oo Agwao, agwao agwao duade agwao 2x Wuoyaa Wuolɛi kɛ loo eba eee 2x Nyɛhaa wɔyaa okwaafoi aŋɔɔ, Wɔyaŋɔ duade Wɔbaaye fufui eee Wɔmiiha nyɛ ayekoo.
Amɛwoɔ nitsulɔi ahewalɛ ni ewaa amɛ amɛgbeɔ amɛnitsumɔ naa oya.
Wiemɔi ni yɔɔ lalai lɛ amli lɛ woɔ nitsulɔi ahewalɛ ni amɛbɔɔ nitsumɔ lɛ he mɔdɛŋ.
Atsɔɔ nɔ akɛtswaa nitsumɔ lɛ ahe adafi.
Akɛ nitsumɔ lalai jieɔ nitsulɔi ayi koni egbala mɛi krokomɛi hu.
Nitsumɔ lalai haa gbeyei mɔmɔɔ mɛi fɔji ni baato amɛyiŋ akɛ amɛbaapila nitsulɔi lɛ.
Yɛ bɔ ni nibii yaa nɔ yɛ okusum lɛ mli lɛ, te obaatsɔɔ nitsumɔ lala shishi oha tɛŋŋ?
Haramɔ nɔkwɛmɔnii etɛ yɛ okusum lɛ mli ni ogbalaa nitsumɔ lalai henɔi lɛ amli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ ahewɔ ni nitsulɔi ni yɔɔ okutso lɛ mli lɛ kɛ nitsumɔ lalai tsuɔ nii.
Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai
Nikasemɔ he sanegbaa: Klasi muu fɛɛ
Nyɛwiea nitsumɔ lala henɔi lɛ ahe
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Akwɛ nitsumɔ lalai amli wiemɔ (ahara otii, akwɛ jebɔ, wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, wiemɔ kɛ ŋaa, kn.kn)
Nifeemɔ mli nikasemɔ: Foo ni ola nitsumɔ lala.
Haramɔ nitssumɔ lalai henɔi enyɔ kɛjɛ okusum lɛ mli koni owie ejebɔ, eshikamɔ, (rhythm, mɛlɔdi) kɛ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsu nii lɛ mli (no ni, tamɔ no, oookɛɛ gbɔmɔ, mfoniri, kɔnsonanti nɔtoonɔtoo, okadii kɛ nii kɛ nii).
Wiemɔ nibii etɛ ni nitsumɔ lalai feɔ yɛ okutso lɛ mli.
Kɛtsɔ onilee ni oyɔɔ yɛ jeŋnilee saji ahe lɛ, sɔɔmɔ nitsumɔ lala kɛha nitsumɔ ni obaasumɔ akɛ otsu wɔ sɛɛ lɛ.
Mlijaa 9: Lalai OTSI 20 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Awie shwɛmɔ lalai ahe OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SHWƐMƆ LALAI Shwɛmɔ Lalai Shwɛmɔ lalai lɛ alaa yɛ shwɛmɔ be mli.
Blema lɛ, Ga yei kɛ hii fɛɛ yɛ shwɛmɔi babaoo ni kɛjiajɛ ŋmɔ kɛ wuo aba shia ni je ena ni nɔ ko feemɔ bɛ lɛ, ayaa blohuiaŋ loo ataraa koishi loo kpo teŋ ni ashwɛɔ.
Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ, nakai shihilɛ lɛ bɛ dɔŋŋ ejaakɛ nibii babaoo etsake shi moŋ oboade nifeemɔi yɛ, mɛi ni efeee.
Ga yei shwɛɔ adaawe ni amɛtuɔ ampe ni hii tswaa ntoosa, amɛshiɔ amɛjo ni amɛshwɛɔ ajamanaa kɛ shwɛmɔi srɔtoi babaoo.
Adaawe ji yei ashwɛmɔi lɛ eko ni shwɛmɔ nɛɛ mli lamɔ fa naakpa.
Oblayei lɛ laa lalai srɔtoi ni ekomɛi kɔɔ gbalashihilɛ mli ŋɔɔmɔi kɛ joomɔi ahe.
Oblahii ni bɛkɛŋɔ shwɛmɔ nɛɛ naa hegbɛ kɛnaa amɛhefatalɔi.
Yɛ lalai lɛ alamɔ mli lɛ, mɔ ko woɔ lala lɛ ni fabii lɛ hereɔ nɔ.
Mɔ ni woɔ lala lɛ ji Boma ni mɛi ni hereɔ nɔ ji Asafo.
Shwɛmɔ lalai srɔtoi komɛi Namɔ ye lɛ awui?
Boma: Namɔ ye lɛ awui?
N’atsɛ lɛ aha mi ei;
Dɔkita Bruce eye m’terema Dɔkita Bruce eye m’terema Dɔkita Bruce eye m’terema Dɔkita Bruce eye m’terema Timiŋtimiŋ nyɛmaale Timiŋtimiŋ, nyɛmaale, Nyɛmaale ee klikli, Klikli loo musuade, Musuade modua, Modua modua sakumɔ tɛtrɛtɛ, Ei ajeŋkuma kɛ Osamaŋkɔliŋ kɔŋŋ Ga yei nyɛɛɛ wiɛbi amɔ Boma: Adaawee!
Boma: Obi lɛ ta shi ha bo, Asafo: Shaayoo baaye nii ei, yoo!
Ekɛ ekomefeemɔ kɛ miihɛɛ baa.
Emli ni tsɔseɔ mɔ.
Mɛni ji shwɛmɔ lala?
Kɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ okusum lɛ mli atsɔɔ shwɛmɔ lala henɔi ni ayɔɔ.
Agba shwɛmɔ lalai ahe sane
Agba shwɛmɔ lalai henɔi lɛ ahe sane
Awie shwɛmɔi hesɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe.
Mlijaa 9 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ wie lalai ahe.
Atsɔɔ kaselɔi lala henɔi srɔtoi lɛ: nitsumɔ lala kɛ shwɛmɔ lala.
Wɔkɛ wɔhiɛ ma lalai, nitsumɔ lalai amligbalamɔ he.
Nilee ni ana yɛ lalai kɛ lalai asɔɔ lɛ baawa kaselɔi kɛna wiemɔkulibii he ni amɛna hesalɛ yɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ehaa yiŋkaa, wiemɔshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ niaŋpɛimɔ hesalɛ yaa hiɛ.
Kɛfata he lɛ, ehaa kaselɔi yɔseɔ amɛdromɔ nikenii ni amɛtsuɔ ehe nii kɛha amɛwɔsɛɛ he.
Mlijaa nɛɛ kɛ nikanemɔi krokomɛi tamɔ yinɔ saji akasemɔ, jeŋ nilee kɛ lala yɛ tsakpaa.
Mlijaa nɛɛ haa wɔnuɔ he haa mɛi ni wɔnyɛɔ wɔtsɔɔ wɔjwɛŋmɔ yɛ blema nifeemɔ, kusum nifeemɔ kɛ akutsoŋ nifeemɔi amli.
Lalai asɔɔ kɛ emɔmɔ yɛ yitsoŋ lɛ haa wɔnaa hesalɛ yɛ nikaimɔ, agbo naa wiemɔ kɛ hiɛkaa yɛ wiemɔ mli mli.
Awo tsɔɔlɔ lɛ hewalɛ ni ekɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi, yelikɛbuamɔ nibii kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛ srɔtoi atsu nii kɛye ebua kaselɔi.
Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
OTI: JEŊ NILEE Oti Mlijaa: WOJIAŊ SAJI Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalawiemɔi ahe lɛ anya lalawiemɔi ahe Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ lalawiemɔi ahe kpo atsɔɔ HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAALƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ wieɔ wojiaŋ asaji he.
Wojiaŋ asaji henɔ ni abaawie ji lalawiemɔ.
Akɛ kaselɔi baatsɔ lalawiemɔ henyamɔ otii lɛ amli.
Abaawie lalawiemɔ henyamɔ hu he.
Nɛkɛ mlijaa nɛɛ hɛe yɛ sɛɛnamɔi kɛha kaselɔi jeee yɛ Ghana wiemɔ kasemɔ mli pɔtɛɛ shi ekɛ tsakpaa hu baa lala kɛ jeŋnilee kasemɔ.
Mlijaa lɛ hu haa kaselɔi nilee ni mli kwɔ kɛ shishinumɔ yɛ amɛdiɛŋtsɛ amɛkusum kɛ ŋaa naa.
Abi ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ana nilee yɛ gbɛjianɔtoo, nibii, tsakemɔi kɛ kaa he ŋai ni yeɔ ebua kaselɔi lɛ.
Otsii kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ni mlijaa lɛ wieɔ he:
Otsi 21: Ayɔse ni awie lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ ahe (lalawiemɔ lɛ mli wiemɔi, otii, kukuji, liamɔi, wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, shitiimɔi kn.kn)
Otsi 23: Lalawiemɔi ahenyamɔ NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
Mlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi leɔ kaselɔi ni yɔɔ nitsɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔi amli naagbai koni amɛtsɔɔ ŋaagbɛi anɔ kɛku susumɔi ni ejaaa fɛɛ anaa yɛ klasi lɛ mli.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi aniishishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ Ga wiemɔ nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Yɛ Ŋɛlɛ 3 lɛ, ahaa niiahesusumɔ vii he kaa kɛtsɔɔ niŋmaa kukuji loo kakadaji anɔ kɛkaa kaselɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mlikwɔlɛ kɛ shishinumɔ ni amɛyɔɔ yɛ lalawiemɔ henyamɔ he kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ otii lɛ baatsu nii kɛnya lalawiemɔ ko he.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii koni amɛkɛna kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ, amɛmii kɛ hetoo kɛha amɛnikasemɔ muu lɛ nɔyaa.
Kaa ni kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo yaa Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ Kaa henɔ Ayɔse ni awie lalawiemɔ mli otii la ahe (nk., kukuji, liamɔi, wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi kn.kn) 3 Shia nitsumɔ Lalawiemɔi ahenyamɔ 4 Sukuu nitsumɔ Lalawiemɔi ahenyamɔ 4 Nitsumɔ
Mlijaa 10: LalawiemɔMlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 21 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Ayɔse ni awie lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ ahe (nk., lalawiemɔ lɛ mli wiemɔi, otii, kukuji, liamɔi, wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, shitiimɔi, kn.kn) OTI HE NI AWIEƆ: LALAWIEMƆ
Lalawiemɔ mli otii
Lalawiemɔ he sɛɛnamɔ Lalawiemɔ mligbalamɔ Lalawiemɔ ji niŋmaa ko ni akɛ wiemɔ tsuɔ nii yɛ ŋaa gbɛ nɔ kɛsɔɔ kɛjieɔ susumɔ, henumɔ loo yiŋtoo pɔtɛɛ ko kpo.
Eji gbɛ kple nɔ ni ŋmalɔi tsɔɔ nɔ kɛjieɔ amɛhesalɛ kpo ni amɛkɛ shɛɛwiemɔ pɔtɛɛ ko fɔɔ jaraŋ.
Bei pii lɛ, lalawiemɔi kɛ wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi tsuɔ nii, ekomɛi ji: Tamɔ no, No ni, abuomama kɛ mfonirishamɔ.
Wiemɔi ni akɛtsɔɔ jwɛŋmɔ
Shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ (akɛ wiemɔkulibii fioo ko pɛ kɛkɛ shaa nibii babaoo ahe mfoniri)
Akɛ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔi tsuɔ nii.
Lalawiemɔ mli otii Lalawiemɔ ni gbɛ srɔto ko nɔ ni atsɔɔ aŋmaa nii ni akɛ wiemɔheŋɔɔwolɔi babaoo tsuɔ nii kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ kɛ henumɔ kpo.
Mlijaa nɛɛ baakwɛ lalawiemɔ henɔi ejwɛ:
Lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ liamɔ nyɔŋma kɛ ejwɛ.
Lalawiemɔi ni hiɛ liamɔi kɛ kukuji srɔtoi abɔ ni ehiɛɛɛ gbɛjianɔtoo ko
Lalwiemɔ ni gbaa sane
Lalawiemɔ ni jieɔ henumɔ kpo Lalwiemɔ nitsumɔi krokomɛi
Mfonirishamɔ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni akɛ wiemɔ shaa nibii komɛi ahe mfoniri yɛ nikanelɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mli.
Enyɛɔ eyafeɔ nibii komɛi ni anaa ahe susumɔ kɛtsɔɔmɔ, gbɛɛmɔi, fu, nibii aŋɔɔmɔ kɛ joomɔ kɛ nibii asu.
Wiemɔkulibii aharamɔ: Eji wiemɔkulibii ni aharaa kɛsɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ jeɔ gbɛ eharaa wiemɔkulibii lɛ kɛ sɔɔ gbee srɔto ko ni mɔɔ kanelɔi lɛ ajwɛŋmɔ ni kɛ shɛɛwiemɔ lɛ hu ŋmɛɔ jara.
Kɛha anyɛ akɛ shishinumɔ lɛ aŋmɛ jaraŋ lɛ, lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛ nibii nɛɛ ŋmɛɔ tsuɔ nii:
Wiemɔkulibii haram ɔ: Eji wiemɔkulibii ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛtsuɔ ni kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ kɛ gbee lɛ kpo.
Gbee: Eji henɔumɔ ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛŋmaa lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Eji henumɔ kɛ jemba ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ tsɔ wiemɔkulibii nɔ kɛŋmɛ jara.
Bɔ ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ toɔ wiemɔkulibii lɛ anaa lɛ hu tsɔɔ gbee ni ekɛŋma lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Ŋaagbɛ: Wiemɔ ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ tsɔ kɛjie eyiŋtoo lɛ kpo.
Ebaanyɛ efee agwaseiaŋ nɔ, tipɛŋfoi anɔ, lalawiemɔ henɔ, loo sanegbaa nɔ.
Otii: Enɛ ji yiŋtoo, shɛɛwiemɔ loo nɔ pɛtɛɛ he ni lalawiemɔ lɛ wieɔ lɛ.
Kukuji: Eji liamɔi ni ajara amɛmli kɛwo kuui amli, ni yɛ hiɛ shitiimɔ henɔ kome.
Lalawiemɔfolɔi shiɔ gbɛ kɛjaraa kukuji lɛ eko fɛɛ eko amli.
Liamɔi : Eji wiemɔkulibii ni atsɛrɛ yɛ kpaa kome nɔ ni feɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Ekomɛi feɔ kakadaji ni ekomɛi hu feɔ kukuji.
Amɛnaagbee gbɛɛmɔi lɛ hu nyɛɔ efeɔ srɔto.
Liamɔi lɛ nyɛɔ efeɔ sanemuu, enyɛɔ efeɔ sanekuku loo wiemɔkulibii fioo ko pɛ hu.
Wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛ wiemɔ ni shishinumɔ bɛ hiɛhiɛ tsuɔ nii kɛteɔ henuɔ shi loo tsɔɔ jwɛŋmɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji no ni, tamɔ no, mfonirishamɔ, oookɛɛgbɔmɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Shitiimɔi : Eji ŋaa nɔ ni atsɔɔ nɔ akɛtsuɔ lalawiemɔ he nii, be pii lɛ atiɔ liamɔi komɛi amli ni eha lɛ lala su titri naagbee liamɔi lɛ.
Ehaa lalawiemɔ lɛ ŋɔɔ ni etsakeɔ ejebɔ lɛ hu.
Lalawiemɔ Jebɔ Lalawiemɔ lɛ jebɔ ji bɔ ni atoɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ naa.
Eji liamɔ, kukuji kɛ shitiimɔi.
Bei pii lɛ, lalawiemɔ jebɔ lɛ nyɛɔ eyeɔ ebuaa yɛ shishinumɔ muu kɛ gbee namɔ ni enaa nɔnyɛɛ yɛ kanelɔi anɔ.
Bei pii lɛ, lalawiemɔi kɛ jebɔi srɔtoi kɛjeɔ henumɔi kɛ susumɔi akpo yɛ gbɛ srɔto ko.
Jebɔi nɛɛ ekomɛi ji:
Shikamɔ: Bɔ ni lalawiemɔ lɛ yɔɔ ehaa kɛ eshikamɔ, tamɔ sonɛti (lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ liamɔi 14), lalawiemɔi aŋmaaa yɛ kukuji amli)
Liamɔi: Eji wiemɔkulibii ni atsɛrɛ yɛ kpaa kome nɔ ni feɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Shitiimɔ henɔ: Eji shiitiimɔi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli, titri lɛ yɛ liamɔ lɛ naagbee gbɛ.
Liamɔ mlitsɛrɛmɔ: Eji hei yɛ lalawiemɔ mli ni liamɔ hee tsaa nɔ yɛ he ni liamɔ ni eho lɛ gbeɔ naa yɛ lɛ.
Enɛ he hia yɛ lalawiemɔ mli ejaakɛ ehaa liamɔi lɛ tsaa nɔ ni lalawiemɔ lɛ hu yaa lɛ oyaoya.
Bei komɛi hu lɛ, lalawiemɔ mlitsɛrɛrmɔ tsɔɔ mumɔ shrao yɛ bei amli ni akɛ niŋmaa mli okadii tsuuu nii.
Mfonirishamɔ kɛ Agwaseiaŋ Wiemɔi: Mfonirishamɔ ŋaa gbɛ lɛ kɛ wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsuɔ nii lɛ susuɔ nibii ni awieɔ amɛhe lɛ etsɔɔ ni eteɔ henumɔ shi.
Kanemɔ ni onya lalawiemɔ kuku nɛɛ he ni ohara: Takwa jɛkɛ!
Oketeke lɛ baa nɛɛ;
Kpeŋleŋ-kpeŋleŋ, Kpeŋleŋ-kpeŋleŋ, Dade kɛ dade ŋnɔ;
Poko-poko, poko-poko, Lasu pukɔŋ faa nɛɛ!
Tsɔɔmɔ ni ogbala wiemɔheŋɔɔwolɔi ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli tamɔ:
Gbɛigbɛɛlɔ Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛi
Mɛni ji lalawiemɔ
Nyɛwiea lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ ahe
Nyɛwiea lalawiemɔ he sɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Nyɛwiea lalawiemɔ ko he
Mɔ kome nifeemɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ ahe lɛ asɔ lalawiemɔ.
Kaa Oti: DoK Ŋɛlɛ 3- Shia Nitsumɔ Daamɔ nilee ni ona yɛ lalawiemɔi ahe lɛ nɔ ni osɔ lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ kukuji etɛ yɛ otii nɛɛ ateŋ ekome nɔ
Mlijaa 10: LalawiemɔMlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 22 Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Lalawiemɔ henyamɔ Akwɛ nɔ ni eho Afi 1 Otsi 6: Agba mɔnaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve kn.kn.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: LALAWIEMƆ HENYAMƆ Kanemɔ nibii ni aŋmala yɛ lalawiemɔ he nyɛsɛɛ otsi lɛ ni okɛkai ohe.
Kanemɔ Lalawiemɔ l ɛ: Kanemɔ lalawiemɔ ni ahara lɛ shii abɔ ni onu emli wiemɔ lɛ, gbee (henumɔ ni akɛŋma lalawiemɔ loo wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu lɛ) shishi kɛ wiemɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ Otii lɛ: Tsɔɔmɔ otii ni lalawiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ.
Otii ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ nyɛɔ efeɔ;
toiŋjɔlɛ, suɔmɔ, hiɛmiamɔ, gbele, awerɛhoo kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Kpa mfonirii kɛ wiemɔi haŋtsii lɛ ahe mama: Kwɛmɔ bɔ ni akɛ mfonirishamɔ (henumɔ nibii), no ni, tamɔ no.
oookɛɛ gbɔmɔ, awohe, kɛ ekrokomɛi etsu nii lɛ.
Kwɛmɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ jebɔ: Nibii komɛi ni ahe hia yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ jebɔ lɛ mli ji:
Kukuji abɔ ni yɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Kwɛmɔ shitiimɔ henɔ lɛ.
Kɛ niŋmaa okadi ‘ A, B, D…’ kɛtsɔɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ shitiimɔ henɔi lɛ amli.
Kwɛmɔ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ nɛɛ nɔ Aflimata Aflimata bibioo ko A Ni hɔmɔ miiye lɛ B Esɔmɔ shi, esɔmɔ shi C Gɔbi gɔbi gɔbi, D Ni jwɛ, jwɛ, jwɛ ni etu.
Kwɛmɔ Wiemɔ kɛ Gbee ni akɛtsuu lɛ
Kwɛmɔ wiemɔ henɔ ni akɛtsuu lɛ.
Kɛji eji hiɛdɔɔ nɔ, tipɛŋfoi loo nɔ ni haa mɔ ko tsakeɔ ejwɛŋmɔ.
Kwɛmɔ gbee lɛ (subaŋ/henumɔ) – bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ obaaha nɔkwɛmɔnii!
Haramɔ Wiemɔ Heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi lɛ Taomɔ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi komɛi tamɔ:
Kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi: Eji kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ henɔ kome ni akɛje wiemɔkulibii ashishi ni tsara amɛhe nɔ yɛ liamɔ mli.
Vaoli gbɛɛmɔi: Eji vaoli gbɛɛmɔ henɔ kome ni akɛje wiemɔkulibii ashishi ni tsara amɛhe nɔ yɛ liamɔ.
Tamɔ no, kɛ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi krokomɛi ni akɛtsu nii yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Gbalam ɔ shishinumɔ lɛ mli Kadimɔ nibii nɛɛ:
Hiɛhiɛ shishinumɔ: Eji klɛŋklɛŋ shishinumɔ ni anaa.
Shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ: Eji shishinumɔ ni tɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii loo wiemɔkukuji ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ hiɛhiɛ shishinumɔ lɛ nɔ.
Ŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo: Ŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo lɛ ji oti ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛŋma lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Yiŋtoo lɛ gbalaa mɛi ajwɛŋmɔ kɛbaa nɔ ni yaa nɔ lɛ nɔ ni ewoɔ oblantai lɛ aŋaa.
Tsɔɔmɔ oshidaamɔ yɛ henumɔ gbɛfaŋ: Tsɔɔmɔ henumɔ ni lalawiemɔ lɛ kɛbaa ni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔɔ.
Ha ohiɛ aka Nifeemɔ lɛ nɔ Wiemɔ nibii nɛɛ amli:
Blema saji: Eji beiaŋ ni aŋmaa lalawiemɔ lɛ kɛgbalaa winɔ saji loo kɛ shaa wɔsɛɛ jeŋ lɛ he mfoniri.
Kaimɔ akɛ kɛji lalawiemɔ lɛ miiwie ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ saji ahe tete kɛjɛ beiaŋ ni aŋma lɛ lɛ, blema nifeemɔ lɛ ahe hiaa ejaakɛ ekudɔɔ ŋmalɔ lɛ.
Kusumɔ nifeemɔ: Lalawiemɔ ko kusum lɛ feɔ hemɔkɛyeli nibii, wiemɔ lɛ shishijee, akutsoŋ nifeemɔi loo kuu ko mli ni asɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ yɛ lɛ.
He ni Ŋ malɔ lɛ jɛ: Enɛ tsɔɔ nibii ni ŋmalɔ lɛ etsɔ mli, enitsumɔ kɛ esubaŋ.
He ni ŋmalɔ lɛ jɛ lɛ naa nɔnyɛɛ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ekɛtsuɔ nii kɛ nibii krokomɛi hu.
Buamɔ oshishinumɔ lɛ naa: Daamɔ onilee yɛ otii ni yɔɔ nifeemɔi lɛ amli lɛ nɔ kɛbua nibii ni oyɔse yɛ lalawiemɔ mli kɛna shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛi
Ati lalawiemɔ kɛ shwɛmɔ otii lɛ amli ekoŋŋ
Agba lalawiemɔ otii lɛ ahe sane
Ahara lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ yɛ lalawiemɔi amli
Ahara lalawiemɔ mli otii ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ ni akane lɛ mli
Klɛŋklɛŋ sanebimɔ 1 Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni lalawiemɔ ŋmalɔ lɛ kɛ mfonirishamɔ tsu nii yɛ Shamaŋsheo (Vincent Okunnor) lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Sanebimɔ 2 Ŋmalamɔ wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi fɛɛ ni yɔɔ Nugbɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Eko fɛɛ eko lɛ kɛ liamɔ ko yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ afata he.
Sanebimɔ 3 Bo diɛŋtsɛ sɔɔ lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ kukuji etɛ kɛ liamɔi ejwɛjwɛɛjwɛ.
Mlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 23 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Lalawiemɔ henyamɔ Kaimɔ Afi 1 Agba mɔnaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve kn.kn.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: LALAWIEMƆ HENYAMƆ Ati lalawiemɔ ji mli: Kwɛmɔ otsi 21 kɛ 22 kɛna mligbalamɔ ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ he kɛ nibii ni adamɔɔ nɔ kɛnyaa lalawiemɔ he.
Nibii Ni Esa Akɛ Akwɛ Kɛji Aanya Lalawiemɔ He Gbɛjianɔtoo ni baa nɛɛ ji nibii komɛi ni esa akɛ wɔdamɔ nɔ kɛnya lalawiemɔ ko he.
Oti : No ji shɛɛwiemɔ loo oti ni lalawiemɔ lɛ hiɛ lɛ.
Mfonirishamɔ: Bɔ ni akɛ henumɔ nibii komɛi shaa mfoniri nibii ahe yɛ wɔyitsei amli.
Gbee ni akɛŋma lalawiemɔ lɛ: Lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ subaŋ loo eshidaamɔ yɛ henumɔ gbɛfaŋ kɛha oti lɛ.
Wiemɔi ni akɛtsu nii: Wiemɔkulibii ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii, sintasi kɛ bɔ ni eŋma enii lɛ eha.
Wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi: Enɛmɛi ji heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsu nii tamɔ: no ni, tamɔ no, oookɛɛ gbɔmɔ kɛ awohe, kɛ krokomɛi tamɔ gbɛigbɛɛlɔ, kɔsonanti shitɛlɛlɔi.
Jebɔ : Eji lalawiemɔ lɛ shikamɔ.
Tamɔ liamɔi, kukuji kɛ liamɔi mligbalamɔ.
Shitiimɔ : Eji bɔ ni akɛ shitiimɔ tsuɔ nii kɛfeɔ lala henɔ ko.
Enyɛɔ ebaa lɛ hu akɛ, shitiimɔ bɛ ekomɛi amli.
Nifeemɔ: Blema, kusumɔ kɛ mɔ diɛŋtsɛ nifeemɔi anɔ ni adamɔ nɔ kɛŋma lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Henumɔ shihɛlemɔ: Bɔ ni lalawiemɔ lɛ teɔ henumɔ shi ni ekɔɔ kanelɔi ahe eko lɛ.
Lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ yiŋtoo: Lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ yiŋtoo kɛ shɛɛwiemɔ lɛ.
Ani sɛɛnamɔ baa lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ yiŋtoo lɛ he?
Okaditoo : Nibii, sui, kɛ okadii ni akɛdamɔɔ shi haa susumɔi.
Alushiŋ (Allusion): Nɔkwɛɔmɔnii hamɔ kɛjɛ niŋmaai krokomɛi, adesai loo yinɔ saji ni tee nɔ lɛ amli.
Ga sɛɛsɛɛ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ naaŋmɔlɔ nɔ kɛtsɔɔ srɔto yɛ nɔ ni sa ni ofee kɛ nɔ ni yaa nɔ lɛ teŋ.
Gbɛɛmɔi he heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi : Heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi tamɔ, shitɛrɛlɔi, vaoli henɔ kome kɛ kɔnsonatii.
Kɛji wɔdamɔ nibii nɛɛ anɔ lɛ, kanelɔi baanyɛ ana shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ ni amɛnya lalawiemɔ lɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ, otii kɛ ŋaagbɛ ni akɛŋma nii lɛ he.
Nikasemɔ Mli Nitsumɔ
Sɔɔmɔ lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ kukuji etɛ, oti lɛ ji ‘tsuishitoo’
Kɛkɔ nɔ ni okase yɛ lalawiemɔ he tsuutsu lɛ, tsɔɔmɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsu ni yɛ lalawiemɔ nɛɛ he.
Shamaŋsheo (Vincent Okunnor) Mishienyɛ jurɔ ni midɔɔ ohe;
Ni mitsui shweɔ o daadaa, Bo okeme mitaoɔ yɛ teemɔ mli, Mikɛ o ato ajina nɛɛ.
Aha mi wolo ni miyajɔɔ mihe, No hewɔ mifoɔ mihe fioo nɛɛ;
Shi he pɔtɛɛ ni miyaa lɛ mileee, Ni be ni maku misɛɛ hu minako.
Mi lɛ, naa, minɔ bɛ kaama, Shi bo kɛ wɔshwiei lɛ moŋ eshwɛ;
Okɛ naashwɔmɔ kɛ suɔmɔ Abaa amɛyi tamɔ wuɔ kɛ ebii.
Shɛɛ wɔkuuŋbii lɛ oha mi, Ni oka he ola lamɔ kɛ ŋɔɔmɔ;
Kɛ ashi agbo hiɛ futɛfutɛ lɛ, too he okɛɛ: Kpaoo, je ena yaa sɛɛ!
Nikasemɔ mli sanegbaa: Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nifeemɔ:
Tii lalawiemɔ mli otii amli
Wiemɔ lalawiemɔ oti ahe.
Wiemɔ gbɛi anɔ ni kɛnyaa lalawiemɔ he.
Nya lalawiemɔ ko he
Ku mli nitsumɔ/ ekomefeemɔ nikasemɔ- Mɛi enynyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ
Kanemɔ lalawiemɔ kɛjɛ lalawiemɔ wolo lɛ mli.
Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalawiemɔ henyamɔ he lɛ anya lalawiemɔ okane lɛ he.
Sɔɔmɔ sɔnɛti (lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ liamɔ kpaanyɔ) yɛ oti ni osumɔɔ ni kɔɔ owala mli sane loo Ghana kusum he.
Nya lalawiemɔ ni osɔɔ lɛ he.
Mlijaa 10 Mlikwɛmɔ Nɛkɛ mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ nibii fɛɛ ni atsɔɔ yɛ otsii 21, 22 kɛ 23 lɛ mli.
Ewie lalawiemɔ jebɔ he.
Ewie lalawiemɔ mli otii kɛ ehesɛɛnamɔi ahe hu, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ehe saji krokomɛi ahe.
Nɔ ni akwɛɔ lɛ damɔ bɔ ni akɛ lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ sɔɔ lalawiemɔ srɔtoi kɛ ehenɔilɛ, kɛ amɛhe nyamɔ.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ kɛji akɛ kaselɔi tsɔ mlijaa nɛɛ mli, amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛna lɛ baanya lalawiemɔ he yɛ Blɔfo wiemɔ mli kɛ Blɔfo jeŋnilee saji ahe.
OTSI 22 Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
Mlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 23 Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
